// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BagButton.cs =====
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

/// <summary>
/// Marker for the Bag button. Exposes its screen rect so BallDrag can detect drops on it,
/// and greys out the button when the bag pool is empty.
/// </summary>
public class BagButton : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static BagButton Instance { get; private set; }

    private RectTransform _rectTransform;
    private Canvas _canvas;
    private Button _button;

    public Rect ScreenRect { get; private set; }

    private void Awake()
    {
        Instance = this;
        _rectTransform = GetComponent<RectTransform>();
        _canvas = GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();
        _button = GetComponent<Button>();
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (_rectTransform == null) return;

        // --- Screen rect for drop detection ---
        Camera cam = (_canvas != null && _canvas.renderMode != RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay)
            ? _canvas.worldCamera
            : null;

        Vector3[] corners = new Vector3[4];
        _rectTransform.GetWorldCorners(corners);

        Vector2 min = RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(cam, corners[0]);
        Vector2 max = RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(cam, corners[2]);

        ScreenRect = new Rect(min.x, min.y, max.x - min.x, max.y - min.y);

        // --- Grey-out when the bag is empty ---
        if (_button != null && BagManager.Instance != null)
        {
            bool hasBalls = !BagManager.Instance.IsEmpty();
            bool bagLocked = BagManager.Instance.IsBeingEditedBySomeone;
            bool interactable = hasBalls && !bagLocked;
            if (_button.interactable != interactable)
                _button.interactable = interactable;
        }   
    }

    public bool IsMouseOver()
    {
        return ScreenRect.Contains(Input.mousePosition);
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BagCounterDisplay.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text;
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// IMGUI panel that shows bag ball counts: on board, per player's hand,
/// and remaining in bag. Only visible when online.
/// </summary>
public class BagCounterDisplay : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private float panelWidth = 300f;
    [SerializeField] private float panelMargin = 16f;

    private GUIStyle _titleStyle;
    private GUIStyle _rowStyle;
    private GUIStyle _headerStyle;
    private GUIStyle _totalStyle;
    private bool _stylesReady;

    private void EnsureStyles()
    {
        if (_stylesReady) return;

        _titleStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label)
        {
            fontSize = 20,
            fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold,
            alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft,
            normal = { textColor = Color.white }
        };

        _headerStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label)
        {
            fontSize = 14,
            fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold,
            alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft,
            normal = { textColor = new Color(0.7f, 0.85f, 1f) }
        };

        _rowStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label)
        {
            fontSize = 14,
            alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft,
            normal = { textColor = Color.white }
        };

        _totalStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label)
        {
            fontSize = 15,
            fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold,
            alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft,
            normal = { textColor = new Color(1f, 0.9f, 0.4f) }
        };

        _stylesReady = true;
    }

    private void OnGUI()
    {
        var nm = NetworkManager.Singleton;
        if (nm == null || !nm.IsListening) return;
        if (BagManager.Instance == null) return;

        EnsureStyles();

        int boardCount = CountBoardBagBalls();

        

        var playerLines = new List<(string name, int count)>();
        if (PlayerManager.Instance != null)
        {
            foreach (var pd in FindObjectsByType<PlayerData>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
            {
                if (pd == null || !pd.IsSpawned) continue;
                string name = pd.PlayerName.Value.Length > 0
                    ? pd.PlayerName.Value.ToString()
                    : $"Player {pd.OwnerClientId}";
                playerLines.Add((name, pd.HandBagCount.Value));
            }
        }

        int totalHand = 0;
        foreach (var line in playerLines) totalHand += line.count;

        int inBag = BagManager.Instance.RemainingCount();

        // Layout dimensions.
        float lineHeight = 22f;
        float panelHeight = 60f + (playerLines.Count + 3) * lineHeight;
        Rect panelRect = new Rect(
            panelMargin,
            Screen.height - panelHeight - panelMargin,
            panelWidth,
            panelHeight);

        // Background.
        GUI.color = new Color(0.07f, 0.12f, 0.16f, 0.9f);
        GUI.DrawTexture(panelRect, Texture2D.whiteTexture);
        GUI.color = Color.white;

        GUILayout.BeginArea(new Rect(
            panelRect.x + 12,
            panelRect.y + 10,
            panelRect.width - 24,
            panelRect.height - 20));

        string bagName = BagManager.Instance.Library != null
        ? BagManager.Instance.Library.GetName(BagManager.Instance.ActiveBagIndex)
        : "?";
        GUILayout.Label($"Bag Balls - {bagName}", _titleStyle);
        GUILayout.Space(4);
        DrawDivider();
        GUILayout.Space(4);

        // Board.
        GUILayout.Label($"Board:            {boardCount}", _rowStyle);

        // Per-player hand.
        if (playerLines.Count > 0)
        {
            GUILayout.Space(6);
            GUILayout.Label("Hands:", _headerStyle);
            foreach (var line in playerLines)
            {
                GUILayout.Label($"  {line.name}:  {line.count}", _rowStyle);
            }
        }

        GUILayout.Space(4);
        DrawDivider();
        GUILayout.Space(4);

        GUILayout.Label($"Total in hands:   {totalHand}", _totalStyle);
        GUILayout.Label($"In bag:           {inBag}", _totalStyle);

        GUILayout.EndArea();
    }

    private void DrawDivider()
    {
        Rect r = GUILayoutUtility.GetRect(1, 1, GUILayout.ExpandWidth(true));
        GUI.color = new Color(0.4f, 0.5f, 0.6f, 0.6f);
        GUI.DrawTexture(new Rect(r.x, r.y, r.width, 1), Texture2D.whiteTexture);
        GUI.color = Color.white;
    }

    private int CountBoardBagBalls()
    {
        int count = 0;
        var all = FindObjectsByType<NetworkBall>(FindObjectsSortMode.None);
        foreach (var nb in all)
        {
            if (nb == null || !nb.IsSpawned) continue;
            if (nb.CurrentZone.Value != (byte)BallZone.ServerBoard) continue;
            if (nb.IsBagBall.Value) count++;
        }
        return count;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BagDefinition.cs =====
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Configurable pool of bag balls. Create via Assets → Create → Bag → Bag Definition.
/// Fill in content + count entries; the Bag Manager uses these as its starting pool.
/// </summary>
[CreateAssetMenu(menuName = "Bag/Bag Definition", fileName = "DefaultBag")]
public class BagDefinition : ScriptableObject
{
    [Serializable]
    public class Entry
    {
        [Tooltip("Text displayed inside the ball, e.g. '3', '+', 'x'.")]
        public string content;

        [Min(0)]
        [Tooltip("How many copies of this ball start in the bag.")]
        public int count = 1;
    }

    public List<Entry> entries = new List<Entry>();
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BagDefinitionFiller.cs =====
#if UNITY_EDITOR
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

public static class BagDefinitionFiller
{
    // ============================================================
    // Menu items
    // ============================================================

    [MenuItem("Tools/Bags/Fill: Default (Algebra)")]
    public static void FillDefault() => Fill("Default");

    [MenuItem("Tools/Bags/Fill: Numbers Only")]
    public static void FillNumbers() => Fill("Numbers");

    [MenuItem("Tools/Bags/Fill: Operators Only")]
    public static void FillOperators() => Fill("Operators");

    [MenuItem("Tools/Bags/Fill: Easy Start")]
    public static void FillEasy() => Fill("Easy");

    [MenuItem("Tools/Bags/Fill: Advanced (functions)")]
    public static void FillAdvanced() => Fill("Advanced");

    [MenuItem("Tools/Bags/Fill: Custom (= only)")]
    public static void FillCustom() => Fill("Custom");

    [MenuItem("Tools/Bags/Fill: Scrabble (100 tiles)")]
    public static void FillScrabble() => Fill("Scrabble");

    // ============================================================

    private static void Fill(string preset)
    {
        var def = Selection.activeObject as BagDefinition;
        if (def == null)
        {
            EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Fill Bag",
                "Select a BagDefinition asset first.", "OK");
            return;
        }

        def.entries.Clear();

        switch (preset)
        {
            case "Default":   FillAlgebra(def);    break;
            case "Numbers":   FillNumbersPreset(def); break;
            case "Operators": FillOperatorsPreset(def); break;
            case "Easy":      FillEasyPreset(def); break;
            case "Advanced":  FillAdvancedPreset(def); break;
            case "Custom":    FillCustomPreset(def); break;
            case "Scrabble":  FillScrabblePreset(def); break;
        }

        EditorUtility.SetDirty(def);
        AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
        AssetDatabase.Refresh();

        int total = 0;
        foreach (var e in def.entries) total += e.count;
        Debug.Log($"Filled '{def.name}' with {def.entries.Count} distinct entries, {total} balls total ({preset}).");
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Presets
    // ============================================================

    private static void FillAlgebra(BagDefinition def)
    {
        Add(def, "1", 3);
        Add(def, "2", 3);
        Add(def, "3", 3);
        Add(def, "4", 2);
        Add(def, "5", 2);
        Add(def, "6", 1);
        Add(def, "7", 1);
        Add(def, "8", 1);
        Add(def, "9", 1);
        Add(def, "10", 1);
        Add(def, "+", 3);
        Add(def, "-", 3);
        Add(def, "*", 2);
        Add(def, "/", 2);
        Add(def, "^", 1);
        Add(def, "=", 4);
        Add(def, "x", 2);
        Add(def, "y", 2);
        Add(def, "(", 2);
        Add(def, ")", 2);
    }

    private static void FillNumbersPreset(BagDefinition def)
    {
        for (int n = 1; n <= 20; n++) Add(def, n.ToString(), 2);
        Add(def, "=", 4);
    }

    private static void FillOperatorsPreset(BagDefinition def)
    {
        Add(def, "+", 6);
        Add(def, "-", 6);
        Add(def, "*", 6);
        Add(def, "/", 6);
        Add(def, "^", 2);
        Add(def, "=", 4);
    }

    private static void FillEasyPreset(BagDefinition def)
    {
        Add(def, "1", 4);
        Add(def, "2", 4);
        Add(def, "3", 4);
        Add(def, "4", 3);
        Add(def, "5", 3);
        Add(def, "+", 5);
        Add(def, "-", 3);
        Add(def, "=", 4);
    }

    private static void FillAdvancedPreset(BagDefinition def)
    {
        FillAlgebra(def);
        Add(def, "pi", 1);
        Add(def, "e", 1);
        Add(def, "sin", 1);
        Add(def, "cos", 1);
        Add(def, "tan", 1);
        Add(def, "root", 1);
        Add(def, "log", 1);
        Add(def, "!", 1);
        Add(def, "%", 1);
        Add(def, "(", 2);
        Add(def, ")", 2);
    }

    private static void FillCustomPreset(BagDefinition def)
    {
        Add(def, "1", 8); //
        Add(def, "2", 6); //
        Add(def, "3", 5); //
        Add(def, "4", 4); //
        Add(def, "5", 3); 
        Add(def, "6", 3); 
        Add(def, "7", 1); 
        Add(def, "8", 2);  
        Add(def, "9", 1); 
        Add(def, "10", 1); 

         Add(def, "-1", 5); //
        Add(def, "-2", 3); //
        Add(def, "-3", 2); //
        Add(def, "-4", 1); //
        Add(def, "-5", 1); 
        Add(def, "-6", 1); 
        Add(def, "-7", 0); 
        Add(def, "-8", 1);  
        Add(def, "-9", 0); 
        Add(def, "-10", 0); 

        Add(def, "+", 12); //
        Add(def, "*", 10); //
        Add(def, "/", 2); 
        Add(def, "/3", 1); 
        Add(def, "/4", 1); 
        Add(def, "/5", 1); 
        Add(def, "/6", 1);

        Add(def, "^", 3);
        Add(def, "^3", 2);
        Add(def, "^4", 1);


        Add(def, "=", 6); //
        Add(def, "x", 1); 
        Add(def, "y", 1); 
      

    }

    private static void FillScrabblePreset(BagDefinition def)
    {
        // Standard Scrabble tile distribution (100 tiles total).
        // A-9, B-2, C-2, D-4, E-12, F-2, G-3, H-2, I-9, J-1, K-1, L-4,
        // M-2, N-6, O-8, P-2, Q-1, R-6, S-4, T-6, U-4, V-2, W-2, X-1,
        // Y-2, Z-1, blank-2.
        Add(def, "a", 9);
        Add(def, "b", 2);
        Add(def, "c", 2);
        Add(def, "d", 4);
        Add(def, "e", 12);

        Add(def, "g", 3);
        Add(def, "h", 2);
        Add(def, "i", 9);
        Add(def, "j", 1);
        Add(def, "k", 1);
        Add(def, "l", 4);

        Add(def, "w", 2);
        Add(def, "x", 1);
        Add(def, "y", 2);
        Add(def, "z", 1);
        Add(def, "_", 2);   // blanks
    }

    // ============================================================

    private static void Add(BagDefinition def, string content, int count)
    {
        def.entries.Add(new BagDefinition.Entry { content = content, count = count });
    }

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BagLibrary.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

[CreateAssetMenu(menuName = "Bag/Bag Library", fileName = "BagLibrary")]
public class BagLibrary : ScriptableObject
{
    [System.Serializable]
    public class NamedBag
    {
        public string displayName = "New Bag";
        public BagDefinition definition;
    }

    public List<NamedBag> bags = new List<NamedBag>();

    public int Count => bags.Count;

    public BagDefinition Get(int index)
    {
        if (index < 0 || index >= bags.Count) return null;
        return bags[index]?.definition;
    }

    public string GetName(int index)
    {
        if (index < 0 || index >= bags.Count) return $"Bag {index}";
        string n = bags[index]?.displayName;
        return string.IsNullOrEmpty(n) ? $"Bag {index}" : n;
    }

    public int IndexOf(BagDefinition def)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < bags.Count; i++)
            if (bags[i]?.definition == def) return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BagManager.cs =====
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

public struct BagEntryState : INetworkSerializable, System.IEquatable<BagEntryState>
{
    public FixedString64Bytes Content;
    public int Count;

    public void NetworkSerialize<T>(BufferSerializer<T> s) where T : IReaderWriter
    {
        s.SerializeValue(ref Content);
        s.SerializeValue(ref Count);
    }

    public bool Equals(BagEntryState other) => Content.Equals(other.Content) && Count == other.Count;
}

public class BagManager : NetworkBehaviour
{
    public static BagManager Instance { get; private set; }

    [SerializeField] private BagLibrary library;

    // Networked pool (server authoritative).
    private NetworkList<BagEntryState> _networkPool;

    // Offline-only pool.
    private List<BagEntryState> _offlinePool = new List<BagEntryState>();

    private System.Action<string> PendingDrawCallback;

    // Shared online state.
    private readonly NetworkVariable<byte> _activeBagIndex = new(
        0,
        NetworkVariableReadPermission.Everyone,
        NetworkVariableWritePermission.Server);

    private readonly NetworkVariable<ulong> _editingClientId = new(
        ulong.MaxValue,
        NetworkVariableReadPermission.Everyone,
        NetworkVariableWritePermission.Server);

    // Offline-only equivalent of the active index.
    private int _offlineActiveBagIndex = 0;

    // ==================================================================
    // Public read-only views
    // ==================================================================

    public bool IsOnline =>
        NetworkManager.Singleton != null && NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening;

    public int ActiveBagIndex => IsOnline ? _activeBagIndex.Value : _offlineActiveBagIndex;

    public ulong EditingClientId => IsOnline ? _editingClientId.Value : ulong.MaxValue;

    public bool IsBeingEditedBySomeone =>
        EditingClientId != ulong.MaxValue && EditingClientId != LocalClientId;

    public BagLibrary Library => library;

    private ulong LocalClientId =>
        (NetworkManager.Singleton != null && NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening)
            ? NetworkManager.Singleton.LocalClientId
            : 0UL;

    // ==================================================================
    // Lifecycle
    // ==================================================================

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
        Instance = this;

        _networkPool = new NetworkList<BagEntryState>(
            default,
            NetworkVariableReadPermission.Everyone,
            NetworkVariableWritePermission.Server);
    }

    private void Start()
    {
        if (!IsOnline)
        {
            _offlineActiveBagIndex = Mathf.Clamp(
                PlayerPrefs.GetInt("ActiveBagIndex", 0),
                0,
                library != null && library.Count > 0 ? library.Count - 1 : 0);
            ResetOffline();
        }
    }

    public override void OnNetworkSpawn()
    {
        if (IsServer)
        {
            _networkPool.Clear();
            var def = ActiveDefinition();
            if (def != null)
            {
                foreach (var e in def.entries)
                {
                    if (e == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.content) || e.count <= 0) continue;
                    _networkPool.Add(new BagEntryState { Content = e.content, Count = e.count });
                }
            }

            if (NetworkManager != null)
                NetworkManager.OnClientDisconnectCallback += OnClientDisconnected;
        }
    }

    public override void OnNetworkDespawn()
    {
        if (IsServer && NetworkManager != null)
            NetworkManager.OnClientDisconnectCallback -= OnClientDisconnected;
    }

    private void OnClientDisconnected(ulong id)
    {
        if (IsServer && _editingClientId.Value == id)
            _editingClientId.Value = ulong.MaxValue;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Queries
    // ==================================================================

    public int RemainingCount()
    {
        if (IsOnline)
        {
            int total = 0;
            foreach (var e in _networkPool) total += e.Count;
            return total;
        }

        int offline = 0;
        foreach (var e in _offlinePool) offline += e.Count;
        return offline;
    }

    public bool IsEmpty() => RemainingCount() == 0;

    public List<(string content, int count)> GetEntriesSnapshot()
    {
        var list = new List<(string, int)>();
        if (IsOnline)
        {
            foreach (var e in _networkPool) list.Add((e.Content.ToString(), e.Count));
        }
        else
        {
            foreach (var e in _offlinePool) list.Add((e.Content.ToString(), e.Count));
        }
        return list;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Bag selection
    // ==================================================================

    public void RequestSelectBag(int index)
    {
        if (library == null || index < 0 || index >= library.Count) return;

        if (!IsOnline)
        {
            _offlineActiveBagIndex = index;
            PlayerPrefs.SetInt("ActiveBagIndex", index);
            PlayerPrefs.Save();
            ResetOffline();
            return;
        }

        if (IsServer) { SelectBagServerSide(index); return; }
        RequestSelectBagServerRpc((byte)index);
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    private void RequestSelectBagServerRpc(byte index, RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (_editingClientId.Value != ulong.MaxValue) return;
        SelectBagServerSide(index);
    }

    private void SelectBagServerSide(int index)
    {
        _activeBagIndex.Value = (byte)index;
        ResetServerSide();
        _editingClientId.Value = ulong.MaxValue;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Editing lock
    // ==================================================================

    public void RequestBeginEdit()
    {
        if (!IsOnline) return;
        if (IsServer) BeginEditServerSide(LocalClientId);
        else RequestBeginEditServerRpc();
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    private void RequestBeginEditServerRpc(RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        BeginEditServerSide(rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId);
    }

    private void BeginEditServerSide(ulong clientId)
    {
        if (_editingClientId.Value != ulong.MaxValue &&
            _editingClientId.Value != clientId) return;
        _editingClientId.Value = clientId;
    }

    public void RequestEndEdit()
    {
        if (!IsOnline) return;
        if (IsServer) EndEditServerSide(LocalClientId);
        else RequestEndEditServerRpc();
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    private void RequestEndEditServerRpc(RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        EndEditServerSide(rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId);
    }

    private void EndEditServerSide(ulong clientId)
    {
        if (_editingClientId.Value == clientId)
            _editingClientId.Value = ulong.MaxValue;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Entry edits
    // ==================================================================

    public void RequestAddEntry(string content, int count)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(content) || count <= 0) return;

        if (!IsOnline) { AddEntryOffline(content, count); return; }

        if (IsServer) AddEntryServerSide(content, count, LocalClientId);
        else RequestAddEntryServerRpc(content, count);
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    private void RequestAddEntryServerRpc(string content, int count, RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        AddEntryServerSide(content, count, rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId);
    }

    private void AddEntryServerSide(string content, int count, ulong sender)
    {
        if (_editingClientId.Value != sender) return;
        for (int i = 0; i < _networkPool.Count; i++)
        {
            var e = _networkPool[i];
            if (e.Content.ToString() == content)
            {
                e.Count += count;
                _networkPool[i] = e;
                return;
            }
        }
        _networkPool.Add(new BagEntryState { Content = content, Count = count });
    }

    private void AddEntryOffline(string content, int count)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < _offlinePool.Count; i++)
        {
            var e = _offlinePool[i];
            if (e.Content.ToString() == content) { e.Count += count; _offlinePool[i] = e; return; }
        }
        _offlinePool.Add(new BagEntryState { Content = content, Count = count });
    }

    public void RequestSetEntryCount(string content, int newCount)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(content)) return;
        if (!IsOnline) { SetCountOffline(content, newCount); return; }
        if (IsServer) SetCountServerSide(content, newCount, LocalClientId);
        else RequestSetEntryCountServerRpc(content, newCount);
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    private void RequestSetEntryCountServerRpc(string content, int newCount, RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        SetCountServerSide(content, newCount, rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId);
    }

    private void SetCountServerSide(string content, int newCount, ulong sender)
    {
        if (_editingClientId.Value != sender) return;
        for (int i = 0; i < _networkPool.Count; i++)
        {
            var e = _networkPool[i];
            if (e.Content.ToString() != content) continue;
            if (newCount <= 0) { _networkPool.RemoveAt(i); return; }
            e.Count = newCount;
            _networkPool[i] = e;
            return;
        }
        if (newCount > 0)
            _networkPool.Add(new BagEntryState { Content = content, Count = newCount });
    }

    private void SetCountOffline(string content, int newCount)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < _offlinePool.Count; i++)
        {
            var e = _offlinePool[i];
            if (e.Content.ToString() != content) continue;
            if (newCount <= 0) { _offlinePool.RemoveAt(i); return; }
            e.Count = newCount;
            _offlinePool[i] = e;
            return;
        }
        if (newCount > 0)
            _offlinePool.Add(new BagEntryState { Content = content, Count = newCount });
    }

    public void RequestRemoveEntry(string content) => RequestSetEntryCount(content, 0);

    // ==================================================================
    // Draw
    // ==================================================================

    public void RequestDraw(System.Action<string> onContentReady)
    {
        if (!IsOnline)
        {
            string content = DrawOffline();
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(content)) onContentReady?.Invoke(content);
            return;
        }

        if (IsServer)
        {
            string content = DrawServerSide();
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(content)) onContentReady?.Invoke(content);
        }
        else
        {
            PendingDrawCallback = onContentReady;
            RequestDrawServerRpc();
        }
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    private void RequestDrawServerRpc(RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;

        string content = DrawServerSide();
        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;

        var target = new ClientRpcParams
        {
            Send = new ClientRpcSendParams { TargetClientIds = new[] { sender } }
        };

        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
            DrawResultClientRpc(false, string.Empty, target);
        else
            DrawResultClientRpc(true, content, target);
    }

    [ClientRpc]
    private void DrawResultClientRpc(bool success, string content, ClientRpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (success && PendingDrawCallback != null)
            PendingDrawCallback(content);
        PendingDrawCallback = null;
    }

    private string DrawServerSide()
    {
        int total = 0;
        foreach (var e in _networkPool) total += e.Count;
        if (total <= 0) return null;

        int roll = UnityEngine.Random.Range(0, total);
        for (int i = 0; i < _networkPool.Count; i++)
        {
            var entry = _networkPool[i];
            if (roll < entry.Count)
            {
                string content = entry.Content.ToString();
                entry.Count--;
                if (entry.Count <= 0) _networkPool.RemoveAt(i);
                else _networkPool[i] = entry;
                return content;
            }
            roll -= entry.Count;
        }
        return null;
    }

    private string DrawOffline()
    {
        int total = 0;
        foreach (var e in _offlinePool) total += e.Count;
        if (total <= 0) return null;

        int roll = UnityEngine.Random.Range(0, total);
        for (int i = 0; i < _offlinePool.Count; i++)
        {
            var entry = _offlinePool[i];
            if (roll < entry.Count)
            {
                string content = entry.Content.ToString();
                entry.Count--;
                if (entry.Count <= 0) _offlinePool.RemoveAt(i);
                else _offlinePool[i] = entry;
                return content;
            }
            roll -= entry.Count;
        }
        return null;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Return to bag
    // ==================================================================

    public void RequestReturnToBag(string content)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(content)) return;

        if (!IsOnline) { ReturnOffline(content); return; }

        if (IsServer) ReturnServerSide(content);
        else RequestReturnToBagServerRpc(content);
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    private void RequestReturnToBagServerRpc(string content)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        ReturnServerSide(content);
    }

    private void ReturnServerSide(string content)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < _networkPool.Count; i++)
        {
            var entry = _networkPool[i];
            if (entry.Content.ToString() == content)
            {
                entry.Count++;
                _networkPool[i] = entry;
                return;
            }
        }
        _networkPool.Add(new BagEntryState { Content = content, Count = 1 });
    }

    private void ReturnOffline(string content)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < _offlinePool.Count; i++)
        {
            var entry = _offlinePool[i];
            if (entry.Content.ToString() == content)
            {
                entry.Count++;
                _offlinePool[i] = entry;
                return;
            }
        }
        _offlinePool.Add(new BagEntryState { Content = content, Count = 1 });
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Reset
    // ==================================================================

    public void RequestReset()
    {
        if (!IsOnline)
        {
            ResetOffline();

            var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
            if (spawner != null) spawner.DeleteAllHandBagBalls();

            var boardBalls = spawner?.GetAllBoardPieces();
            if (boardBalls != null)
            {
                foreach (var go in boardBalls)
                {
                    if (go == null) continue;
                    if (go.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null)
                        spawner.DestroyBoardBall(go);
                }
            }
            return;
        }

        if (IsServer) RequestResetServerRpc();
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestResetServerRpc(RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;

        ResetServerSide();

        var all = FindObjectsByType<NetworkBall>(FindObjectsSortMode.None);
        foreach (var nb in all)
        {
            if (nb != null && nb.IsSpawned && nb.IsBagBall.Value)
                nb.NetworkObject.Despawn(true);
        }

        ResetHandBagBallsClientRpc();
    }

    [ClientRpc]
    private void ResetHandBagBallsClientRpc()
    {
        var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
        if (spawner != null) spawner.DeleteAllHandBagBalls();
    }

    private void ResetServerSide()
    {
        _networkPool.Clear();
        var def = ActiveDefinition();
        if (def == null) return;
        foreach (var e in def.entries)
        {
            if (e == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.content) || e.count <= 0) continue;
            _networkPool.Add(new BagEntryState { Content = e.content, Count = e.count });
        }
    }

    private void ResetOffline()
    {
        _offlinePool.Clear();
        var def = ActiveDefinition();
        if (def == null) return;
        foreach (var e in def.entries)
        {
            if (e == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.content) || e.count <= 0) continue;
            _offlinePool.Add(new BagEntryState { Content = e.content, Count = e.count });
        }
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Helpers
    // ==================================================================

    private BagDefinition ActiveDefinition()
    {
        if (library == null) return null;
        return library.Get(ActiveBagIndex);
    }

#if UNITY_EDITOR
    internal void SaveActiveDefinitionFromPool()
    {
        var def = ActiveDefinition();
        if (def == null) return;
        def.entries.Clear();
        foreach (var e in _networkPool)
            def.entries.Add(new BagDefinition.Entry
            {
                content = e.Content.ToString(),
                count = e.Count
            });
        UnityEditor.EditorUtility.SetDirty(def);
        UnityEditor.AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
    }
#endif
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BagMenuUI.cs =====
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

public class BagMenuUI : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private float panelWidth = 420f;
    [SerializeField] private float panelHeight = 480f;

    private bool _isOpen;
    private Vector2 _scroll;
    private string _newContent = "";
    private string _newCount = "1";

    public void Open()
    {
        _isOpen = true;
        var bag = BagManager.Instance;
        if (bag != null && bag.IsOnline) bag.RequestBeginEdit();
    }

    private void Close()
    {
        _isOpen = false;
        BagManager.Instance?.RequestEndEdit();
    }

    private ulong LocalId() =>
        NetworkManager.Singleton != null && NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening
            ? NetworkManager.Singleton.LocalClientId : 0UL;

    private void OnGUI()
    {
        if (!_isOpen) return;
        var bag = BagManager.Instance;
        if (bag == null) return;

        Rect panel = new Rect(
            (Screen.width - panelWidth) * 0.5f,
            (Screen.height - panelHeight) * 0.5f,
            panelWidth, panelHeight);

        GUI.color = new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.55f);
        GUI.DrawTexture(new Rect(0, 0, Screen.width, Screen.height), Texture2D.whiteTexture);
        GUI.color = Color.white;

        GUI.Box(panel, GUIContent.none, GUI.skin.window);
        GUILayout.BeginArea(new Rect(panel.x + 16, panel.y + 12,
                                     panel.width - 32, panel.height - 24));

        // Header
        GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
        GUILayout.Label("<b>Bag Manager</b>", new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label)
        {
            richText = true, fontSize = 16, alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft
        }, GUILayout.Height(28));
        GUILayout.FlexibleSpace();
        if (GUILayout.Button("X", GUILayout.Width(28), GUILayout.Height(24)))
        {
            Close();
            GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
            GUILayout.EndArea();
            return;
        }
        GUILayout.EndHorizontal();

        bool lockedByOther = bag.IsBeingEditedBySomeone;
        bool lockedByMe = bag.IsOnline
                          && bag.EditingClientId == LocalId()
                          && bag.EditingClientId != ulong.MaxValue;

        // Bag picker
        GUILayout.Space(6);
        GUILayout.Label("Active bag:", GUI.skin.label);
        var lib = bag.Library;
        if (lib != null)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < lib.Count; i++)
            {
                bool selected = i == bag.ActiveBagIndex;
                GUI.enabled = !bag.IsBeingEditedBySomeone;
                if (GUILayout.Button(
                        (selected ? "[x] " : "[ ] ") + lib.GetName(i),
                        GUILayout.Height(28)))
                {
                    bag.RequestSelectBag(i);
                }
                GUI.enabled = true;
            }
        }
        else
        {
            GUILayout.Label("No BagLibrary assigned on BagManager.");
        }

        GUILayout.Space(8);

        // Lock gate
        if (lockedByOther)
        {
            var style = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label);
            style.normal.textColor = new Color(1f, 0.7f, 0.35f);
            string editor = PlayerManager.GetPlayerName(bag.EditingClientId);
            GUILayout.Label($"Locked - {editor} is editing this bag.", style);
            if (GUILayout.Button("Close", GUILayout.Height(28)))
            {
                Close();
                GUILayout.EndArea();
                return;
            }
            GUILayout.EndArea();
            return;
        }

        if (bag.IsOnline && !lockedByMe)
        {
            if (GUILayout.Button("Start editing", GUILayout.Height(30)))
            {
                bag.RequestBeginEdit();
            }
            GUILayout.EndArea();
            return;
        }

        // Entries


        _scroll = GUILayout.BeginScrollView(_scroll, GUILayout.ExpandHeight(true));
        var entries = bag.GetEntriesSnapshot();
        int total = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < entries.Count; i++)
        {
            var (content, count) = entries[i];
            total += count;
            GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
            GUILayout.Label(content, GUILayout.Width(80));
            GUILayout.Label($"x{count}", GUILayout.Width(50));

            if (GUILayout.Button("-", GUILayout.Width(28)))
                bag.RequestSetEntryCount(content, count - 1);
            if (GUILayout.Button("+", GUILayout.Width(28)))
                bag.RequestSetEntryCount(content, count + 1);
            if (GUILayout.Button("del", GUILayout.Width(40)))
                bag.RequestRemoveEntry(content);

            GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
        }
        GUILayout.EndScrollView();

        GUILayout.Space(4);
        GUILayout.Label($"Total in bag: {total}");

        // Add
        GUILayout.Space(6);
        GUILayout.Label("Add content:", GUI.skin.label);
        GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
        _newContent = GUILayout.TextField(_newContent, 24, GUILayout.Width(180));
        _newCount = GUILayout.TextField(_newCount, 4, GUILayout.Width(60));
        if (GUILayout.Button("Add"))
        {
            if (int.TryParse(_newCount, out int c) && c > 0 && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_newContent))
            {
                bag.RequestAddEntry(_newContent.Trim(), c);
                _newContent = "";
                _newCount = "1";
            }
        }
        GUILayout.EndHorizontal();

        GUILayout.Space(8);
        GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
        if (GUILayout.Button("Done editing", GUILayout.Height(30)))
        {
            bag.RequestEndEdit();
            _isOpen = false;
        }
        if (GUILayout.Button("Reset bag", GUILayout.Height(30)))
        {
            bag.RequestReset();
        }
#if UNITY_EDITOR
        if (NetworkManager.Singleton != null && NetworkManager.Singleton.IsServer)
        {
            if (GUILayout.Button("Save to asset", GUILayout.Height(30)))
                bag.SaveActiveDefinitionFromPool();
        }
#endif
        GUILayout.EndHorizontal();

        GUILayout.EndArea();
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BagRing.cs =====
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Draws a blue ring around a bag ball. Crescent-aware: if the ball is a
/// directional operator, the ring wraps the polygon's world bounds instead
/// of the disabled CircleCollider2D.
/// </summary>
public class BagRing : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private Color ringColor    = new Color(0.2f, 0.6f, 1f, 1f);
    [SerializeField] private float ringWidth    = 0.05f;
    [SerializeField] private float radiusPadding = 0.12f;
    [SerializeField] private int   segments     = 48;

    private NetworkBall      _networkBall;
    private BagBallMarker    _marker;
    private LineRenderer     _line;

    private void Awake()
    {
        _networkBall = GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        _marker      = GetComponent<BagBallMarker>();
    }

    private void Start()
    {
        var go = new GameObject("Bag Ring");
        go.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
        go.transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;

        _line = go.AddComponent<LineRenderer>();
        _line.useWorldSpace  = false;
        _line.loop           = true;
        _line.positionCount  = segments;
        _line.startWidth     = ringWidth;
        _line.endWidth       = ringWidth;
        _line.material       = new Material(Shader.Find("Sprites/Default"));
        _line.startColor     = ringColor;
        _line.endColor       = ringColor;
        _line.sortingOrder   = 24;
        _line.numCapVertices    = 4;
        _line.numCornerVertices = 4;
        _line.enabled = false;

        RebuildGeometry();
        UpdateVisibility();
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (_line == null) return;
        UpdateVisibility();
    }

    private bool IsBagBall()
    {
        if (_marker != null) return true;
        if (_networkBall != null) return _networkBall.IsBagBall.Value;
        return false;
    }

    private void UpdateVisibility()
    {
        bool isBag = IsBagBall();
        if (_line.enabled != isBag)
        {
            _line.enabled = isBag;
            if (isBag) RebuildGeometry();
        }

        if (isBag)
        {
            int layer = gameObject.layer;
            if (_line.gameObject.layer != layer)
                _line.gameObject.layer = layer;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Rebuild the ring in local space sized to whichever collider is active
    /// on the ball. Safe to call every frame if needed.
    /// </summary>
    private void RebuildGeometry()
    {
        float radius = GetActiveRadius() + radiusPadding;
        for (int i = 0; i < segments; i++)
        {
            float a = i * Mathf.PI * 2f / segments;
            _line.SetPosition(i, new Vector3(
                Mathf.Cos(a) * radius,
                Mathf.Sin(a) * radius,
                -0.04f));
        }
    }

    private float GetActiveRadius()
    {
        var dop = GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();
        if (dop != null && dop.IsActive)
        {
            var poly = GetComponent<PolygonCollider2D>();
            if (poly != null && poly.enabled)
                return Mathf.Max(poly.bounds.extents.x, poly.bounds.extents.y);
        }

        var circle = GetComponent<CircleCollider2D>();
        if (circle != null && circle.enabled)
            return circle.radius;

        return 0.5f;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallAppearance.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

// Creates each ball's text label and colors the ball according to its content type.
public class BallAppearance : MonoBehaviour
{
    private static readonly Color DefaultLabelColor = new Color(0.85f, 0.86f, 0.86f);
    private static readonly Color EquationIdleColor = new Color(0.20f, 0.45f, 0.95f);   // blue
    private static readonly Color CorrectEquationBallColor = new Color(0.28f, 0.72f, 0.32f);   // green
    private static readonly Color AmbiguousOperandColor = new Color(0.92f, 0.16f, 0.18f);   // red


    private static readonly Color[] NumberAndSymbolColors =
    {
        new Color(0.22f, 0.24f, 0.26f),
        new Color(0.36f, 0.38f, 0.40f),
        new Color(0.50f, 0.52f, 0.54f),
        new Color(0.64f, 0.66f, 0.68f),
        new Color(0.78f, 0.80f, 0.82f)
    };

    private static readonly Color[] AlgebraColors =
    {
        new Color(1.00f, 0.50f, 0.04f),
        new Color(1.00f, 0.58f, 0.08f),
        new Color(0.96f, 0.46f, 0.02f),
        new Color(1.00f, 0.66f, 0.12f),
        new Color(0.92f, 0.52f, 0.04f),
        new Color(1.00f, 0.42f, 0.01f)
    };

    private static readonly Color[] MixedContentColors =
    {
        new Color(1.00f, 0.76f, 0.08f),
        new Color(1.00f, 0.84f, 0.12f),
        new Color(0.94f, 0.70f, 0.04f),
        new Color(1.00f, 0.90f, 0.20f)
    };

    private static readonly Color[] MathSymbolColors =
    {
        new Color(0.16f, 0.39f, 0.78f),
        new Color(0.20f, 0.51f, 0.86f),
        new Color(0.28f, 0.62f, 0.91f),
        new Color(0.12f, 0.45f, 0.70f),
        new Color(0.35f, 0.55f, 0.82f)
    };

    private static readonly Color[] SolverOutputColors =
    {
        new Color(0.20f, 0.62f, 0.24f),
        new Color(0.28f, 0.72f, 0.32f),
        new Color(0.36f, 0.58f, 0.18f),
        new Color(0.16f, 0.50f, 0.22f),
        new Color(0.42f, 0.76f, 0.30f),
        new Color(0.24f, 0.66f, 0.38f)
    };

    private static readonly Color[] InvalidMathColors =
    {
        new Color(0.92f, 0.16f, 0.18f),
        new Color(0.82f, 0.10f, 0.12f),
        new Color(1.00f, 0.28f, 0.24f),
        new Color(0.72f, 0.08f, 0.10f)
    };

    private static readonly Color[] OtherContentColors =
    {
        new Color(0.20f, 0.66f, 0.67f),
        new Color(0.20f, 0.51f, 0.76f),
        new Color(0.35f, 0.73f, 0.51f),
        new Color(0.25f, 0.75f, 0.78f),
        new Color(0.34f, 0.62f, 0.57f),
        new Color(0.32f, 0.44f, 0.76f)
    };

    // Rim color drawn under the ball sprite when this piece is in the player's hand.
    private static readonly Color HandPieceRimColor = new Color(1f, 0.85f, 0.2f, 0.9f);
    private SpriteRenderer handRim;
    private bool isInHand;

    // Cached render components and the last selected color category.
    private TextMesh label;
    private MeshRenderer labelRenderer;
    private SpriteRenderer ballRenderer;
    private Color defaultBallColor;
    private Color baseBallColor;
    private Color baseLabelColor = DefaultLabelColor;
    private int currentColorKind = -1;
    private bool currentIsSolverOutput;
    private byte equationState;  
    private bool isAmbiguousOperand;
    

    

    public void SetEquationState(byte state)
    {
        if (equationState == state) return;
        equationState = state;
        ApplyBallColor();
    }

    // Cache the ball sprite and create a child TextMesh for its label.
    private void Awake()
    {
        ballRenderer = GetComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
        if (ballRenderer != null)
        {
            Color c = ballRenderer.color;
            if (c.a < 0.01f) c = Color.white;
            ballRenderer.color = c;
            defaultBallColor = c;
            baseBallColor = c;
        }

        CreateLabel();
    }

    // Refit the text each frame so changes in text length remain inside the ball.
    private void LateUpdate()
    {
        if (labelRenderer != null && labelRenderer.enabled)
        {
            FitLabel();
        }
    }

    // Set the label and optionally classify its text to choose a ball color.
    public void SetContent(string content, bool colorFromContents, bool isSolverOutput)
    {
        Debug.Log($"[Appearance] SetContent('{content}', colorFromContents={colorFromContents}, solverOut={isSolverOutput}) " +
              $"on {name}, label={(label == null ? "NULL" : "ok")}, " +
              $"ballRenderer={(ballRenderer == null ? "NULL" : "ok")}");
        
        if (label == null)
        {
            return;
        }

      
        label.text = content;
        labelRenderer.enabled = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(content);

        if (!colorFromContents)
        {
            currentColorKind = -1;
            currentIsSolverOutput = false;
            baseLabelColor = DefaultLabelColor;
            ApplyLabelColor();
            if (ballRenderer != null)
            {
                baseBallColor = string.IsNullOrEmpty(content) ? Color.white : defaultBallColor;
                ApplyBallColor();
            }

            return;
        }

        MathContentKind kind = MathExpression.IsMathematicalSyntaxContent(content) &&
            !MathExpression.HasValidSyntax(content)
            ? MathContentKind.InvalidMath
            : MathExpression.Classify(content);
        baseLabelColor = kind == MathContentKind.InvalidMath
            ? new Color(0.16f, 0.17f, 0.18f)
            : DefaultLabelColor;
        ApplyLabelColor();

        int colorKind = (int)kind;
        if (colorKind == currentColorKind && isSolverOutput == currentIsSolverOutput)
        {
            ApplyBallColor();
            return;
        }

        currentColorKind = colorKind;
        currentIsSolverOutput = isSolverOutput;
        if (ballRenderer == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (isSolverOutput)
        {
            baseBallColor = SolverOutputColors[Random.Range(0, SolverOutputColors.Length)];
            ApplyBallColor();
            return;
        }

        if (kind == MathContentKind.Empty)
        {
            baseBallColor = Color.white;
            ApplyBallColor();
            return;
        }

        Color[] colors = kind switch
        {
            MathContentKind.Algebra => AlgebraColors,
            MathContentKind.Mixed => MixedContentColors,
            MathContentKind.Operator => MathSymbolColors,
            MathContentKind.InvalidMath => InvalidMathColors,
            MathContentKind.Other => OtherContentColors,
            _ => NumberAndSymbolColors
        };
        baseBallColor = colors[Random.Range(0, colors.Length)];
        ApplyBallColor();
    }

    // Early-out if the value hasn't changed, so per-tick re-application is cheap.
    public void SetAmbiguousOperand(bool isAmbiguous)
    {
        if (isAmbiguousOperand == isAmbiguous)
        {
            return;
        }

        isAmbiguousOperand = isAmbiguous;
        ApplyBallColor();
    }

   
    private void ApplyLabelColor()
    {
        if (label != null)
        {
            label.color = baseLabelColor;
        }
    }

    private void ApplyBallColor()
    {
        if (ballRenderer == null) return;
    Debug.Log($"[Appearance] ApplyBallColor on {name}, " +
              $"eqState={equationState}, isInHand={isInHand}, isAmbiguous={isAmbiguousOperand}, " +
              $"baseBallColor={baseBallColor}, currentColor={(ballRenderer != null ? ballRenderer.color.ToString() : "null")}");
    // ...


        // Priority:
        // 1. '=' ball green (correct)
        // 2. '=' ball red (incorrect)
        // 3. '=' ball blue (idle)
        // 4. red (ambiguous operand / function)
        // 5. hand piece (untouched)
        // 6. normal base color
        if (equationState == 1) { ballRenderer.color = CorrectEquationBallColor; return; }
        if (equationState == 2) { ballRenderer.color = AmbiguousOperandColor; return; }
        if (equationState == 0 && IsEqualsBall()) { ballRenderer.color = EquationIdleColor; return; }

        if (isAmbiguousOperand) { ballRenderer.color = AmbiguousOperandColor; return; }
        if (isInHand) return;
        ballRenderer.color = baseBallColor;
    }

    // Helper: is this ball the '=' ball?
    private bool IsEqualsBall()
    {
        // The '=' ball has no label styling we can rely on here, so check
        // the GameObject's stored state. If BallAppearance doesn't know the
        // ball's content, add a public bool field set by BallNumber.
        return _isEqualsBall;
    }

    private bool _isEqualsBall;
    public void MarkAsEqualsBall() => _isEqualsBall = true;

    // Create and configure the child text mesh used to display ball contents.
    private void CreateLabel()
    {
        GameObject labelObject = new GameObject("Ball Number");
        labelObject.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
        labelObject.transform.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, 0f, 0.02f);

        label = labelObject.AddComponent<TextMesh>();
        label.font = Resources.GetBuiltinResource<Font>("LegacyRuntime.ttf");
        label.anchor = TextAnchor.MiddleCenter;
        label.alignment = TextAlignment.Center;
        label.characterSize = 0.2f;
        label.fontSize = 64;
        label.fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold;
        label.color = DefaultLabelColor;
        labelRenderer = labelObject.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>();
        labelRenderer.sortingOrder = ballRenderer != null ? ballRenderer.sortingOrder + 1 : 1;
        labelRenderer.enabled = false;
    }

    // Scale and orient the label so it stays centered and fits within the sprite.
    private void FitLabel()
    {
        if (ballRenderer == null || labelRenderer == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        Transform labelTransform = label.transform;
        labelTransform.localScale = Vector3.one;

        Vector3 labelSize = labelRenderer.bounds.size;
        Vector3 ballSize = ballRenderer.bounds.size;
        if (labelSize.x <= Mathf.Epsilon || labelSize.y <= Mathf.Epsilon)
        {
            return;
        }

        float widthScale = ballSize.x * 0.62f / labelSize.x;
        float heightScale = ballSize.y * 0.62f / labelSize.y;
        float fitScale = Mathf.Min(1f, widthScale, heightScale);
        labelTransform.localScale = Vector3.one * fitScale;
        labelTransform.rotation = Quaternion.identity;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Draws (or removes) a colored ring around the ball to mark it as a hand piece.
    /// Only affects visuals — nothing network-related.
    /// </summary>
    public void SetHandPiece(bool inHand)
    {
        if (isInHand == inHand) return;
        isInHand = inHand;

        if (inHand)
        {
            if (handRim == null) handRim = CreateHandRim();
            handRim.enabled = true;
            if (ballRenderer != null)
            {
                Color c = ballRenderer.color;
                float lum = 0.299f * c.r + 0.587f * c.g + 0.114f * c.b;
                ballRenderer.color = Color.Lerp(c, new Color(lum, lum, lum, c.a), 0.35f);
            }
        }
        else
        {
            if (handRim != null) handRim.enabled = false;
            ApplyBallColor();
        }
    }

    private SpriteRenderer CreateHandRim()
    {
        var go = new GameObject("Hand Rim");
        go.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
        go.transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;

        var sr = go.AddComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
        sr.sprite = ballRenderer != null ? ballRenderer.sprite : null;
        sr.color = HandPieceRimColor;
        sr.sortingOrder = ballRenderer != null ? ballRenderer.sortingOrder - 1 : -1;
        go.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * 1.12f;
        return sr;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallConnectionRules.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Decides whether two balls are "connected" for math purposes.
///
/// Works for any Collider2D combination (circle / polygon / crescent).
/// Directional operators ('-', '/', '^') use crescent-shaped colliders and
/// have special rules: they connect ONLY to a ball placed on the correct
/// operand side (inside the crescent's cavity, or just outside its outer arm).
/// </summary>
public static class BallConnectionRules
{
    /// <summary>Max edge-to-edge gap (in world units) for two ordinary balls to count as connected.</summary>
    public const float ConnectionGap = 0.28f;

    /// <summary>Max edge-to-edge gap for a directional operator's port to capture an operand.</summary>
    private const float OperandCaptureGap = 0.30f;

    public static bool AreConnected(Collider2D first, Collider2D second)
    {
        if (first == null || second == null) return false;
        if (first == second) return false;

        DirectionalOperator opFirst  = first.GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();
        DirectionalOperator opSecond = second.GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();

        bool firstIsOp  = opFirst  != null && opFirst.IsActive;
        bool secondIsOp = opSecond != null && opSecond.IsActive;

        // --- directional operator vs. ordinary ball ---
        if (firstIsOp && !secondIsOp)
            return opFirst.ConnectsTo(second);
        if (secondIsOp && !firstIsOp)
            return opSecond.ConnectsTo(first);

        // --- directional operator vs. directional operator ---
        // Two crescents never carry a connection between them; they're
        // resolved by the rewrite engine as invalid when they touch.
        if (firstIsOp && secondIsOp)
            return false;

        // --- ordinary ball vs. ordinary ball: edge-to-edge gap ---
        return EdgeGap(first, second) <= ConnectionGap;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Signed gap between the two colliders (negative means overlapping).
    /// Uses Unity's built-in closest-point query, which handles any shape.
    /// </summary>
    private static float EdgeGap(Collider2D a, Collider2D b)
    {
        // Distance() returns 0 when overlapping; we subtract a small fudge
        // so tiny overlaps still read as "touching" rather than "connected by 0".
        if (a.Distance(b).isOverlapped) return -1f;
        return a.Distance(b).distance;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallDrag.cs =====
using UnityEngine;
using Unity.Netcode;

public class BallDrag : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static bool isDragging;
    public float releaseVelocityMultiplier = 0.2f;
    public float velocityDamping = 4f;
    public float minimumVelocity = 0.04f;
    private const float MaximumReleaseSpeed = 1.8f;

    private Vector3 offset;
    private Vector2 previousMousePosition;
    private Rigidbody2D body;
    private Vector2 dragVelocity;
    private bool isBeingDragged;
    private bool draggingGroup;
    private DirectionalOperator directionalOperator;
    private bool useKinematicOperatorDrag;
    private bool hasPendingOperatorPosition;
    private Vector2 pendingOperatorPosition;

    private NetworkBall networkBall;

    private bool _waitingForGrab;
    private bool _dragAllowed;

    private Vector2 _lastConnCheckPos;
    private const float ConnectionCheckDistance = 0.05f;

    

    private void Awake()
    {
        body = GetComponent<Rigidbody2D>();
        networkBall = GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        directionalOperator = GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();
    }

    private bool IsHandPiece()
    {
        int handLayer = LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces");
        return handLayer >= 0 && gameObject.layer == handLayer;
    }

    private Camera ActiveCamera()
    {
        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        if (setup == null) return Camera.main;
        return IsHandPiece() ? setup.HandCamera : setup.BoardCamera;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Grab result callback (invoked from NetworkBall.GrabResultClientRpc)
    // ==================================================================

    public static void OnGrabResult(NetworkBall nb, bool acquired)
    {
        if (nb == null) return;
        var drag = nb.GetComponent<BallDrag>();
        if (drag == null) return;

        drag._waitingForGrab = false;

        if (!acquired)
        {
            drag._dragAllowed = false;
            drag.isBeingDragged = false;
            BallDrag.isDragging = false;
            return;
        }

        drag._dragAllowed = true;
        drag.isBeingDragged = true;
        BallDrag.isDragging = true;
        drag.body.linearVelocity = Vector2.zero;
        drag.dragVelocity = Vector2.zero;
        drag.draggingGroup = LassoSelection.IsSelected(drag);
        drag.BeginDirectionalOperatorDrag();

        var cam = drag.ActiveCamera();
        if (cam == null) return;
        Vector3 mp = cam.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);
        mp.z = 0f;
        drag.offset = drag.transform.position - mp;
        drag.previousMousePosition = mp;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Mouse
    // ==================================================================

    private void OnMouseDown()
    {
        if (!Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0) || LassoSelection.IsLassoActive ||
            Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.RightShift)) return;

        bool online = NetworkManager.Singleton != null && NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening;

        // Hand piece: drag immediately.
        if (IsHandPiece())
        {
            BeginLocalDrag();
            return;
        }

        // Online board piece: request grab, wait for confirmation.
        if (online && networkBall != null && networkBall.IsSpawned)
        {
            _waitingForGrab = true;
            _dragAllowed = false;
            isBeingDragged = false;
            isDragging = true;   // block other input while we wait
            networkBall.RequestGrabServerRpc();
            return;
        }

        // Offline board piece: drag immediately.
        BeginLocalDrag();
    }

    private void BeginLocalDrag()
    {
        if (directionalOperator == null)
            directionalOperator = GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();

        isDragging = true;
        isBeingDragged = true;
        _dragAllowed = true;
        _waitingForGrab = false;
        draggingGroup = LassoSelection.IsSelected(this);
        body.linearVelocity = Vector2.zero;
        dragVelocity = Vector2.zero;
        BeginDirectionalOperatorDrag();

        var cam = ActiveCamera();
        if (cam == null) return;
        Vector3 mp = cam.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);
        mp.z = 0f;
        offset = transform.position - mp;
        previousMousePosition = mp;
    }

    private void OnMouseDrag()
    {
        if (_waitingForGrab) return;
        if (!_dragAllowed) return;

        if (!isBeingDragged || !Input.GetMouseButton(0) || LassoSelection.IsLassoActive) return;

        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        if (setup == null) return;

        bool mouseInHand = Input.mousePosition.y < Screen.height * setup.HandFraction;

        bool crossed = false;
        if (IsHandPiece() && !mouseInHand) crossed = true;
        else if (!IsHandPiece() && mouseInHand) crossed = true;

        if (crossed)
        {
            CompleteDirectionalOperatorDrag();
            isBeingDragged = false;
            isDragging = false;
            body.linearVelocity = Vector2.zero;

            // Release any board lock before transferring.
            if (!IsHandPiece() && networkBall != null && networkBall.IsSpawned && _dragAllowed)
                networkBall.RequestReleaseServerRpc();

            _dragAllowed = false;

            bool online = NetworkManager.Singleton != null && NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening;

            if (IsHandPiece())
            {
                BallTransferManager.Instance.StartTransferFromHand(gameObject, transform.position);
            }
            else if (online && networkBall != null && networkBall.IsSpawned)
            {
                BallTransferManager.Instance.StartTransferFromBoard(networkBall, transform.position);
            }
            else
            {
                BallTransferManager.Instance.StartTransferFromLocalBoard(gameObject, transform.position);
            }

            return;
        }

        var cam = ActiveCamera();
        if (cam == null) return;

        Vector3 mousePosition = cam.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);
        mousePosition.z = 0f;
        Vector3 nextPosition = mousePosition + offset;

        Vector2 mouseDelta = (Vector2)mousePosition - previousMousePosition;
        float deltaTime = Mathf.Max(Time.deltaTime, Mathf.Epsilon);
        if (mouseDelta.sqrMagnitude > 0f) dragVelocity = mouseDelta / deltaTime;
        previousMousePosition = mousePosition;
        Vector2 movement = (Vector2)(nextPosition - transform.position);

        if (directionalOperator != null && directionalOperator.IsActive)
        {
            directionalOperator.ApplyRotateDrag(mouseDelta);

            if (useKinematicOperatorDrag)
            {
                float targetAngle = directionalOperator.CurrentTargetAngle;
                Vector2 pinnedPos = directionalOperator.CenterForMouseToKeepGrabPoint(
                    mousePosition, targetAngle);

                // Teleport both body and transform — same mechanism the ball uses.
                body.position      = pinnedPos;
                transform.position = pinnedPos;
                body.rotation      = targetAngle;
                transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, targetAngle);

                if (networkBall != null && networkBall.IsSpawned &&
                    networkBall.CurrentZone.Value == (byte)BallZone.ServerBoard)
                {
                    networkBall.RequestBoardMoveServerRpc(pinnedPos, targetAngle);
                }
            }
        }
        else if (networkBall != null && networkBall.IsSpawned)
        {
            body.position = nextPosition;
            body.transform.position = nextPosition;

            if (networkBall.CurrentZone.Value == (byte)BallZone.ServerBoard)
                networkBall.RequestBoardMoveServerRpc(nextPosition, transform.eulerAngles.z);
        }
        else
        {
            body.position = nextPosition;
            body.transform.position = nextPosition;
        }

        if (!useKinematicOperatorDrag)
            body.linearVelocity = Vector2.zero;
        if (draggingGroup) LassoSelection.TryMoveSelection(this, movement);
    }

    private void OnMouseUp()
    {
        
        // If we released over the bag button, return the bag ball to the bag.
        if (IsBagBall() && BagButton.Instance != null && BagButton.Instance.IsMouseOver())
        {
            CompleteDirectionalOperatorDrag();
            ReturnBagBallToPool();
            return;
        }

        // Release the lock if we held one.
        if (!IsHandPiece() && networkBall != null && networkBall.IsSpawned && _dragAllowed)
            networkBall.RequestReleaseServerRpc();

        _dragAllowed = false;
        _waitingForGrab = false;

        CompleteDirectionalOperatorDrag();
        if (!isBeingDragged) return;

        isDragging = false;
        isBeingDragged = false;
        draggingGroup = false;

        if (!IsHandPiece())
        {
            float speed = Mathf.Min(
                dragVelocity.magnitude * Mathf.Max(0f, releaseVelocityMultiplier),
                MaximumReleaseSpeed);
            if (speed > 0.02f)
            {
                body.WakeUp();
                body.linearVelocity = dragVelocity.normalized * speed;
            }
        }
    }

    private bool IsBagBall()
    {
        if (GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null) return true;
        return networkBall != null && networkBall.IsBagBall.Value;
    }

    private void ReturnBagBallToPool()
    {
        string content = GetComponent<BallNumber>()?.GetRawDisplayedKey();
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(content)) return;

        if (IsHandPiece())
        {
            var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
            if (spawner != null) spawner.DestroyHandBall(gameObject);

            if (BagManager.Instance != null)
                BagManager.Instance.RequestReturnToBag(content);
        }
        else if (networkBall != null && networkBall.IsSpawned)
        {
            // Server RPC despawns + increments pool.
            if (NetworkBallManager.Instance != null)
                NetworkBallManager.Instance.RequestBagReturnFromBoardServerRpc(
                    networkBall.NetworkObject, content);
        }
        else
        {
            // Offline board bag ball: destroy + return locally.
            var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
            if (spawner != null) spawner.DestroyBoardBall(gameObject);

            if (BagManager.Instance != null)
                BagManager.Instance.RequestReturnToBag(content);
        }

        _dragAllowed = false;
        _waitingForGrab = false;
        isDragging = false;
        isBeingDragged = false;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Misc
    // ==================================================================

    public void MoveByGroupDelta(Vector2 delta)
    {
        if (body == null) return;
        body.position += delta;
        body.linearVelocity = Vector2.zero;
    }

    public static void CancelActiveDragForLasso() => isDragging = false;

    private void FixedUpdate()
    {
        if (isBeingDragged || body.linearVelocity.sqrMagnitude == 0f) return;
        body.linearVelocity *= Mathf.Exp(-Mathf.Max(0f, velocityDamping) * Time.fixedDeltaTime);
        float stopSpeed = Mathf.Max(0f, minimumVelocity);
        if (body.linearVelocity.sqrMagnitude < stopSpeed * stopSpeed) body.linearVelocity = Vector2.zero;
    }

    private void CompleteDirectionalOperatorDrag()
    {
        if (!useKinematicOperatorDrag)
            return;

        directionalOperator.EndRotateDrag();
        directionalOperator.EndDrag();
        useKinematicOperatorDrag = false;
    }

    private void BeginDirectionalOperatorDrag()
    {
        useKinematicOperatorDrag =
            directionalOperator != null && directionalOperator.IsActive;
        if (!useKinematicOperatorDrag) return;

        directionalOperator.BeginDrag();

        // Record where the player grabbed in world space so the triangle can
        // rotate around that point as the mouse moves.
        var cam = ActiveCamera();
        if (cam != null)
        {
            Vector3 mp = cam.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);
            mp.z = 0f;
            directionalOperator.BeginRotateDrag(mp);
        }
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallImpactLimiter.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

[RequireComponent(typeof(Rigidbody2D))]
public class BallImpactLimiter : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Tooltip("Max speed the ball can have immediately after any collision.")]
    [SerializeField] private float maxPostCollisionSpeed = 1.0f;

    private Rigidbody2D body;

    private void Awake() => body = GetComponent<Rigidbody2D>();

    private void OnCollisionEnter2D(Collision2D _)
    {
        ClampNow();
    }

    private void OnCollisionStay2D(Collision2D _)
    {
        ClampNow();
    }

    private void ClampNow()
    {
        if (body == null) return;
        float cap = maxPostCollisionSpeed;
        if (body.linearVelocity.sqrMagnitude > cap * cap)
            body.linearVelocity = body.linearVelocity.normalized * cap;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallInputController.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

// Reads keyboard input for one ball and manages the active edit session.
public class BallInputController : MonoBehaviour
{
    // Only one ball is edited at a time; opening another commits the previous edit.
    private static BallInputController activeEditor;

    // Automatically finish an edit after the player pauses typing.
    private const float EditTimeout = 5f;

    // Ball reference and timing state used to process input once per frame.
    private BallNumber ball;
    private float lastInputTime;
    private int lastInputFrame = -1;

    public bool IsEditing { get; private set; }

    // Find the BallNumber component that owns this input controller.
    private void Awake()
    {
        ball = GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ball == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError("BallInputController requires a BallNumber component.", this);
        }
    }

    // Continue editing the active ball until timeout or Enter is pressed.
    private void Update()
    {
        if (!IsEditing || activeEditor != this || ball == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (lastInputFrame == Time.frameCount)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (Time.unscaledTime - lastInputTime >= EditTimeout ||
            Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Return) || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadEnter))
        {
            FinishEditing();
            return;
        }

        if (ball.InputMode == BallInputMode.DisplayKey)
        {
            ProcessTextInput();
        }
        else
        {
            ProcessNumberInput();
        }
    }

        // Start editing on the first valid key over the ball, or toggle its lock with Enter.
        private void OnMouseOver()
        {
            if (ball == null) return;

        // Bag balls are read-only.
        if (GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null) return;
        var nb = GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (nb != null && nb.IsBagBall.Value) return;
            
        if (ball == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        // Don't handle per-ball input while a lasso selection is active.
        if (LassoSelection.GetSelectedBalls().Count > 0)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Return) || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadEnter))
        {
            ball.ToggleLock();
            if (IsEditing)
            {
                FinishEditing();
            }
            return;
        }

        if (IsEditing)
        {
            return;
        }

        if (ball.InputMode == BallInputMode.DisplayKey)
        {
            if (!HasTextInput())
            {
                return;
            }

            BeginEditing();
            ProcessTextInput();
            return;
        }

        if (!HasNumberInput(ball.InputMode == BallInputMode.Algebra))
        {
            return;
        }

        BeginEditing();
        ProcessNumberInput();
    }

    // Commit current text when the pointer leaves the ball.
    private void OnMouseExit()
    {
        if (IsEditing)
        {
            FinishEditing();
        }
    }

    // Stop editing without applying an additional change.
    public void CancelEditing()
    {
        IsEditing = false;
        if (activeEditor == this)
        {
            activeEditor = null;
        }
    }

    // Read a newly pressed digit from either the number row or numeric keypad.
    private bool TryReadDigit(out int digit)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i <= 9; i++)
        {
            KeyCode numberKey = (KeyCode)((int)KeyCode.Alpha0 + i);
            KeyCode keypadNumberKey = (KeyCode)((int)KeyCode.Keypad0 + i);
            if (Input.GetKeyDown(numberKey) || Input.GetKeyDown(keypadNumberKey))
            {
                digit = i;
                return true;
            }
        }

        digit = 0;
        return false;
    }

    // Read a newly pressed alphabetic key and return its lowercase letter.
    private bool TryReadLetter(out char letter)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < 26; i++)
        {
            KeyCode letterKey = (KeyCode)((int)KeyCode.A + i);
            if (Input.GetKeyDown(letterKey))
            {
                letter = (char)('a' + i);
                return true;
            }
        }

        letter = '\0';
        return false;
    }

    // Detect keys that are valid in algebra/number entry mode.
    private bool HasNumberInput(bool allowLetters)
    {
        return TryReadDigit(out _) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Backspace) ||
               allowLetters && TryReadLetter(out _) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Plus) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadPlus) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Equals) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Minus) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadMinus) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadMultiply) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Alpha8) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Slash) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadDivide) ||
               allowLetters &&
               (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.LeftBracket) ||
                Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.At) ||
                Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Alpha2) &&
                (Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.RightShift)));
    }

    // Apply one numeric or algebraic key to the current ball value.
    private void ProcessNumberInput()
    {
        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Backspace))
        {
            if (ball.ClearContent())
            {
                MarkInputChanged();
            }
            return;
        }

        int number = ball.NumberValue;
        char variable = ball.Variable;
        int power = ball.VariablePower;

        if (ball.InputMode == BallInputMode.Algebra && (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.At) ||
            (Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.RightShift)) &&
            Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Alpha2)))
        {
            ball.SetAlgebraContent(number, '\0', 0);
            MarkInputChanged();
            return;
        }

        if (ball.InputMode == BallInputMode.Algebra && TryReadLetter(out char letter))
        {
            if (variable == letter)
            {
                power++;
            }
            else
            {
                variable = letter;
                power = 1;
                if (number == 0)
                {
                    number = 1;
                }
            }

            ball.SetAlgebraContent(number, variable, power);
            MarkInputChanged();
            return;
        }

        if (ball.InputMode == BallInputMode.Algebra && Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.LeftBracket))
        {
            if (power > 1)
            {
                power--;
            }
            else if (power == 1)
            {
                variable = '\0';
                power = 0;
            }

            ball.SetAlgebraContent(number, variable, power);
            MarkInputChanged();
            return;
        }

        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Plus) || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadPlus) ||
            (Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.RightShift)) &&
            Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Equals))
        {
            number++;
            if (number == 0)
            {
                variable = '\0';
                power = 0;
            }

            ball.SetAlgebraContent(number, variable, power);
            MarkInputChanged();
            return;
        }

        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Minus) || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadMinus))
        {
            number--;
            if (number == 0)
            {
                variable = '\0';
                power = 0;
            }

            ball.SetAlgebraContent(number, variable, power);
            MarkInputChanged();
            return;
        }

        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadMultiply) ||
            (Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.RightShift)) &&
            Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Alpha8))
        {
            ball.SetAlgebraContent(number * 2, variable, power);
            MarkInputChanged();
            return;
        }

        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Slash) || Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.KeypadDivide))
        {
            ball.SetAlgebraContent(Mathf.FloorToInt(number / 2f), variable, power);
            MarkInputChanged();
            return;
        }

        if (TryReadDigit(out int digit))
        {
            ball.SetAlgebraContent(digit, variable, power);
            MarkInputChanged();
        }
    }

    // Detect typed characters, backspace, or space in free-form text mode.
    private bool HasTextInput()
    {
        return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Input.inputString) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Backspace) ||
               Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Space);
    }

    // Apply text-mode input; space resets the ball to empty.
    private void ProcessTextInput()
    {
        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Space))
