        {
            ball.ResetToEmpty();
            MarkInputChanged();
            return;
        }

        if (ball.ApplyTextInput(Input.inputString, Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Backspace)))
        {
            MarkInputChanged();
        }
    }

    // Make this the active editor, finishing any other ball's edit first.
    private void BeginEditing()
    {
        if (activeEditor != null && activeEditor != this)
        {
            activeEditor.FinishEditing();
        }

        activeEditor = this;
        IsEditing = true;
    }

    // Record activity time/frame and refresh the ball's visible label.
    private void MarkInputChanged()
    {
        lastInputTime = Time.unscaledTime;
        lastInputFrame = Time.frameCount;
        ball.RefreshDisplay();
    }

    // Commit the edit and release the global active-editor slot.
    private void FinishEditing()
    {
        IsEditing = false;
        ball.FinishEditing();
        if (activeEditor == this)
        {
            activeEditor = null;
        }
    }
}


// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallNumber.cs =====
using System.Globalization;
using UnityEngine;

// Selects whether a ball accepts algebra, numbers only, or free-form display-key text.
public enum BallInputMode
{
    // Store an integer coefficient with an optional variable and exponent.
    Algebra,
    // Store a numeric coefficient without allowing variables.
    NumbersOnly,
    // Store the exact text typed into the ball.
    DisplayKey
}

// Stores a ball's mathematical value and coordinates editing, solving, and display.
public class BallNumber : MonoBehaviour
{
    // Cached helper components responsible for visuals and keyboard input.
    private BallAppearance appearance;
    private BallInputController inputController;
    private DirectionalOperator directionalOperator;

    // Algebra-mode value: an integer coefficient, optional variable, and its exponent.
    private int ballNumber;
    private char ballVariable;
    private int variablePower;

    // Display-key text is stored separately from algebra-mode values.
    private string displayedKey = string.Empty;
    private BallInputMode inputMode = BallInputMode.Algebra;
    private bool isSolverOutput;
    private bool isLocked;

        // Prefab-swap tracking (see BallPrefabSwapper).
    private bool _swapQueued;
    private bool _allowSwaps;

    // Read-only views used by input handling and other gameplay scripts.
    public int NumberValue
    {
        get { return ballNumber; }
    }

    public char Variable
    {
        get { return ballVariable; }
    }

    public int VariablePower
    {
        get { return variablePower; }
    }

    public BallInputMode InputMode
    {
        get { return inputMode; }
    }

    public bool IsSolverOutput
    {
        get { return isSolverOutput; }
    }

    public bool IsLocked
    {
        get { return isLocked; }
    }

    public bool IsBeingEdited
    {
        get { return inputController != null && inputController.IsEditing; }
    }

    public long ChangeVersion 
    { 
        get; private set; 
    }

    // Find or add the companion components, then show the initial empty value.
    private void Awake()
    {
        appearance = GetComponent<BallAppearance>();
        if (appearance == null)
        {
            appearance = gameObject.AddComponent<BallAppearance>();
        }

        inputController = GetComponent<BallInputController>();
        if (inputController == null)
        {
            inputController = gameObject.AddComponent<BallInputController>();
        }

        directionalOperator = GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();

        RefreshDisplay();

        StartCoroutine(AllowSwapsNextFrame());
    }

    // Switch entry modes and clear the value left over from the previous mode.
    public void SetInputMode(BallInputMode mode)
    {
        inputController.CancelEditing();
        inputMode = mode;
        ResetToEmpty();
    }

    // Clear this ball and remove its locked state.
    public void ResetToEmpty()
    {
        ClearContent();
        isLocked = false;
    }

    // Erase all stored content and report whether anything changed.
    public bool ClearContent()
    {
        bool changed = ballNumber != 0 || ballVariable != '\0' || variablePower != 0 ||
                       displayedKey.Length > 0 || isSolverOutput;
        ballNumber = 0;
        ballVariable = '\0';
        variablePower = 0;
        displayedKey = string.Empty;
        isSolverOutput = false;
        if (changed) { ChangeVersion++; MathEquationBuilder.MarkContentChanged(); }
        RefreshDisplay();;
        return changed;
    }

    // Provide text to the equation builder only for display-key balls.
    public string GetMathContent()
    {
        return inputMode == BallInputMode.DisplayKey && (!isSolverOutput || isLocked)
            ? displayedKey
            : string.Empty;
    }

    // Read calculated content without exposing it as user-entered equation text.
    public string GetSolverOutputContent()
    {
        return isSolverOutput ? displayedKey : string.Empty;
    }

    public string GetSolverOutputDisplayContent()
    {
        return isSolverOutput ? FormatSolverOutputForDisplay(displayedKey) : string.Empty;
    }

    public void SetEquationState(byte state)
    {
        if (appearance != null) appearance.SetEquationState(state);
    }

    public void SetAmbiguousOperand(bool isAmbiguous)
    {
        if (appearance != null)
        {
            appearance.SetAmbiguousOperand(isAmbiguous);
        }
    }

    // Toggle whether this ball is protected from editing or solver output.
    public void ToggleLock()
    {
        isLocked = !isLocked;
        ChangeVersion++;
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkContentChanged();
        RefreshDisplay();
    }

    public void SetAlgebraContent(int number, char variable, int power)
    {
        ballNumber = number;
        ballVariable = variable;
        variablePower = power;
        ChangeVersion++;
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkContentChanged();
    }

    // Apply typed display-key text, or clear it when backspace is pressed.
    public bool ApplyTextInput(string typedText, bool backspaceKeyDown)
    {
        if (backspaceKeyDown || typedText.IndexOf('\b') >= 0)
        {
            return ClearContent();
        }

        bool changed = false;
        if (isSolverOutput && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(typedText))
        {
            displayedKey = string.Empty;
            isSolverOutput = false;
            changed = true;
        }

        foreach (char character in typedText)
        {
            if (!char.IsControl(character) && displayedKey.Length < 24)
            {
                displayedKey += character;
                changed = true;
            }
        }

                if (changed) { ChangeVersion++; MathEquationBuilder.MarkContentChanged(); }
        return changed;
    }

    // Store a calculated result as display-key content and redraw the ball.
    public void SetSolverOutput(string content)
    {
        inputMode = BallInputMode.DisplayKey;
        displayedKey = content;
        isSolverOutput = true;
        RefreshDisplay();
    }

    // Remove a previous calculated answer when its equation is no longer valid.
    public void ClearSolverOutput()
    {
        if (!isSolverOutput)
        {
            return;
        }

        displayedKey = string.Empty;
        isSolverOutput = false;
        RefreshDisplay();
    }

    // Format the current mode's content and send it to the appearance component.
    public void RefreshDisplay()
    {
        if (appearance == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        string content = inputMode == BallInputMode.DisplayKey
            ? displayedKey
            : FormatNumber();

        // Explicit reciprocal tokens such as '/3' render as '1/3'.
        if (inputMode == BallInputMode.DisplayKey && !isSolverOutput &&
            content.Length > 0 && content[0] == '/')
        {
            string suffix = content.Substring(1).Trim();
            if (suffix.Length > 0)
                content = "1/" + suffix;
        }

        if (isSolverOutput)
        {
            content = FormatSolverOutputForDisplay(content);
        }

        if (directionalOperator != null)
        {
            string operatorContent = inputMode == BallInputMode.DisplayKey && !isSolverOutput
                ? displayedKey.Trim()
                : string.Empty;
            directionalOperator.Refresh(operatorContent);
        }

        if (content.Trim() == "=") appearance.MarkAsEqualsBall();

        appearance.SetContent(
            content,
            inputMode == BallInputMode.DisplayKey,
            isSolverOutput && !isLocked);
        CheckForPrefabSwap();
    }

    // Simplify editable addition text before ending the edit session.
    public void FinishEditing()
    {
        if (inputMode == BallInputMode.DisplayKey &&
            MathExpression.TrySimplifyAddition(displayedKey, out string simplified))
        {
            displayedKey = simplified;
        }

        RefreshDisplay();
    }

    // Limit solver-result display to two decimal places without changing stored text.
    private string FormatSolverOutputForDisplay(string content)
    {
        if (!decimal.TryParse(
                content,
                NumberStyles.Float,
                CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                out decimal value))
        {
            return content;
        }

        string formatted = value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        int decimalPoint = formatted.IndexOf('.');
        if (decimalPoint >= 0 && formatted.Length - decimalPoint > 3)
        {
            return formatted.Substring(0, decimalPoint + 3);
        }

        return formatted;
    }

    // Turn the algebra fields into text such as "7", "-x", or "3y^2".
    private string FormatNumber()
    {
        if (ballNumber == 0)
        {
            return string.Empty;
        }

        if (ballVariable == '\0')
        {
            return ballNumber.ToString();
        }

        string coefficient = ballNumber == 1
            ? string.Empty
            : ballNumber == -1
                ? "-"
                : ballNumber.ToString();
        string power = variablePower > 1 ? "^" + variablePower : string.Empty;
        return coefficient + ballVariable + power;
    }

    // ---- Network-facing raw accessors (add to BallNumber) ----

        public string GetRawDisplayedKey() => displayedKey;

        public void SetRawDisplayedKey(string key, bool solverOutput)
        {
            displayedKey   = key ?? string.Empty;
            isSolverOutput = solverOutput;
                        // Solver writes must NOT invalidate the cache; user input must.
            if (!solverOutput) { ChangeVersion++; MathEquationBuilder.MarkContentChanged(); }
        }

        public void SetInputModeRaw(BallInputMode mode)
        {
            inputMode = mode;
            // Intentionally skip ResetToEmpty() here; the caller sends the full state.
        }

                public void SetAlgebraContentRaw(int number, char variable, int power)
        {
            ballNumber    = number;
            ballVariable  = variable;
            variablePower = power;
        }

        public void SetLockedRaw(bool locked) => isLocked = locked;

            // ---- Prefab swapping (see BallPrefabSwapper) ----

    private System.Collections.IEnumerator AllowSwapsNextFrame()
    {
        // Let the first RefreshDisplay (from Awake/Start) settle before we
        // start comparing prefab kinds. Otherwise a freshly spawned triangle
        // with content "-" would immediately try to swap back to a ball.
        yield return null;
        _allowSwaps = true;
    }

    private void CheckForPrefabSwap()
    {
        string contentNow = GetMathContent().Trim();
        bool currDir = GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>() != null;
        bool shouldDir = DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(contentNow);
        Debug.Log($"[Swap] ball={name} content='{contentNow}' " +
                $"currentlyDirectional={currDir} shouldBeDirectional={shouldDir} " +
                $"_allowSwaps={_allowSwaps} _swapQueued={_swapQueued}");

        if (!_allowSwaps) return;
        if (_swapQueued) return;

        bool currentlyDirectional = GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>() != null;
        bool shouldBeDirectional  = DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(
            GetMathContent().Trim());

        if (currentlyDirectional == shouldBeDirectional) return;

        _swapQueued = true;
        StartCoroutine(ExecuteSwapNextFrame());
    }

    private System.Collections.IEnumerator ExecuteSwapNextFrame()
    {
        // Defer by one frame: RefreshDisplay is still executing on this
        // object, and we're about to destroy it.
        yield return null;

        if (BallPrefabSwapper.Instance != null)
            BallPrefabSwapper.Instance.Swap(this);
        else
            Debug.LogError("BallPrefabSwapper.Instance is null. Add a " +
                           "BallPrefabSwapper component to a scene GameObject.");

        _swapQueued = false;   // in case Swap didn't destroy us (e.g. no-op)
    }
}


// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallPrefabSwapper.cs =====
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Performs the despawn+spawn dance when a ball's content changes kind
/// (e.g. '2' → '-'). Called from BallNumber once per frame when a swap
/// is queued.
///
/// Handles three contexts:
///   - Offline (no NetworkManager): local Destroy + Spawner.Spawn*
///   - Server / host with networked board ball: Despawn + SpawnBoard*
///   - Client with networked board ball: RPC to server, which does the above
///
/// Hand pieces are never networked, so they always go through the offline path.
/// </summary>
public class BallPrefabSwapper : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static BallPrefabSwapper Instance { get; private set; }

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
        Instance = this;
    }

    public void Swap(BallNumber oldBall)
    {
        if (oldBall == null) return;

        string content = oldBall.GetMathContent().Trim();
        bool shouldBeDirectional  = DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(content);
        bool currentlyDirectional = oldBall.GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>() != null;
        if (shouldBeDirectional == currentlyDirectional) return;

        var go = oldBall.gameObject;
        var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null) return;

        // ---- capture state from the old ball ----
        Vector3 pos      = go.transform.position;
        float   rotZ     = go.transform.eulerAngles.z;
        string  key      = oldBall.GetRawDisplayedKey();
        int     number   = oldBall.NumberValue;
        char    variable = oldBall.Variable;
        int     power    = oldBall.VariablePower;
        byte    mode     = (byte)oldBall.InputMode;
        bool    solverO  = oldBall.IsSolverOutput;
        bool    locked   = oldBall.IsLocked;

        var nb = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        bool isNetworked = nb != null && nb.IsSpawned;
        bool isHand      = go.layer == LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces");
        bool isBag       = go.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null
                           || (nb != null && nb.IsBagBall.Value);

        var nm = NetworkManager.Singleton;
        bool online   = nm != null && nm.IsListening;
        bool isServer = nm != null && nm.IsServer;

        // ==========================================================
        // Case 1: networked board ball
        // ==========================================================
        if (isNetworked && !isHand)
        {
            if (online && !isServer)
            {
                // Client: ask server to swap.
                if (NetworkBallManager.Instance != null)
                {
                    NetworkBallManager.Instance.RequestSwapBallServerRpc(
                        nb.NetworkObject, shouldBeDirectional,
                        key, number, variable, power, mode, solverO, locked, isBag, rotZ);
                }
                return;
            }

            // Server / host: do it directly.
            nb.NetworkObject.Despawn(true);

            if (shouldBeDirectional)
                NetworkBallManager.Instance.SpawnBoardOperator(pos, key, rotZ);
            else
                NetworkBallManager.Instance.SpawnBoardBall(pos, key, rotZ);

            RestoreContentOnNewestBoard(
                shouldBeDirectional, key, number, variable, power,
                mode, solverO, locked, isBag);
            return;
        }

        // ==========================================================
        // Case 2: hand piece (local, never networked)
        // ==========================================================
        if (isHand)
        {
            spawner.DestroyHandBall(go);

            GameObject newBall = shouldBeDirectional
                ? spawner.SpawnLocalHandOperator(pos, key, rotZ)
                : spawner.SpawnLocalHandBallReturning(pos, key, number, variable, power, rotZ);

            if (newBall != null)
            {
                var bn = newBall.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
                if (bn != null)
                {
                    bn.SetInputModeRaw((BallInputMode)mode);
                    bn.SetRawDisplayedKey(key, solverO);
                    bn.SetLockedRaw(locked);
                    bn.RefreshDisplay();
                }
                if (isBag && newBall.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() == null)
                {
                    newBall.AddComponent<BagBallMarker>();
                    if (newBall.GetComponent<BagRing>() == null)
                        newBall.AddComponent<BagRing>();
                }
            }
            return;
        }

        // ==========================================================
        // Case 3: offline board ball (no NetworkBall, not on hand layer)
        // ==========================================================
        spawner.DestroyBoardBall(go);

        GameObject newLocal = shouldBeDirectional
            ? spawner.SpawnLocalBoardOperator(pos, key, rotZ)
            : spawner.SpawnBallAtPublicReturning(pos, key, rotZ);

        if (newLocal != null)
        {
            var bn = newLocal.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
            if (bn != null)
            {
                bn.SetInputModeRaw((BallInputMode)mode);
                bn.SetRawDisplayedKey(key, solverO);
                bn.SetLockedRaw(locked);
                bn.RefreshDisplay();
            }
            if (isBag && newLocal.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() == null)
            {
                newLocal.AddComponent<BagBallMarker>();
                if (newLocal.GetComponent<BagRing>() == null)
                    newLocal.AddComponent<BagRing>();
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// After a server-side networked swap, find the most recently spawned
    /// board ball (the one we just spawned) and restore its content, bag
    /// flag, and lock. Board balls are appended to the Spawner's
    /// boardBalls list, so "last in the list" is the one we want.
    /// </summary>
    private void RestoreContentOnNewestBoard(
        bool wasDirectional, string key, int number, char variable, int power,
        byte mode, bool solverO, bool locked, bool isBag)
    {
        var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null) return;

        var all = spawner.GetAllBoardPieces();
        if (all == null || all.Count == 0) return;

        var newest = all[all.Count - 1];
        if (newest == null) return;

        var bn = newest.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (bn != null)
        {
            bn.SetInputModeRaw((BallInputMode)mode);
            bn.SetRawDisplayedKey(key, solverO);
            bn.SetLockedRaw(locked);
            bn.RefreshDisplay();
        }

        var nb = newest.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (nb != null)
        {
            if (isBag) nb.IsBagBall.Value = true;
            nb.PushImmediate();
        }
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallSpeedCap.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

[RequireComponent(typeof(Rigidbody2D))]
public class BallSpeedCap : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private float maxSpeed = 2f;

    private Rigidbody2D body;

    private void Awake() => body = GetComponent<Rigidbody2D>();

    private void FixedUpdate()
    {
        if (body == null) return;
        if (body.linearVelocity.sqrMagnitude > maxSpeed * maxSpeed)
            body.linearVelocity = body.linearVelocity.normalized * maxSpeed;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallState.cs =====
using System;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;

[Serializable]
public struct BallState
{
    public Vector2 position;
    public float   rotationZ;
    public int     ballNumber;
    public char    ballVariable;
    public int     variablePower;
    public string  displayedKey;
    public byte    inputMode;
    public bool    isSolverOutput;
    public bool    isLocked;
    public uint    ownerClientId;
    public byte    zone;
    public bool    isBagBall;

    public const int MaxDisplayedKeyLength = 24;

    public static BallState FromBall(BallNumber ball, uint ownerClientId, byte zone, bool isBagBall)
    {
        return new BallState
        {
            position       = ball.transform.position,
            rotationZ      = ball.transform.eulerAngles.z,
            ballNumber     = ball.NumberValue,
            ballVariable   = ball.Variable,
            variablePower  = ball.VariablePower,
            displayedKey   = ball.GetRawDisplayedKey(),
            inputMode      = (byte)ball.InputMode,
            isSolverOutput = ball.IsSolverOutput,
            isLocked       = ball.IsLocked,
            ownerClientId  = ownerClientId,
            zone           = zone,
            isBagBall      = isBagBall,
        };
    }

    public void ApplyTo(BallNumber ball, bool authoritative)
    {
        ball.transform.position = position;
        bool isDirectionalOperator = inputMode == (byte)BallInputMode.DisplayKey &&
            !isSolverOutput &&
            DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(
                string.IsNullOrEmpty(displayedKey) ? string.Empty : displayedKey.Trim());
        if (isDirectionalOperator)
        {
            float currentRotation = ball.transform.eulerAngles.z;
            if (Mathf.Abs(Mathf.DeltaAngle(currentRotation, rotationZ)) > 0.1f)
                MathEquationBuilder.MarkConnectionsChanged();
            ball.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, rotationZ);
        }

        ball.SetInputModeRaw((BallInputMode)inputMode);
        ball.SetAlgebraContentRaw(ballNumber, ballVariable, variablePower);
        ball.SetRawDisplayedKey(displayedKey ?? string.Empty, isSolverOutput);
        ball.SetLockedRaw(isLocked);
        ball.RefreshDisplay();

        if (authoritative)
        {
            var rb = ball.GetComponent<Rigidbody2D>();
            if (rb != null)
            {
                rb.linearVelocity = Vector2.zero;
                if (DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperator(ball))
                    rb.angularVelocity = 0f;
            }
        }
    }

    public void Write(BinaryWriter w)
    {
        w.Write(position.x);
        w.Write(position.y);
        w.Write(rotationZ);
        w.Write(ballNumber);
        w.Write((ushort)ballVariable);
        w.Write(variablePower);
        w.Write(inputMode);
        w.Write(isSolverOutput);
        w.Write(isLocked);
        w.Write(ownerClientId);
        w.Write(zone);
        w.Write(isBagBall);

        string key = displayedKey ?? string.Empty;
        if (key.Length > MaxDisplayedKeyLength) key = key.Substring(0, MaxDisplayedKeyLength);
        w.Write((byte)key.Length);
        for (int i = 0; i < key.Length; i++) w.Write(key[i]);
    }

    public static BallState Read(BinaryReader r)
    {
        var s = new BallState
        {
            position       = new Vector2(r.ReadSingle(), r.ReadSingle()),
            rotationZ      = r.ReadSingle(),
            ballNumber     = r.ReadInt32(),
            ballVariable   = (char)r.ReadUInt16(),
            variablePower  = r.ReadInt32(),
            inputMode      = r.ReadByte(),
            isSolverOutput = r.ReadBoolean(),
            isLocked       = r.ReadBoolean(),
            ownerClientId  = r.ReadUInt32(),
            zone           = r.ReadByte(),
            isBagBall      = r.ReadBoolean(),
        };
        int len = r.ReadByte();
        var chars = new char[len];
        for (int i = 0; i < len; i++) chars[i] = r.ReadChar();
        s.displayedKey = new string(chars);
        return s;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallSyntaxChecker.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

// Per-ball local syntax validation. Runs before the rewrite engine and
// marks balls red if they have no valid role in their immediate neighborhood.
// Under the new semantics:
//   - numbers, algebra, '/N', '/x'       -> operands  (merge by adjacency)
//   - sin/cos/..., %, !, 1/x              -> functions (consume one operand)
//   - -, /, ^, ^N, ^x, *, +               -> operators
//   - (, )                                -> brackets
//   - =                                   -> region splitter
public static class BallSyntaxChecker
{
    public static void MarkLocalSyntaxErrors(
        List<BallNumber> cluster,
        Collider2D[] colliders,
        HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        var neighborsByBall = new Dictionary<BallNumber, List<BallNumber>>();
        for (int i = 0; i < cluster.Count; i++)
        {
            var list = new List<BallNumber>();
            if (colliders[i] != null)
            {
                for (int j = 0; j < cluster.Count; j++)
                {
                    if (i == j || colliders[j] == null) continue;
                    if (BallConnectionRules.AreConnected(colliders[i], colliders[j]))
                        list.Add(cluster[j]);
                }
            }
            neighborsByBall[cluster[i]] = list;
        }

        MarkLocalSyntaxErrors(cluster, neighborsByBall, ambiguous);
    }

    public static void MarkLocalSyntaxErrors(
        List<BallNumber> cluster,
        Dictionary<BallNumber, List<BallNumber>> neighborsByBall,
        HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        foreach (BallNumber ball in cluster)
        {
            if (ball == null) continue;

            string content = ball.GetMathContent().Trim();
            if (content.Length == 0) continue;         // empty ball: never red

            if (!neighborsByBall.TryGetValue(ball, out var neighbors))
                continue;

            int operandNeighbors = 0;
            int operatorNeighbors = 0;
            int functionNeighbors = 0;
            int bracketNeighbors = 0;

            foreach (BallNumber nb in neighbors)
            {
                string nbText = nb.GetMathContent().Trim();
                if (IsFunction(nbText)) functionNeighbors++;

                if (IsBracket(nbText))                       { bracketNeighbors++; continue; }
                if (IsOperand(nbText) || nbText.Length == 0) { operandNeighbors++; }
                if (IsOperator(nbText))                      { operatorNeighbors++; continue; }
            }

            int argumentSlots = operandNeighbors + bracketNeighbors + functionNeighbors;

            bool red = false;

            // Two operators touching → both red.
            if (IsOperator(content))
            {
                foreach (BallNumber nb in neighbors)
                {
                    if (IsOperator(nb.GetMathContent().Trim())) { red = true; break; }
                }
            }

            if (IsFunction(content))
            {
                // Functions consume exactly one operand-group.
                bool hasBracketNeighbor = false;
                int operandCount = 0;

                foreach (BallNumber nb in neighbors)
                {
                    string nbText = nb.GetMathContent().Trim();
                    if (IsBracket(nbText)) { hasBracketNeighbor = true; continue; }
                    if (IsOperand(nbText) || nbText.Length == 0) operandCount++;
                }

                if (!hasBracketNeighbor && operandCount != 1) red = true;
                // If a bracket neighbor exists, the bracket will resolve first and
                // become a single group; the arity check runs again post-bracket.
            }
            else if (IsDirectionalOperator(content))
            {
                if (argumentSlots != 2) red = true;
            }
            else if (MathSyntax.IsPowerBall(content))
            {
                if (argumentSlots != 1) red = true;
            }
            else if (IsOperator(content))
            {
                // '*' and '+' need at least 2 operand slots (brackets count).
                if (argumentSlots < 2) red = true;
            }
            else if (IsOperand(content))
            {
                // A plain operand touching 2+ functions is contested.
                if (functionNeighbors >= 2) red = true;
            }

            if (red) ambiguous.Add(ball);
        }
    }

    // --- helpers ---

    private static bool IsOperand(string content)
    {
        if (content.Length == 0) return false;
        if (double.TryParse(content,
                System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Float,
                System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out _))
            return true;
        if (content.Length == 1 && MathSyntax.IsVariableLetter(content[0])) return true;
        if (MathSyntax.IsConstantName(content)) return true;
        // '/', '/N', '/x' are NUMBERS under the new rules.
        if (MathSyntax.IsFractionBall(content)) return true;
        return false;
    }

    private static bool IsFunction(string content)
    {
        string lower = content.ToLowerInvariant();
        if (MathSyntax.IsUnaryFunctionName(lower)) return true;
        if (lower == "%" || lower == "!" || lower == "1/x") return true;
        return false;
    }

    private static bool IsOperator(string content)
    {
        return DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(content) ||
               MathSyntax.IsPowerBall(content) ||
               content == "*" || content == "\u00d7" || content == "+";
    }

    private static bool IsDirectionalOperator(string content)
    {
        return DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(content);
    }

    private static bool IsBracket(string content)
    {
        return content == "(" || content == ")";
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BallTransferManager.cs =====
using UnityEngine;
using Unity.Netcode;

/// <summary>
/// Handles a piece being dragged across the gray bar into the other world.
/// While pending, the source ball is deleted and a fake visual follows the cursor.
/// On release, spawns the real ball in whichever world the release happened in,
/// or snaps back to origin if released outside both viewports.
/// </summary>
public class BallTransferManager : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static BallTransferManager Instance { get; private set; }

    ///THIS IS WHERE I LEFT OFF I WAS SUPPOSED TO CHANGE THIS
    private struct PendingTransfer
    {
        public bool Active;
        public bool ContentReady;
        public bool ReleaseRequested;
        public bool CancelRequested;
        public BallZone SourceZone;
        public BallZone TargetZone;
        public Vector3 SourceWorldPos;
        public Vector3 ReleaseWorldPos;
        public string DisplayedKey;
        public int Number;
        public char Variable;
        public int Power;
        public byte InputMode;
        public bool IsSolverOutput;
        public bool IsLocked;
        public bool IsBagBall;
        public float RotationZ;
    }

    private PendingTransfer pending;
    private GameObject fakeVisual;

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
        Instance = this;
    }

    public bool IsTransferInProgress => pending.Active;
    public bool IsFakeVisualActive => fakeVisual != null;

    // ==========================================================
    // Called by BallDrag when a hand piece crosses the divider.
    // ==========================================================
    public void StartTransferFromHand(GameObject handPiece, Vector3 sourceWorldPos)
    {
        if (pending.Active) return;
        var ballNumber = handPiece.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ballNumber == null) return;

        bool isBag = handPiece.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null;

        // Capture content locally.
        pending.Active = true;
        pending.ContentReady = true;
        pending.ReleaseRequested = false;
        pending.SourceZone = BallZone.Hand;
        pending.SourceWorldPos = sourceWorldPos;
        pending.RotationZ = handPiece.transform.eulerAngles.z;
        pending.DisplayedKey = ballNumber.GetRawDisplayedKey();
        pending.Number = ballNumber.NumberValue;
        pending.Variable = ballNumber.Variable;
        pending.Power = ballNumber.VariablePower;
        pending.InputMode = (byte)ballNumber.InputMode;
        pending.IsSolverOutput = ballNumber.IsSolverOutput;
        pending.IsLocked = ballNumber.IsLocked;
        pending.IsBagBall = isBag;

        // Delete the local hand piece now.
        var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
        if (spawner != null) spawner.DestroyHandBall(handPiece);

        // Create the fake visual for the pending piece.
        CreateFakeVisual(pending);
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // Called by BallDrag when a board piece crosses the divider.
    // ==========================================================
    public void StartTransferFromBoard(NetworkBall networkBall, Vector3 sourceWorldPos)
    {
        if (pending.Active) return;
        if (networkBall == null || !networkBall.IsSpawned) return;

        pending.Active = true;
        pending.ContentReady = false;
        pending.ReleaseRequested = false;
        pending.SourceZone = BallZone.ServerBoard;
        pending.SourceWorldPos = sourceWorldPos;
        pending.IsBagBall = networkBall.IsBagBall.Value;
        pending.RotationZ = networkBall.Ball.transform.eulerAngles.z;

        // Ask server to despawn the board piece and send us its content.
        networkBall.RequestBoardToHandServerRpc();
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // Called by NetworkBallManager.ClientRpc when board content arrives.
    // ==========================================================
    public void ReceiveBoardContentFromServer(
    string displayedKey, int number, char variable, int power,
    byte inputMode, bool isSolverOutput, bool isLocked, bool isBagBall, float rotationZ)
    {
        if (!pending.Active || pending.SourceZone != BallZone.ServerBoard) return;

        pending.ContentReady = true;
        pending.DisplayedKey = displayedKey;
        pending.Number = number;
        pending.Variable = variable;
        pending.Power = power;
        pending.InputMode = inputMode;
        pending.IsSolverOutput = isSolverOutput;
        pending.IsLocked = isLocked;
        pending.IsBagBall = isBagBall;   
        pending.RotationZ = rotationZ;

        if (pending.CancelRequested)
        {
            RespawnSource();
            Clear();
            return;
        }

        CreateFakeVisual(pending);

        if (pending.ReleaseRequested)
            ExecuteSpawn();
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // Per-frame: fake visual follows mouse; detect mouse release.
    // ==========================================================
    private void Update()
    {
        if (!pending.Active) return;

        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        if (setup == null) return;

        // Update fake visual to follow the mouse in the target world's coords.
        bool mouseInHand = Input.mousePosition.y < Screen.height * setup.HandFraction;
        Camera targetCam = mouseInHand ? setup.HandCamera : setup.BoardCamera;

       

        if (fakeVisual != null)
        {
            Vector3 mouseWorld = targetCam.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);
            mouseWorld.z = 0f;
            fakeVisual.transform.position = mouseWorld;

            int targetLayer = (targetCam == setup.HandCamera)
                ? LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces")
                : LayerMask.NameToLayer("BoardPieces");
            ApplyLayerRecursive(fakeVisual.transform, targetLayer);
        }

        // Detect release.
        if (Input.GetMouseButtonUp(0))
        {
            // NEW: universal bag-ball return — released over bag button.
            if (pending.IsBagBall &&
                BagButton.Instance != null &&
                BagButton.Instance.IsMouseOver())
            {
                if (BagManager.Instance != null)
                    BagManager.Instance.RequestReturnToBag(pending.DisplayedKey);

                Clear();
                return;
            }

            if (!IsInAnyViewport(setup))
            {
                Cancel();
                return;
            }

            pending.ReleaseRequested = true;
            pending.TargetZone = mouseInHand ? BallZone.Hand : BallZone.ServerBoard;

            Vector3 releaseWorld = targetCam.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);
            releaseWorld.z = 0f;
            pending.ReleaseWorldPos = releaseWorld;

            if (pending.ContentReady)
                ExecuteSpawn();
        }
    }




    private bool IsInAnyViewport(DualCameraSetup setup)
    {
        // Mouse is considered "in a viewport" if it's on-screen (both viewports
        // together cover the full vertical range in the current layout).
        var m = Input.mousePosition;
        return m.x >= 0 && m.x <= Screen.width && m.y >= 0 && m.y <= Screen.height;
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // Spawn the real ball in the target world with the saved content.
    // ==========================================================
    
    private void ExecuteSpawn()
    {
        var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null) { Cancel(); return; }

        

        Vector3 pos = pending.ReleaseWorldPos;
        var targetZone = pending.TargetZone;

        if (targetZone == BallZone.Hand)
        {
            if (pending.IsBagBall)
                spawner.SpawnLocalHandBagBall(pos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
            else
                spawner.SpawnLocalHandBall(
                    pos, pending.DisplayedKey,
                    pending.Number, pending.Variable, pending.Power, pending.RotationZ);
        }
        else // board
        {
            bool online = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
                        Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening;
            bool isServer = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
                            Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsServer;

            if (online && isServer && NetworkBallManager.Instance != null)
            {
                if (pending.IsBagBall)
                    NetworkBallManager.Instance.SpawnBoardBagBall(pos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
                else
                    NetworkBallManager.Instance.SpawnBoardBall(pos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
            }
            else if (online && !isServer && NetworkBallManager.Instance != null)
            {
                if (pending.IsBagBall)
                    NetworkBallManager.Instance.RequestSpawnBoardBagBallServerRpc(
                        pos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
                else
                    NetworkBallManager.Instance.RequestSpawnBoardBallServerRpc(
                        pos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
            }
            else
            {
                // Offline: spawn locally. For bag balls we need to mark them.
                if (pending.IsBagBall)
                    SpawnOfflineBoardBagBall(spawner, pos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
                else
                    spawner.SpawnBallAtPublic(pos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
            }
        }

        Clear();
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // Cancel: put the source ball back at its original position.
    // ==========================================================
    private void Cancel()
    {
        // If content hasn't arrived yet (board→hand mid-flight), defer.
        if (!pending.ContentReady)
        {
            pending.CancelRequested = true;
            return;
        }

        RespawnSource();
        Clear();
    }

    private void RespawnSource()
    {
        var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null) return;

        if (pending.SourceZone == BallZone.Hand)
        {
            if (pending.IsBagBall)
                spawner.SpawnLocalHandBagBall(
                    pending.SourceWorldPos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
            else
                spawner.SpawnLocalHandBall(
                    pending.SourceWorldPos,
                    pending.DisplayedKey,
                    pending.Number, pending.Variable, pending.Power, pending.RotationZ);
        }
        else
        {
            bool online = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
                        Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening;
            bool isServer = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
                            Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsServer;

            if (online && isServer && NetworkBallManager.Instance != null)
            {
                if (pending.IsBagBall)
                    NetworkBallManager.Instance.SpawnBoardBagBall(
                        pending.SourceWorldPos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
                else
                    NetworkBallManager.Instance.SpawnBoardBall(
                        pending.SourceWorldPos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
            }
            else if (online && !isServer && NetworkBallManager.Instance != null)
            {
                if (pending.IsBagBall)
                    NetworkBallManager.Instance.RequestSpawnBoardBagBallServerRpc(
                        pending.SourceWorldPos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
                else
                    NetworkBallManager.Instance.RequestSpawnBoardBallServerRpc(
                        pending.SourceWorldPos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
            }
            else
            {
                if (pending.IsBagBall)
                    SpawnOfflineBoardBagBall(
                        spawner, pending.SourceWorldPos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
                else
                    spawner.SpawnBallAtPublic(
                        pending.SourceWorldPos, pending.DisplayedKey, pending.RotationZ);
            }
        }
    }

    private void Clear()
    {
        pending = default;
        if (fakeVisual != null)
        {
            Destroy(fakeVisual);
            fakeVisual = null;
        }
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // Fake visual
    // ==========================================================
    
    private void CreateFakeVisual(PendingTransfer p)
    {
        if (fakeVisual != null) return;

        var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null) return;

        bool isDirectional = DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(
            (p.DisplayedKey ?? string.Empty).Trim());
        GameObject prefab = isDirectional
            ? spawner.directionalOperatorPrefab
            : spawner.ballPrefab;
        if (prefab == null) return;

        fakeVisual = Instantiate(prefab, p.SourceWorldPos, Quaternion.identity);
        fakeVisual.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, p.RotationZ);
        fakeVisual.name = "FakePendingBallVisual";

        DestroyComponent<NetworkBall>(fakeVisual);
        DestroyComponent<Unity.Netcode.NetworkObject>(fakeVisual);
        DestroyComponent<LocalHandBall>(fakeVisual);
        DestroyComponent<BallDrag>(fakeVisual);
        DestroyComponent<Rigidbody2D>(fakeVisual);
        DestroyComponent<BallInputController>(fakeVisual);

        int defaultLayer = LayerMask.NameToLayer("Default");
        if (defaultLayer >= 0) ApplyLayerRecursive(fakeVisual.transform, defaultLayer);

        var ballNumber = fakeVisual.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ballNumber != null)
        {
            ballNumber.SetInputModeRaw((BallInputMode)p.InputMode);
            ballNumber.SetAlgebraContentRaw(p.Number, p.Variable, p.Power);
            ballNumber.SetRawDisplayedKey(p.DisplayedKey, p.IsSolverOutput);
            ballNumber.SetLockedRaw(p.IsLocked);
            ballNumber.RefreshDisplay();
        }

        if (isDirectional)
        {
            var dop = fakeVisual.GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();
            if (dop != null)
                dop.Refresh((p.DisplayedKey ?? string.Empty).Trim());
            fakeVisual.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, p.RotationZ);
        }

        foreach (Collider2D c in fakeVisual.GetComponents<Collider2D>())
            c.enabled = false;

        if (p.IsBagBall)
        {
            fakeVisual.AddComponent<BagBallMarker>();
            fakeVisual.AddComponent<BagRing>();
        }
    }

    private static void DestroyComponent<T>(GameObject go) where T : Component
    {
        var c = go.GetComponent<T>();
        if (c != null) Destroy(c);
    }

    private static void ApplyLayerRecursive(Transform t, int layer)
    {
        t.gameObject.layer = layer;
        foreach (Transform child in t) ApplyLayerRecursive(child, layer);
    }

    public void StartTransferFromLocalBoard(GameObject boardPiece, Vector3 sourceWorldPos)
    {
        if (pending.Active) return;
        var ballNumber = boardPiece.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ballNumber == null) return;

        bool isBag = boardPiece.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null;

        pending.Active = true;
        pending.ContentReady = true;
        pending.ReleaseRequested = false;
        pending.SourceZone = BallZone.ServerBoard;
        pending.SourceWorldPos = sourceWorldPos;
        pending.RotationZ = boardPiece.transform.eulerAngles.z;
        pending.DisplayedKey = ballNumber.GetRawDisplayedKey();
        pending.Number = ballNumber.NumberValue;
        pending.Variable = ballNumber.Variable;
        pending.Power = ballNumber.VariablePower;
        pending.InputMode = (byte)ballNumber.InputMode;
        pending.IsSolverOutput = ballNumber.IsSolverOutput;
        pending.IsLocked = ballNumber.IsLocked;
        pending.IsBagBall = isBag;

        // Remove the board piece locally.
        var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
        if (spawner != null) spawner.DestroyBoardBall(boardPiece);

        CreateFakeVisual(pending);
    }

    private static void SpawnOfflineBoardBagBall(
        Spawner spawner, Vector3 pos, string content, float rotationZ)
    {
        // Offline board pieces are local but still on the BoardPieces layer and
        // still participate in board physics. Reuse SpawnBallAtPublic then mark
        // the new piece as a bag ball.
        spawner.SpawnBallAtPublic(pos, content, rotationZ);

        // The most recently spawned board piece is the one we just created.
        // Mark it as a bag ball. We reach into the Spawner to get the last one.
        var last = spawner.GetAllBoardPieces();
        if (last == null || last.Count == 0) return;
        var newest = last[last.Count - 1];
        if (newest == null) return;

        if (newest.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() == null)
            newest.AddComponent<BagBallMarker>();
        if (newest.GetComponent<BagRing>() == null)
            newest.AddComponent<BagRing>();
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\BoardHandDevider.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Draws a horizontal gray divider between the two viewports and lets you
/// drag it up/down to change how much screen space the hand gets.
/// </summary>
public class BoardHandDivider : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private float stripHeightPx = 8f;
    [SerializeField] private Color stripColor = new Color(0.55f, 0.55f, 0.60f, 1f);
    [SerializeField] private Color stripHoverColor = new Color(0.85f, 0.85f, 0.90f, 1f);
    [SerializeField] private float dragSpeedFactor = 1f;

    private bool dragging;

    private void OnGUI()
    {
        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        if (setup == null) return;

        float y = Screen.height * (1f - setup.HandFraction);
        Rect strip = new Rect(0f, y - stripHeightPx * 0.5f, Screen.width, stripHeightPx);

        bool hover = strip.Contains(Event.current.mousePosition);
        GUI.color = (dragging || hover) ? stripHoverColor : stripColor;
        GUI.DrawTexture(strip, Texture2D.whiteTexture);
        GUI.color = Color.white;

        if (hover && Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0))
            dragging = true;

        if (Input.GetMouseButtonUp(0))
            dragging = false;

        if (dragging)
        {
            float newFraction = 1f - (Event.current.mousePosition.y + (1 - dragSpeedFactor) * 0f) / Screen.height;
            newFraction = 1f - Event.current.mousePosition.y / Screen.height;
            setup.HandFraction = Mathf.Clamp(newFraction, 0.15f, 0.6f);
        }
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\DirectionalOperator.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Triangle-shaped operator for '-' (subtract), '/' (divide), '^' (power).
///
///     ┌───────────────┐
///     │   flat side   │
///     │    (input)    │
///     │               │
///     └──────────────►│   tip (output)
///
/// The flat side is the input operand ("outside"). The tip is the output
/// operand ("inside"). Rotation is driven explicitly by whoever is dragging.
///
/// This component is logic only. The triangle sprite and polygon collider
/// come from the prefab. Geometry (tip and base positions) is read from the
/// polygon collider once at Awake, so resizing the prefab's triangle is
/// enough — no code changes needed.
/// </summary>
[DisallowMultipleComponent]
public sealed class DirectionalOperator : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Header("Cone tolerances")]
    [Tooltip("Half-angle (degrees) of the input cone (flat side).")]
    [SerializeField] private float inputConeHalfAngle = 75f;

    [Tooltip("Half-angle (degrees) of the output cone (tip side).")]
    [SerializeField] private float outputConeHalfAngle = 75f;

    [Tooltip("Extra reach beyond the operand's own radius.")]
    [SerializeField] private float capturePadding = 0.10f;

    private BallNumber        ball;
    private Rigidbody2D       body;
    private CircleCollider2D  circleCollider;
    private PolygonCollider2D polygonCollider;

    private Vector2 tipLocal;
    private Vector2 baseMidLocal;

    private bool            originalFreezeRotation;
    private RigidbodyType2D originalBodyType;
    private Vector3         originalLabelPosition;
    private Quaternion      originalLabelRotation;
    private Transform       labelTransform;

    private bool  initialized;
    private bool  isBeingDragged;
    private float currentTipAngle;

    private SpriteRenderer inputPortMarker;
    private SpriteRenderer outputPortMarker;
    private static Sprite _dotSprite;

    [SerializeField] private float tipTurnSpeedDegPerSec = 720f;
    private float smoothedTipAngle;

    [Header("Rotate-drag (grab point leads)")]
    [Tooltip("Max turn speed (deg/sec) when aligning the grab point to the mouse movement direction.")]
    [SerializeField] private float grabTurnSpeedDegPerSec = 360f;

    private Vector2 _grabLocal;
    private bool    _hasGrabLocal;
    private float   _grabLocalAngleDeg;

    public bool IsActive { get; private set; }

    public Vector2 TipDirection =>
        new(Mathf.Cos(currentTipAngle * Mathf.Deg2Rad),
            Mathf.Sin(currentTipAngle * Mathf.Deg2Rad));

    // ==================================================================

    private void Awake()
    {
        ball            = GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        body            = GetComponent<Rigidbody2D>();
        circleCollider  = GetComponent<CircleCollider2D>();
        polygonCollider = GetComponent<PolygonCollider2D>();

                if (body != null)
        {
            originalFreezeRotation = body.freezeRotation;
                    originalBodyType = body.bodyType;
        }

        ReadGeometryFromCollider();
        smoothedTipAngle = currentTipAngle;

        CreatePortMarkers();

        if (polygonCollider != null)
            polygonCollider.enabled = false;

        initialized = true;
    }

    private void Start()
    {
        CacheLabelTransform();
        if (ball != null)
            Refresh(ball.GetMathContent().Trim());
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (!IsActive) return;

        if (inputPortMarker == null || outputPortMarker == null) return;

        bool inputMatched  = false;
        bool outputMatched = false;

        int mask = gameObject.layer == LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces")
            ? LayerMask.GetMask("HandPieces")
            : LayerMask.GetMask("BoardPieces");

        // Scan a small radius around the triangle's centroid.
        var hits = Physics2D.OverlapCircleAll(transform.position, 3f, mask);
        foreach (var h in hits)
        {
            if (h == null) continue;
            if (h.gameObject == gameObject) continue;

            // Ignore other directional operators (they never connect to us).
            var otherOp = h.GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();
            if (otherOp != null && otherOp.IsActive) continue;

            if (!TryGetOperandSide(h, out bool isInside)) continue;
            if (isInside) outputMatched = true;
            else          inputMatched  = true;

            if (inputMatched && outputMatched) break;
        }

               inputPortMarker.color  = inputMatched
            ? new Color(1f, 0.25f, 0.25f, 1f)   // bright red = matched
            : new Color(1f, 0.25f, 0.25f, 0.5f);// faded red  = idle
        outputPortMarker.color = outputMatched
            ? new Color(1f, 0.25f, 0.25f, 1f)
            : new Color(1f, 0.25f, 0.25f, 0.5f);
    }

    private void LateUpdate()
    {
        if (IsActive && labelTransform != null)
            labelTransform.rotation = Quaternion.identity;
    }

    private void CacheLabelTransform()
    {
        if (labelTransform != null) return;
        Transform label = transform.Find("Ball Number");
        if (label != null)
        {
            labelTransform        = label;
            originalLabelPosition = label.localPosition;
            originalLabelRotation = label.localRotation;
        }
    }

        private void ReadGeometryFromCollider()
    {
        tipLocal     = new Vector2(0f,  0.577f);
        baseMidLocal = new Vector2(0f, -0.289f);

        if (polygonCollider == null || polygonCollider.pathCount == 0) return;
        Vector2[] path = polygonCollider.GetPath(0);
        if (path == null || path.Length < 3) return;

        // The built-in Triangle sprite is drawn with its tip pointing +Y
        // in local space. The visual tip is the vertex with the maximum
        // local Y; the base is the midpoint of the other two vertices.
        int tipIndex = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i < path.Length; i++)
            if (path[i].y > path[tipIndex].y) tipIndex = i;

        tipLocal = path[tipIndex];

        Vector2 sumOthers = Vector2.zero;
        int others = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < path.Length; i++)
        {
            if (i == tipIndex) continue;
            sumOthers += path[i];
            others++;
        }
        if (others > 0) baseMidLocal = sumOthers / others;
    }

    // ==================================================================

    public static bool IsDirectionalOperatorText(string content)
        => content == "-" || content == "/" || content == "^";

    public static bool IsDirectionalOperator(BallNumber b)
        => b != null && IsDirectionalOperatorText(b.GetMathContent().Trim());

    public void Refresh(string content)
    {
        if (!initialized) return;

        CacheLabelTransform();
        bool shouldBeActive = IsDirectionalOperatorText(content);
        if (IsActive == shouldBeActive) return;

        IsActive = shouldBeActive;

        if (circleCollider != null)
            circleCollider.enabled = !IsActive;

        if (polygonCollider != null)
            polygonCollider.enabled = IsActive;

        if (body != null)
        {
            body.freezeRotation = !isBeingDragged && (IsActive || originalFreezeRotation);
            if (IsActive) body.angularVelocity = 0f;
        }

        if (labelTransform != null)
        {
            labelTransform.localPosition = originalLabelPosition;
            if (!IsActive) labelTransform.localRotation = originalLabelRotation;
        }

        MathEquationBuilder.MarkConnectionsChanged();
    }

    public void SetTipDirection(Vector2 worldDirection)
    {
        if (!IsActive) return;
        if (worldDirection.sqrMagnitude < 1e-6f) return;

        // The triangle's visual tip lies along local +Y. A rotation of θ
        // maps local +Y to (-sin θ, cos θ), so we solve for the target
        // angle that makes that point along worldDirection.
        float targetAngle = Mathf.Atan2(-worldDirection.x, worldDirection.y) * Mathf.Rad2Deg;

        // Ease the current angle toward the target, respecting max turn speed.
        float delta   = Mathf.DeltaAngle(smoothedTipAngle, targetAngle);
        float maxStep = tipTurnSpeedDegPerSec * Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
        if (Mathf.Abs(delta) <= maxStep)
            smoothedTipAngle = targetAngle;
        else
            smoothedTipAngle += Mathf.Sign(delta) * maxStep;

        smoothedTipAngle = Mathf.Repeat(smoothedTipAngle + 180f, 360f) - 180f;
        currentTipAngle  = smoothedTipAngle;

        if (body != null) body.MoveRotation(currentTipAngle);
        else              transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, currentTipAngle);
    }

    public void BeginDrag()
    {
        if (!IsActive || body == null || isBeingDragged) return;

        isBeingDragged  = true;
        body.freezeRotation = false;   // allow rotation during drag
        body.linearVelocity  = Vector2.zero;
        body.angularVelocity = 0f;
    }

    public void EndDrag()
    {
        if (!isBeingDragged || body == null) return;

        isBeingDragged = false;
        body.freezeRotation = IsActive || originalFreezeRotation;   // restore
        body.linearVelocity  = Vector2.zero;
        body.angularVelocity = 0f;
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkConnectionsChanged();
    }

   public void BeginRotateDrag(Vector2 grabWorldPos)
    {
        if (!IsActive) return;

        _grabLocal    = transform.InverseTransformPoint(grabWorldPos);
        _hasGrabLocal = true;

        if (_grabLocal.sqrMagnitude < 1e-6f)
        {
            // Grabbed dead center — no defined "leading" direction.
            _grabLocalAngleDeg = 0f;
            return;
        }

        _grabLocalAngleDeg = Mathf.Atan2(_grabLocal.y, _grabLocal.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg;
    }

    public void EndRotateDrag()
    {
        _hasGrabLocal = false;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Aligns the grab point with the mouse movement direction. The grabbed
    /// corner leads; the rest of the triangle trails behind. Eased at
    /// grabTurnSpeedDegPerSec so it doesn't snap on sudden direction changes.
    /// </summary>
    public void ApplyRotateDrag(Vector2 mouseWorldDelta)
    {
        if (!IsActive || !_hasGrabLocal) return;
        if (_grabLocal.sqrMagnitude < 1e-6f) return;      // grabbed center: no leading corner
        if (mouseWorldDelta.sqrMagnitude < 1e-8f) return; // no motion: hold current angle

        // Angle of mouse motion in world space.
        float moveAngleDeg = Mathf.Atan2(mouseWorldDelta.y, mouseWorldDelta.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg;

        // Target triangle rotation so the grab point points along the motion.
        float targetRotation = moveAngleDeg - _grabLocalAngleDeg;
        targetRotation       = Mathf.Repeat(targetRotation + 180f, 360f) - 180f;

        // Ease currentTipAngle toward targetRotation at max turn speed.
        float deltaAngle = Mathf.DeltaAngle(currentTipAngle, targetRotation);
        float maxStep    = grabTurnSpeedDegPerSec * Time.deltaTime;
        if (Mathf.Abs(deltaAngle) <= maxStep)
            currentTipAngle = targetRotation;
        else
            currentTipAngle += Mathf.Sign(deltaAngle) * maxStep;

        currentTipAngle  = Mathf.Repeat(currentTipAngle + 180f, 360f) - 180f;
        smoothedTipAngle = currentTipAngle;

        // Teleport rotation directly — same mechanism the ball uses for position.
        Quaternion q = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, currentTipAngle);
        transform.rotation = q;
        if (body != null) body.rotation = currentTipAngle;
    }

    public float CurrentTargetAngle => currentTipAngle;

    /// <summary>
    /// Drives the dynamic body toward a target position and rotation by setting
    /// velocity toward each. The physics engine handles collisions, giving the
    /// operator the same push behavior as a ball.
    /// </summary>
    public void DriveToward(Vector2 targetPosition, float targetAngleDeg)
    {
        if (!IsActive || body == null) return;

        // Position: velocity toward target, capped.
        Vector2 toTarget = targetPosition - (Vector2)transform.position;
        Vector2 desiredVel = toTarget / Mathf.Max(Time.fixedDeltaTime, 1e-4f);

        const float maxDragSpeed = 10f;
        if (desiredVel.sqrMagnitude > maxDragSpeed * maxDragSpeed)
            desiredVel = desiredVel.normalized * maxDragSpeed;

        body.linearVelocity = desiredVel;

        // Rotation: angular velocity toward target angle, capped.
        float angleErr = Mathf.DeltaAngle(transform.eulerAngles.z, targetAngleDeg);
        float desiredAngVel = angleErr / Mathf.Max(Time.fixedDeltaTime, 1e-4f);

        const float maxAngVel = 720f;
        desiredAngVel = Mathf.Clamp(desiredAngVel, -maxAngVel, maxAngVel);

        body.angularVelocity = desiredAngVel;
    }

    public void StopDriving()
    {
        if (body == null) return;
        body.linearVelocity  = Vector2.zero;
        body.angularVelocity = 0f;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Returns where the center must be so that the grab point sits exactly
    /// under mouseWorldPos, given the triangle's current rotation.
    /// </summary>
    public Vector2 CenterForMouseToKeepGrabPoint(Vector2 mouseWorldPos)
    {
        return CenterForMouseToKeepGrabPoint(mouseWorldPos, transform.eulerAngles.z);
    }

    public Vector2 CenterForMouseToKeepGrabPoint(Vector2 mouseWorldPos, float targetAngleDeg)
    {
        if (!_hasGrabLocal) return (Vector2)transform.position;
        Quaternion q = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, targetAngleDeg);
        Vector2 grabWorldOffset = q * _grabLocal;
        return mouseWorldPos - grabWorldOffset;
    }

    public bool TryGetOperandSide(Collider2D operand, out bool isInside)
    {
        isInside = false;
        if (operand == null) return false;

        // Hard gap check: same distance balls use. If the collider edges are
        // farther apart than a ball-to-ball connection would allow, reject.
        if (polygonCollider != null)
        {
            var d = polygonCollider.Distance(operand);
            if (!d.isOverlapped && d.distance > BallConnectionRules.ConnectionGap)
                return false;
        }

        float radius = Mathf.Max(operand.bounds.extents.x, operand.bounds.extents.y);
        return TryGetOperandSide(operand.bounds.center, radius, out isInside);
    }

        public bool TryGetOperandSide(Vector2 worldPosition, float operandRadius, out bool isInside)
    {
        isInside = false;
        if (!IsActive) return false;

        Vector2 local = transform.InverseTransformPoint(worldPosition);

        // Overall reach cap in world units, matching ball-to-ball connection.
        float avgScale = (Mathf.Abs(transform.lossyScale.x) +
                          Mathf.Abs(transform.lossyScale.y)) * 0.5f;
        if (avgScale < 0.0001f) avgScale = 1f;

        float worldDistFromOrigin = Vector2.Distance(worldPosition, transform.position);
        float triangleRadiusWorld = 0.577f * avgScale;
        float maxReach            = BallConnectionRules.ConnectionGap;

        if (worldDistFromOrigin > triangleRadiusWorld + operandRadius + maxReach)
            return false;

        Vector2 axisLocal = (tipLocal - baseMidLocal).normalized;
        Vector2 perpAxis  = new(-axisLocal.y, axisLocal.x);

        // --- Output side (past the tip) ---
        Vector2 relTip     = local - tipLocal;
        float   forwardTip = Vector2.Dot(relTip, axisLocal) * avgScale;
        float   lateralTip = Mathf.Abs(Vector2.Dot(relTip, perpAxis)) * avgScale;

        if (forwardTip >= -operandRadius)
        {
            float angle = Mathf.Atan2(lateralTip, Mathf.Max(forwardTip, 0f)) * Mathf.Rad2Deg;
            float lateralCap = operandRadius + 0.10f;
            if (angle <= outputConeHalfAngle && lateralTip <= lateralCap)
            {
                isInside = true;
                return true;
            }
        }

        // --- Input side (past the base) ---
        Vector2 relBase     = local - baseMidLocal;
        float   forwardBase = Vector2.Dot(relBase, -axisLocal) * avgScale;
        float   lateralBase = Mathf.Abs(Vector2.Dot(relBase, perpAxis)) * avgScale;

        if (forwardBase >= -operandRadius)
        {
            float angle = Mathf.Atan2(lateralBase, Mathf.Max(forwardBase, 0f)) * Mathf.Rad2Deg;
            float lateralCap = operandRadius + 0.10f;
            if (angle <= inputConeHalfAngle && lateralBase <= lateralCap)
            {
                isInside = false;
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    public bool ConnectsTo(Collider2D other)
    {
        if (!IsActive || other == null) return false;
        if (other.GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>() is DirectionalOperator dop && dop.IsActive)
            return false;
        return TryGetOperandSide(other, out _);
    }

        private void CreatePortMarkers()
    {
        inputPortMarker  = CreateMarker("InputPortMarker",  baseMidLocal);
        outputPortMarker = CreateMarker("OutputPortMarker", tipLocal);
    }

        private SpriteRenderer CreateMarker(string childName, Vector2 localPos)
    {
        Transform existing = transform.Find(childName);
        if (existing != null)
        {
            var srExisting = existing.GetComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
            if (srExisting != null)
            {
                existing.localPosition = localPos;
                existing.localScale    = Vector3.one * 0.28f;
                return srExisting;
            }
        }

        var go = new GameObject(childName);
        go.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
        go.transform.localPosition = localPos;
        go.transform.localScale    = Vector3.one * 0.28f;

                var sr = go.AddComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
        sr.sprite = GetDotSprite();
        sr.color  = new Color(1f, 0.25f, 0.25f, 0.5f);
        sr.sortingOrder = 2;   // just hardcode

        // Same unlit material the triangle uses. Without this, dots render
        // through Sprites-Lit-Default and come out black in scenes with no
        // 2D lights.
        var parentSr = GetComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
        if (parentSr != null && parentSr.sharedMaterial != null)
            sr.sharedMaterial = parentSr.sharedMaterial;

        return sr;
    }

    private static Sprite GetDotSprite()
    {
        if (_dotSprite != null) return _dotSprite;

        const int size = 64;
        var tex = new Texture2D(size, size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false);
        var px  = new Color32[size * size];
        float c = size * 0.5f;
        float r = size * 0.5f - 1f;
        for (int y = 0; y < size; y++)
        for (int x = 0; x < size; x++)
        {
            float dx = x - c, dy = y - c;
            float d  = Mathf.Sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
            byte a   = (byte)(Mathf.Clamp01((r - d) / 2f) * 255f);
            px[y * size + x] = new Color32(255, 255, 255, a);
        }
        tex.SetPixels32(px);
        tex.Apply();

        _dotSprite = Sprite.Create(tex, new Rect(0, 0, size, size),
            new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), size);
        _dotSprite.name = "PortDot";
        return _dotSprite;
    }

 
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\DualCameraController.cs =====
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

/// <summary>
/// Mouse-over-viewport-aware pan and zoom. The camera under the pointer gets
/// the wheel zoom; dragging empty space pans that camera only.
/// </summary>
public class DualCameraController : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private float zoomSpeed = 0.8f;
    [SerializeField] private float zoomSmoothTime = 0.10f;
    [SerializeField] private float minOrthoSize = 1.5f;
    [SerializeField] private float maxOrthoSize = 30f;

    private Camera boardCam;
    private Camera handCam;

    private float boardTargetZoom;
    private float boardZoomVel;
    private float handTargetZoom;
    private float handZoomVel;

    private bool isPanning;
    private Camera panCam;
    private Vector3 lastMousePos;

    private void Start()
    {
        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        if (setup == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError("DualCameraController requires DualCameraSetup in the scene.");
            enabled = false;
            return;
        }
        boardCam = setup.BoardCamera;
        handCam = setup.HandCamera;
        boardTargetZoom = boardCam.orthographicSize;
        handTargetZoom = handCam.orthographicSize;
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (boardCam == null || handCam == null) return;

        bool overUI = EventSystem.current != null && EventSystem.current.IsPointerOverGameObject();
        if (overUI) return;

        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        float dividerY = Screen.height * setup.HandFraction;
        bool mouseInHand = Input.mousePosition.y < dividerY;

        // ---- Zoom ----
        // ---- Zoom ----
        if (!TestMenuUI.BlockWheelForMenu)
        {
            float scroll = Input.mouseScrollDelta.y;
            if (Mathf.Abs(scroll) > 0.001f)
            {
                if (mouseInHand)
                    handTargetZoom = Mathf.Clamp(handTargetZoom - scroll * zoomSpeed, minOrthoSize, maxOrthoSize);
                else
                    boardTargetZoom = Mathf.Clamp(boardTargetZoom - scroll * zoomSpeed, minOrthoSize, maxOrthoSize);
            }
        }

        handCam.orthographicSize = Mathf.SmoothDamp(
            handCam.orthographicSize, handTargetZoom, ref handZoomVel, zoomSmoothTime);
        boardCam.orthographicSize = Mathf.SmoothDamp(
            boardCam.orthographicSize, boardTargetZoom, ref boardZoomVel, zoomSmoothTime);

        // ---- Pan ----
        bool shiftHeld = Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.RightShift);
        bool lassoActive = LassoSelection.IsLassoActive;

        if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0) && !shiftHeld && !lassoActive &&
            !BallDrag.isDragging && !IsMouseOverBall(mouseInHand))
        {
            isPanning = true;
            panCam = mouseInHand ? handCam : boardCam;
            lastMousePos = Input.mousePosition;
        }

        if (Input.GetMouseButton(0) && isPanning && panCam != null &&
            !shiftHeld && !lassoActive && !BallDrag.isDragging)
        {
            Vector3 current = Input.mousePosition;
            Vector3 delta = current - lastMousePos;
            float worldPerPixel = (panCam.orthographicSize * 2f) / (Screen.height * panCam.rect.height);
            panCam.transform.position -= delta * worldPerPixel;
            lastMousePos = current;
        }

        if (Input.GetMouseButtonUp(0))
        {
            isPanning = false;
            panCam = null;
        }
    }

    private bool IsMouseOverBall(bool mouseInHand)
    {
        Camera cam = mouseInHand ? handCam : boardCam;
        if (cam == null) return false;

        Vector3 world = cam.ScreenToWorldPoint(Input.mousePosition);
        world.z = 0f;

        int mask = mouseInHand
            ? LayerMask.GetMask("HandPieces")
            : LayerMask.GetMask("BoardPieces");

        return Physics2D.OverlapPoint(world, mask) != null;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\DualCameraSetup.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Creates two orthographic cameras: BoardCamera (top viewport) and
/// HandCamera (bottom viewport). Each renders only its own layer.
/// Exposes a HandFraction used by the divider and controller.
/// </summary>
public class DualCameraSetup : MonoBehaviour
{
    
    
    public static DualCameraSetup Instance { get; private set; }

    [Range(0.15f, 0.6f)]
    [SerializeField] private float handFraction = 0.32f;

    [SerializeField] private Color boardBackground = new Color(0.10f, 0.12f, 0.16f, 1f);
    [SerializeField] private Color handBackground  = new Color(0.22f, 0.20f, 0.18f, 1f);

    [SerializeField] private float initialOrthoSize = 6f;

    public Camera BoardCamera { get; private set; }
    public Camera HandCamera  { get; private set; }

    public float HandFraction
    {
        get => handFraction;
        set
        {
            handFraction = Mathf.Clamp(value, 0.15f, 0.6f);
            ApplyViewports();
        }
    }

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
        Instance = this;

        BoardCamera = CreateCamera("BoardCamera", new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0f));
        BoardCamera.cullingMask = LayerMask.GetMask("BoardPieces");

        HandCamera = CreateCamera("HandCamera", new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0f));
        HandCamera.cullingMask = LayerMask.GetMask("HandPieces");

        BoardCamera.depth = 0;
        HandCamera.depth = 1;
        
        // Both cameras see the world at y≈0 initially (we'll separate via
        // world Y offsets for pieces once Pass 2 is in).
        BoardCamera.transform.position = new Vector3(0f, 0f, -10f);
        HandCamera.transform.position  = new Vector3(0f, 0f, -10f);   
        

        BoardCamera.orthographicSize = initialOrthoSize;
        HandCamera.orthographicSize  = initialOrthoSize * 0.8f;

        // Hide the original Main Camera's rendering of these layers so it
        // doesn't double-draw them. If you already deleted Main Camera, skip.
        var main = Camera.main;
        if (main != null)
        {
            main.cullingMask &= ~LayerMask.GetMask("BoardPieces", "HandPieces");
        }

        ApplyViewports();
    }

    private void ApplyViewports()
    {
        if (BoardCamera == null || HandCamera == null) return;

        BoardCamera.rect = new Rect(0f, handFraction, 1f, 1f - handFraction);
        HandCamera.rect  = new Rect(0f, 0f, 1f, handFraction);

        BoardCamera.backgroundColor = boardBackground;
        HandCamera.backgroundColor  = handBackground;
    }

    private Camera CreateCamera(string name, Color bg)
    {
        var go = new GameObject(name);
        go.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
        var cam = go.AddComponent<Camera>();
        cam.orthographic = true;
        cam.clearFlags = CameraClearFlags.SolidColor;
        cam.backgroundColor = bg;
        cam.nearClipPlane = 0.1f;
        cam.farClipPlane = 100f;
        cam.depth = 0;         // board renders first
        cam.allowHDR = false;
        cam.allowMSAA = false;
        return cam;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\EquationListDisplay.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

public class EquationListDisplay : MonoBehaviour
{
    private readonly List<Text> equationLabels = new List<Text>();
    private Transform equationListContent;
    private GameObject panelRoot;

    private void Awake()
    {
        CreateEquationListUi();
    }

    public void Refresh(List<MathEquationBuilder.ConnectedEquation> equations)
    {
         var nm = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton;
        if (nm != null && nm.IsListening) return;
        
        List<MathEquationBuilder.ConnectedEquation> sortedEquations =
            new List<MathEquationBuilder.ConnectedEquation>(equations);

        // Keep the original order as the final tie-breaker so the list does not
        // shuffle when two equations have the same topmost position.
        Dictionary<MathEquationBuilder.ConnectedEquation, int> originalOrder =
            new Dictionary<MathEquationBuilder.ConnectedEquation, int>();
        for (int index = 0; index < equations.Count; index++)
        {
            originalOrder[equations[index]] = index;
        }

        sortedEquations.Sort((left, right) =>
        {
            Vector3 leftTop = GetTopmostBallPosition(left);
            Vector3 rightTop = GetTopmostBallPosition(right);

            const float positionTolerance = 0.01f;
            float heightDifference = rightTop.y - leftTop.y;
            if (Mathf.Abs(heightDifference) > positionTolerance)
            {
                // Higher topmost ball first.
                return heightDifference > 0f ? 1 : -1;
            }

            float horizontalDifference = leftTop.x - rightTop.x;
            if (Mathf.Abs(horizontalDifference) > positionTolerance)
            {
                // If heights are equal, the construction farther left comes first.
                return horizontalDifference < 0f ? -1 : 1;
            }

            return originalOrder[left].CompareTo(originalOrder[right]);
        });

        bool unchanged = equationLabels.Count >= sortedEquations.Count;
        for (int index = 0; unchanged && index < equationLabels.Count; index++)
        {
            Text label = equationLabels[index];
            bool shouldBeActive = index < sortedEquations.Count;
            if (label.transform.parent.gameObject.activeSelf != shouldBeActive ||
                shouldBeActive && label.text != GetEquationListText(sortedEquations[index]))
            {
                unchanged = false;
            }
        }

        if (unchanged)
        {
            return;
        }

        while (equationLabels.Count < sortedEquations.Count)
        {
            equationLabels.Add(CreateEquationListRow());
        }

        for (int index = 0; index < equationLabels.Count; index++)
        {
            Text label = equationLabels[index];
