            bool hasEquation = index < sortedEquations.Count;
            label.transform.parent.gameObject.SetActive(hasEquation);
            if (hasEquation)
            {
                label.text = GetEquationListText(sortedEquations[index]);
            }
        }
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (panelRoot == null) return;
        var nm = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton;
        bool online = nm != null && nm.IsListening;
        if (panelRoot.activeSelf != !online)
            panelRoot.SetActive(!online);
    }

    private static Vector3 GetTopmostBallPosition(
        MathEquationBuilder.ConnectedEquation equation)
    {
        if (equation == null || equation.balls == null || equation.balls.Count == 0)
        {
            return Vector3.zero;
        }

        // Use the highest ball in the construction. If multiple balls share that
        // height, use the leftmost one as the construction's sorting position.
        Vector3 topmostPosition = equation.balls[0] != null
            ? equation.balls[0].transform.position
            : Vector3.zero;

        foreach (BallNumber ball in equation.balls)
        {
            if (ball == null)
            {
                continue;
            }

            Vector3 position = ball.transform.position;
            const float positionTolerance = 0.01f;
            if (position.y > topmostPosition.y + positionTolerance ||
                Mathf.Abs(position.y - topmostPosition.y) <= positionTolerance &&
                position.x < topmostPosition.x)
            {
                topmostPosition = position;
            }
        }

        return topmostPosition;
    }

    private void CreateEquationListUi()
    {
        GameObject panelObject = CreateUiObject("Equation List Panel", transform);
        panelRoot = panelObject;
        RectTransform panelRect = panelObject.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
        panelRect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 0.08f);
        panelRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0f, 0.92f);
        panelRect.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 0.5f);
        panelRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(16f, 0f);
        panelRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(350f, 0f);

        Image panelImage = panelObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        panelImage.color = new Color(0.07f, 0.12f, 0.16f, 0.9f);

        GameObject titleObject = CreateUiObject("Equation List Title", panelObject.transform);
        RectTransform titleRect = titleObject.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
        titleRect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
        titleRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 1f);
        titleRect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 1f);
        titleRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, -12f);
        titleRect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(-24f, 32f);
        Text title = titleObject.AddComponent<Text>();
        ConfigureUiText(title, 22, FontStyle.Bold, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft);
        title.text = "Equations";

        GameObject viewportObject = CreateUiObject("Equation List Viewport", panelObject.transform);
        RectTransform viewportRect = viewportObject.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
        viewportRect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
        viewportRect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        viewportRect.offsetMin = new Vector2(10f, 10f);
        viewportRect.offsetMax = new Vector2(-10f, -54f);
        Image viewportImage = viewportObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        viewportImage.color = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.02f);
        Mask viewportMask = viewportObject.AddComponent<Mask>();
        viewportMask.showMaskGraphic = false;

        GameObject contentObject = CreateUiObject("Equation List Content", viewportObject.transform);
        RectTransform contentRect = contentObject.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
        contentRect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
        contentRect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        contentRect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 1f);
        contentRect.anchoredPosition = Vector2.zero;
        contentRect.sizeDelta = Vector2.zero;
        VerticalLayoutGroup layout = contentObject.AddComponent<VerticalLayoutGroup>();
        layout.spacing = 8f;
        layout.padding = new RectOffset(2, 2, 2, 2);
        layout.childAlignment = TextAnchor.UpperCenter;
        layout.childControlWidth = true;
        layout.childControlHeight = true;
        layout.childForceExpandWidth = true;
        layout.childForceExpandHeight = false;
        ContentSizeFitter contentSize = contentObject.AddComponent<ContentSizeFitter>();
        contentSize.horizontalFit = ContentSizeFitter.FitMode.Unconstrained;
        contentSize.verticalFit = ContentSizeFitter.FitMode.PreferredSize;

        ScrollRect scrollRect = viewportObject.AddComponent<ScrollRect>();
        scrollRect.viewport = viewportRect;
        scrollRect.content = contentRect;
        scrollRect.horizontal = false;
        scrollRect.vertical = true;
        scrollRect.scrollSensitivity = 30f;
        equationListContent = contentObject.transform;
    }

    private static GameObject CreateUiObject(string objectName, Transform parent)
    {
        GameObject uiObject = new GameObject(objectName, typeof(RectTransform));
        uiObject.layer = parent.gameObject.layer;
        uiObject.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
        return uiObject;
    }

    private static void ConfigureUiText(
        Text text,
        int fontSize,
        FontStyle fontStyle,
        TextAnchor alignment)
    {
        text.font = Resources.GetBuiltinResource<Font>("LegacyRuntime.ttf");
        text.fontSize = fontSize;
        text.fontStyle = fontStyle;
        text.color = Color.white;
        text.alignment = alignment;
        text.horizontalOverflow = HorizontalWrapMode.Wrap;
        text.verticalOverflow = VerticalWrapMode.Truncate;
        text.raycastTarget = false;
    }

    private Text CreateEquationListRow()
    {
        GameObject rowObject = CreateUiObject("Equation List Row", equationListContent);
        Image rowImage = rowObject.AddComponent<Image>();
        rowImage.color = new Color(0.13f, 0.34f, 0.40f, 1f);
        LayoutElement rowLayout = rowObject.AddComponent<LayoutElement>();
        rowLayout.minHeight = 48f;
        rowLayout.preferredHeight = 58f;

        GameObject labelObject = CreateUiObject("Equation Text", rowObject.transform);
        RectTransform labelRect = labelObject.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
        labelRect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
        labelRect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
        labelRect.offsetMin = new Vector2(10f, 4f);
        labelRect.offsetMax = new Vector2(-10f, -4f);
        Text label = labelObject.AddComponent<Text>();
        ConfigureUiText(label, 18, FontStyle.Normal, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft);
        label.resizeTextForBestFit = true;
        label.resizeTextMinSize = 12;
        label.resizeTextMaxSize = 18;
        return label;
    }

    private static string GetEquationListText(
        MathEquationBuilder.ConnectedEquation equation)
    {
        string expression = equation.expression;
        string text = expression.Replace(
            MathEquationSolver.ImplicitMultiplicationSymbol.ToString(),
            "*");
        foreach (BallNumber ball in equation.balls)
        {
            if (ball.GetMathContent().Trim().Length != 0)
            {
                continue;
            }

            string displayedValue = ball.IsSolverOutput
                ? ball.GetSolverOutputDisplayContent()
                : "?";
            text = text.Replace(
                MathEquationSolver.UnknownSymbol.ToString(),
                displayedValue);
            int equalsIndex = text.IndexOf('=');
            int unknownIndex = expression.IndexOf(MathEquationSolver.UnknownSymbol);
            if (equalsIndex > 0 && equalsIndex == text.LastIndexOf('=') &&
                unknownIndex >= 0 &&
                unknownIndex < expression.IndexOf('='))
            {
                string leftSide = text.Substring(0, equalsIndex);
                string rightSide = text.Substring(equalsIndex + 1);
                return rightSide + "=" + leftSide;
            }

            return text;
        }

        return text;
    }
}


// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\GameOverview.cs =====
// ===== FILE: Assets/scripts/GameOverview.cs =====
//
// =====================================================================
//  MatheMonster — Game Overview
// =====================================================================
//
//  A 2D math-puzzle game where players drag physical "balls" (each
//  showing a number, variable, operator, function, bracket, or equals
//  sign) onto a board and connect them by touching. When a chain of
//  connected balls forms a valid equation, the game evaluates it:
//
//    - A '=' ball splits the cluster into left/right regions.
//    - Each region is parsed into an expression tree using rewrite
//      rules (brackets → adjacency → functions → powers → products
//      → sums).
//    - If both sides evaluate equal, the '=' ball turns GREEN.
//      If they disagree, RED. If there's exactly one blank ball in
//      the equation, the solver fills it with the answer.
//    - Balls with no valid role (e.g. two operators touching,
//      a function with two arguments) turn RED.
//
//  The screen is split into two viewports by a draggable gray divider:
//
//    - TOP viewport  = the shared BOARD (networked in multiplayer).
//    - BOTTOM viewport = the local player's HAND (never networked;
//                        only this client sees their own hand).
//
//  Dragging a ball across the divider transfers it between the board
//  and your hand. In multiplayer the transfer round-trips through the
//  server so all clients stay in sync.
//
//  A "bag" system lets players draw random content from a shared pool
//  (server-authoritative when online). Bag balls are visually ringed
//  in blue, cannot be edited or deleted, and return to the pool when
//  dropped on the Bag button.
//
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  SYSTEMS AT A GLANCE
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//
//  ▸ BALLS & CONTENT
//      BallNumber          — stores a ball's value (algebra ints/vars
//                            OR raw display text) and drives display.
//      BallAppearance      — picks a color from the content category
//                            and renders the text label. Also tints
//                            hand pieces and '=' state (blue/green/red).
//      BallInputController — keyboard editing for the ball under the
//                            mouse (numbers, letters, backspace, etc).
//      DirectionalOperator — for '-', '/', '^' balls: renders a crescent
//                            and lets the player choose which side is
//                            the "inside" operand by dragging.
//
//  ▸ MATH ENGINE
//      MathSyntax          — character-level helpers, token validation.
//      MathExpression      — classifies text (number / algebra / mixed /
//                            operator / invalid / other) for coloring.
//      MathSimplifier      — combines like terms in add-only expressions.
//      MathEquationSolver  — parser + evaluator (handles functions,
//                            implicit multiplication, equality chains,
//                            variable substitution, complex numbers).
//      BallSyntaxChecker   — per-ball local error pass (e.g. a '+'
//                            with no neighbors is red before rewrite).
//      MathEquationBuilder — the heart of the puzzle logic:
//                            partitions balls into clusters, runs the
//                            rewrite engine, builds ConnectedEquation
//                            objects, drives solving.
//
//  ▸ CONNECTION & DISPLAY
//      BallConnectionRules — AreConnected(): decides when two balls are
//                            "touching" (bounds overlap or small gap).
//      BagRing / LockRing  — LineRenderer circles for bag / locked balls.
//      Spawner             — owns every ball in the scene; per-frame
//                            partitions board & hand clusters, solves
//                            dirty ones, draws tendril lines between
//                            connected balls.
//      EquationListDisplay — left-side UI panel listing solved equations.
//
//  ▸ DRAG & INPUT
//      BallDrag            — mouse grab, drag, throw, hand↔board
//                            crossing, bag-return-on-drop.
//      BallTransferManager — owns the in-flight state for a ball crossing
//                            the divider; shows a fake ghost and spawns
//                            the real ball on release.
//      LassoSelection      — Shift+drag free-form multi-select and group
//                            move.
//      SelectionInputRouter— keyboard edits applied to a whole selection.
//      DualCameraSetup     — creates BoardCamera and HandCamera and
//                            splits the screen.
//      DualCameraController— per-viewport mouse-wheel zoom and pan.
//      BoardHandDivider    — the draggable gray bar between viewports.
//      TestMenuUI          — right-click+Shift dev menu for spawning
//                            hardcoded test setups (TestSpawner).
//      TestSpawner         — hardcoded puzzle arrangements and their
//                            expected results, used to validate rules.
//
//  ▸ BAG
//      BagDefinition        — ScriptableObject: content + count entries.
//      BagLibrary           — ScriptableObject: named list of defs.
//      BagDefinitionFiller  — Editor menu to fill a def with presets
//                             (Algebra, Numbers, Operators, Scrabble, …).
//      BagManager           — server-authoritative NetworkList pool;
//                             draw / return / add / remove / reset.
//                             Also handles the "who is editing" lock.
//      BagButton            — screen rect for drop detection; greys out
//                             when the pool is empty or locked.
//      BagBallMarker        — tags a local (hand) ball as a bag ball.
//      BagMenuUI            — in-game panel for inspecting/editing the
//                             bag contents (host/client with lock).
//      BagCounterDisplay    — IMGUI panel: on-board / per-hand / in-bag
//                             counts (online only).
//      HandBagCountReporter — each client reports its hand bag count
//                             to the server for BagCounterDisplay.
//
//  ▸ NETWORKING
//      NetworkBall          — NetworkBehaviour wrapper: position sync,
//                              grab lock (LockedBy), zone (Hand/Board),
//                              bag flag, despawn/content RPCs.
//      NetworkBallManager   — spawn helpers, board↔hand transfer,
//                              bag-return from board, host migration.
//      NetworkGameSession   — UGS Relay wrapper: host/join/leave.
//      NetworkLobbyUI       — host/join button panel.
//      PlayerManager        — spawns a PlayerData per client; enforces
//                              unique player names.
//      PlayerData           — NetworkBehaviour with PlayerName and
//                              HandBagCount.
//      PlayerNamePrompt     — modal name-entry dialog.
//      PlayerNameSubmitter  — helper to send the name to the server.
//      LocalHandBall        — marks a non-networked hand-owned ball.
//
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  GAMEPLAY WALKTHROUGH
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//
//  1. Right-click empty space in either viewport to spawn a blank ball
//     (or Shift+Right-click to open the dev test menu).
//  2. Hover a ball and type to edit: digits become the coefficient,
//     letters add a variable and raise its power, Backspace clears.
//     In display-key mode any character is accepted.
//  3. Drag balls so they touch. The Spawner solves dirty clusters each
//     frame and draws a white tendril between every connected pair.
//  4. Drag a ball across the gray divider to move it between your hand
//     and the shared board.
//  5. Press the Bag button to draw a random ball from the pool. Drop a
//     bag ball on the Bag button to return it.
//  6. When a construction is valid and balanced, its '=' ball turns
//     green and any blank ball is filled with the answer.
//
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  KEY CLASSES / FLOW (offline)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//
//   Spawner.Update
//     ├─ MarkConnectionsChanged while dragging
//     ├─ PartitionClusters() per world
//     ├─ SolveDirtyClusters() per world
//     │    ├─ MathEquationBuilder.SolveCluster
//     │    │    ├─ BallSyntaxChecker.MarkLocalSyntaxErrors
//     │    │    ├─ BuildGraph → PartitionByEquals → SolveRegion
//     │    │    └─ EvaluateEquals → byte state (0 blue /1 green /2 red)
//     │    └─ TrySolveEquation → fill one blank, ReEvaluateAfterFill
//     └─ RefreshTendrils(connectedPairs, …)
//
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//  UNITY SETUP NOTES
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
//
//  Layers required:  "HandPieces", "BoardPieces"
//  Scene object:     one DualCameraSetup + DualCameraController + Spawner
//                    + NetworkBallManager + TestSpawner + TestMenuUI
//                    + NetworkGameSession (DontDestroyOnLoad)
//  Prefabs:          ballPrefab, directionalOperatorPrefab, playerDataPrefab
//  Assets:           BagLibrary containing one or more BagDefinition assets
//
// =====================================================================

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\HandBagCounterReporter.cs =====
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Each client counts its own hand bag balls each frame and reports changes
/// to the server. Server stores the count in PlayerData.HandBagCount, which
/// all clients read for the counter display.
/// </summary>
public class HandBagCountReporter : MonoBehaviour
{
    private int _lastReported = -1;
    private float _nextReportTime;
    private const float ReportInterval = 0.2f;

    private void Update()
    {
        var nm = NetworkManager.Singleton;
        if (nm == null || !nm.IsClient) return;

        if (Time.unscaledTime < _nextReportTime) return;
        _nextReportTime = Time.unscaledTime + ReportInterval;

        var spawner = FindFirstObjectByType<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null) return;

        int count = spawner.GetHandBagBallCount();
        if (count == _lastReported) return;

        if (PlayerManager.Instance != null)
            PlayerManager.Instance.ReportHandBagCountServerRpc(count);

        _lastReported = count;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\LassoSelection.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

// Separate component for freeform ball selection and moving selected groups.
// Add this component to the same camera GameObject as CameraController.
public class LassoSelection : MonoBehaviour
{
    public Color selectionColor = new Color(0.2f, 1f, 0.8f, 1f);
    public float selectionOutlineWidth = 0.055f;

    private bool isLassoing;
    private readonly List<Vector2> lassoPoints = new List<Vector2>();
    private readonly HashSet<BallDrag> selectedBalls = new HashSet<BallDrag>();
    private readonly Dictionary<BallDrag, LineRenderer> selectionOutlines = new Dictionary<BallDrag, LineRenderer>();
    private Texture2D lassoPixel;

    public static LassoSelection Instance { get; private set; }
    public static bool IsLassoActive => Instance != null && Instance.isLassoing;

    private void Awake()
    {
        Instance = this;
        lassoPixel = new Texture2D(1, 1);
        lassoPixel.SetPixel(0, 0, Color.white);
        lassoPixel.Apply();
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape))
        {
            ClearSelection();
            isLassoing = false;
            lassoPoints.Clear();
            BallDrag.CancelActiveDragForLasso();
            return;
        }

        bool pointerOverUi = EventSystem.current != null && EventSystem.current.IsPointerOverGameObject();
        bool shiftHeld = Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.RightShift);

        if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0) && shiftHeld && !pointerOverUi)
        {
            isLassoing = true;
            lassoPoints.Clear();
            lassoPoints.Add(Input.mousePosition);
            BallDrag.CancelActiveDragForLasso();
        }

        if (isLassoing && Input.GetMouseButton(0))
        {
            Vector2 point = Input.mousePosition;
            if (lassoPoints.Count == 0 || Vector2.Distance(lassoPoints[lassoPoints.Count - 1], point) >= 3f)
                lassoPoints.Add(point);
        }

        if (isLassoing && Input.GetMouseButtonUp(0))
        {
            FinishLasso();
            isLassoing = false;
        }
    }

    public static bool IsSelected(BallDrag ball) =>
        Instance != null && ball != null && Instance.selectedBalls.Contains(ball);

    public static bool TryMoveSelection(BallDrag grabbedBall, Vector2 worldDelta)
    {
        if (Instance == null || grabbedBall == null || !Instance.selectedBalls.Contains(grabbedBall))
            return false;

        foreach (BallDrag ball in Instance.selectedBalls)
        {
            if (ball != null && ball != grabbedBall) ball.MoveByGroupDelta(worldDelta);
        }
        return true;
    }

    public static void DeselectAll()
    {
        if (Instance != null)
        {
            Instance.ClearSelection();
            Instance.isLassoing = false;
            Instance.lassoPoints.Clear();
        }
    }

    private void FinishLasso()
    {
        ClearSelection();
        if (lassoPoints.Count < 3) return;

        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        if (setup == null) return;

        // Lasso operates in whichever viewport the mouse is in at completion.
        bool mouseInHand = Input.mousePosition.y < Screen.height * setup.HandFraction;
        Camera camera = mouseInHand ? setup.HandCamera : setup.BoardCamera;

        int mask = mouseInHand
            ? LayerMask.GetMask("HandPieces")
            : LayerMask.GetMask("BoardPieces");

        // Only get balls in this world.
        var relevantBalls = new List<BallDrag>();
        foreach (var drag in FindObjectsByType<BallDrag>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
        {
            if (drag == null) continue;
            if (((1 << drag.gameObject.layer) & mask) == 0) continue;
            relevantBalls.Add(drag);
        }

        foreach (var ball in relevantBalls)
        {
            Vector3 screen = camera.WorldToScreenPoint(ball.transform.position);
            if (PointInsidePolygon(new Vector2(screen.x, screen.y), lassoPoints))
            {
                selectedBalls.Add(ball);
                CreateOutline(ball);
            }
        }
    }

    private static bool PointInsidePolygon(Vector2 point, List<Vector2> polygon)
    {
        bool inside = false;
        for (int i = 0, j = polygon.Count - 1; i < polygon.Count; j = i++)
        {
            Vector2 a = polygon[i];
            Vector2 b = polygon[j];
            bool crosses = ((a.y > point.y) != (b.y > point.y)) &&
                (point.x < (b.x - a.x) * (point.y - a.y) /
                ((b.y - a.y) == 0f ? 0.0001f : b.y - a.y) + a.x);
            if (crosses) inside = !inside;
        }
        return inside;
    }

    private void CreateOutline(BallDrag ball)
    {
        GameObject outlineObject = new GameObject("Selection Outline");
        outlineObject.transform.SetParent(ball.transform, false);
        outlineObject.transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;

        LineRenderer line = outlineObject.AddComponent<LineRenderer>();
        line.useWorldSpace = false;
        line.loop          = true;
        line.positionCount = 48;
        line.startWidth    = selectionOutlineWidth;
        line.endWidth      = selectionOutlineWidth;
        line.material      = new Material(Shader.Find("Sprites/Default"));
        line.startColor    = selectionColor;
        line.endColor      = selectionColor;
        line.sortingOrder  = 20;

        // Crescent-aware radius pick.
        float radius = 0.5f;
        var dop = ball.GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();
        if (dop != null && dop.IsActive)
        {
            var poly = ball.GetComponent<PolygonCollider2D>();
            if (poly != null && poly.enabled)
                radius = Mathf.Max(poly.bounds.extents.x, poly.bounds.extents.y);
        }
        else
        {
            var circle = ball.GetComponent<CircleCollider2D>();
            if (circle != null && circle.enabled)
                radius = circle.radius;
        }
        radius += selectionOutlineWidth * 1.5f;

        for (int i = 0; i < line.positionCount; i++)
        {
            float angle = i * Mathf.PI * 2f / line.positionCount;
            line.SetPosition(i, new Vector3(Mathf.Cos(angle) * radius, Mathf.Sin(angle) * radius, -0.05f));
        }
        selectionOutlines[ball] = line;
    }

    private void ClearSelection()
    {
        foreach (LineRenderer outline in selectionOutlines.Values)
            if (outline != null) Destroy(outline.gameObject);
        selectionOutlines.Clear();
        selectedBalls.Clear();
    }

    private void OnGUI()
    {
        if (!isLassoing || lassoPoints.Count < 2) return;
        Color oldColor = GUI.color;
        GUI.color = selectionColor;
        for (int i = 1; i < lassoPoints.Count; i++) DrawScreenLine(lassoPoints[i - 1], lassoPoints[i], 2f);
        DrawScreenLine(lassoPoints[lassoPoints.Count - 1], lassoPoints[0], 2f);
        GUI.color = oldColor;
    }

    private void DrawScreenLine(Vector2 a, Vector2 b, float width)
    {
        a.y = Screen.height - a.y;
        b.y = Screen.height - b.y;
        Vector2 delta = b - a;
        float angle = Mathf.Atan2(delta.y, delta.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg;
        GUIUtility.RotateAroundPivot(angle, a);
        GUI.DrawTexture(new Rect(a.x, a.y - width / 2f, delta.magnitude, width), lassoPixel);
        GUIUtility.RotateAroundPivot(-angle, a);
    }

    private void OnDestroy()
    {
        ClearSelection();
        if (lassoPixel != null) Destroy(lassoPixel);
        if (Instance == this) Instance = null;
    }

    public static List<BallDrag> GetSelectedBalls()
    {
        if (Instance == null)
            return new List<BallDrag>();

        return new List<BallDrag>(Instance.selectedBalls);
    }


}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\LockRing.cs =====
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Draws a red ring around a board piece while it's locked by a player.
/// Crescent-aware, same strategy as BagRing.
/// </summary>
[RequireComponent(typeof(NetworkBall))]
public class LockRing : MonoBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private Color ringColor     = new Color(1f, 0.15f, 0.15f, 1f);
    [SerializeField] private float ringWidth     = 0.055f;
    [SerializeField] private float radiusPadding = 0.08f;
    [SerializeField] private int   segments      = 48;

    private NetworkBall  _networkBall;
    private LineRenderer _line;

    private void Awake()
    {
        _networkBall = GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
    }

    private void Start()
    {
        if (_networkBall == null) return;

        var go = new GameObject("Lock Ring");
        go.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
        go.transform.localPosition = Vector3.zero;

        _line = go.AddComponent<LineRenderer>();
        _line.useWorldSpace  = false;
        _line.loop           = true;
        _line.positionCount  = segments;
        _line.startWidth     = ringWidth;
        _line.endWidth       = ringWidth;
        _line.material       = new Material(Shader.Find("Sprites/Default"));
        _line.startColor     = ringColor;
        _line.endColor       = ringColor;
        _line.sortingOrder   = 25;
        _line.numCapVertices    = 4;
        _line.numCornerVertices = 4;
        _line.enabled = false;

        RebuildGeometry();
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (_networkBall == null || _line == null) return;

        bool locked = _networkBall.LockedBy.Value != ulong.MaxValue;
        if (_line.enabled != locked)
        {
            _line.enabled = locked;
            if (locked) RebuildGeometry();
        }

        int ringLayer = LayerMask.NameToLayer(
            _networkBall.CurrentZone.Value == (byte)BallZone.Hand
                ? "HandPieces"
                : "BoardPieces");
        if (ringLayer >= 0) _line.gameObject.layer = ringLayer;

        if (locked) _line.transform.rotation = Quaternion.identity;
    }

    private void RebuildGeometry()
    {
        float radius = GetActiveRadius() + radiusPadding;
        for (int i = 0; i < segments; i++)
        {
            float a = i * Mathf.PI * 2f / segments;
            _line.SetPosition(i, new Vector3(
                Mathf.Cos(a) * radius,
                Mathf.Sin(a) * radius,
                -0.05f));
        }
    }

    private float GetActiveRadius()
    {
        var dop = GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();
        if (dop != null && dop.IsActive)
        {
            var poly = GetComponent<PolygonCollider2D>();
            if (poly != null && poly.enabled)
                return Mathf.Max(poly.bounds.extents.x, poly.bounds.extents.y);
        }

        var circle = GetComponent<CircleCollider2D>();
        if (circle != null && circle.enabled)
            return circle.radius;

        return 0.5f;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\MathEquationBuilder.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;
using UnityEngine;

// Converts touching ball clusters into expression trees and solves them.
// Rewrite-based: balls form a graph; priority-ordered rules collapse the graph
// into a single ExprNode per side of '='. The ball GameObjects are never touched.
public static class MathEquationBuilder
{
            // ============================================================
    // Change versioning (used to cache solver results)
    // ============================================================

        private static int _structuralVersion;   // spawn / despawn
    private static bool _connectionDirty;    // a ball moved
    private static bool _contentDirty;       // a user edited a ball

    public static void MarkBallSpawned()        => _structuralVersion++;
    public static void MarkBallDespawned()      => _structuralVersion++;
    public static void MarkContentChanged()     => _contentDirty = true;
    public static void MarkConnectionsChanged() => _connectionDirty = true;

    public static int CurrentStructuralVersion => _structuralVersion;

    public static bool ConsumeContentDirty()
    {
        bool d = _contentDirty;
        _contentDirty = false;
        return d;
    }

    public static bool ConsumeConnectionDirty()
    {
        bool d = _connectionDirty;
        _connectionDirty = false;
        return d;
    }
    
        // ============================================================
    // Per-cluster caching support
    // ============================================================

    public sealed class CachedCluster
    {
        public List<BallNumber> balls;
        // Sum of per-ball content versions at last solve. Not exact, but a
        // monotone signal: if it changes, at least one ball changed.
        public long contentVersionAtLastSolve = -1;
        public BuildResult result;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Partitions activeBalls into connected components using a spatial hash
    /// so the cost is O(n * k) instead of O(n^2). Cell size must be at least
    /// 2x the maximum connection reach so that all possible connections are
    /// found inside the 3x3 neighborhood.
    /// </summary>
    public static List<List<BallNumber>> PartitionClusters(List<BallNumber> activeBalls)
    {
        var clusters = new List<List<BallNumber>>();
        if (activeBalls == null || activeBalls.Count == 0) return clusters;

        // The directional crescent's operand ports extend farther than a
        // standard ball's connection radius, so use a larger neighborhood.
        const float CellSize = 6.0f;

        var grid = new Dictionary<Vector2Int, List<int>>();
        var colliders = new Collider2D[activeBalls.Count];
        for (int i = 0; i < activeBalls.Count; i++)
        {
            var ball = activeBalls[i];
            if (ball == null) continue;
            colliders[i] = ball.GetComponent<Collider2D>();
            if (colliders[i] == null) continue;

            var center = colliders[i].bounds.center;
            var cell = new Vector2Int(
                Mathf.FloorToInt(center.x / CellSize),
                Mathf.FloorToInt(center.y / CellSize));
            if (!grid.TryGetValue(cell, out var list)) grid[cell] = list = new List<int>();
            list.Add(i);
        }

        bool[] visited = new bool[activeBalls.Count];

        for (int start = 0; start < activeBalls.Count; start++)
        {
            if (visited[start]) continue;
            visited[start] = true;

            var group = new List<BallNumber>();
            var pending = new Queue<int>();
            pending.Enqueue(start);
            group.Add(activeBalls[start]);

            while (pending.Count > 0)
            {
                int i = pending.Dequeue();
                var ci = colliders[i];
                if (ci == null) continue;

                var center = ci.bounds.center;
                var baseCell = new Vector2Int(
                    Mathf.FloorToInt(center.x / CellSize),
                    Mathf.FloorToInt(center.y / CellSize));

                for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++)
                for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++)
                {
                    var cell = new Vector2Int(baseCell.x + dx, baseCell.y + dy);
                    if (!grid.TryGetValue(cell, out var candidates)) continue;

                    foreach (int j in candidates)
                    {
                        if (visited[j] || j == i) continue;
                        if (colliders[j] == null) continue;
                        if (!BallConnectionRules.AreConnected(ci, colliders[j])) continue;
                        visited[j] = true;
                        pending.Enqueue(j);
                        group.Add(activeBalls[j]);
                    }
                }
            }

            clusters.Add(group);
        }

        return clusters;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Solves a single already-partitioned cluster. Returns a BuildResult
    /// scoped to this cluster only: its equations, connected pairs, and
    /// ambiguity/ignore sets. Never touches any other cluster's balls.
    /// </summary>
    public static BuildResult SolveCluster(List<BallNumber> cluster)
    {
        var result = new BuildResult();
        if (cluster == null || cluster.Count < 2) return result;

        // Record connected pairs for the tendril layer.
        for (int i = 0; i < cluster.Count; i++)
        {
            if (cluster[i] == null) continue;
            Collider2D ci = cluster[i].GetComponent<Collider2D>();
            if (ci == null) continue;

            for (int j = i + 1; j < cluster.Count; j++)
            {
                if (cluster[j] == null) continue;
                Collider2D cj = cluster[j].GetComponent<Collider2D>();
                if (cj == null) continue;
                if (BallConnectionRules.AreConnected(ci, cj))
                    result.connectedPairs.Add((cluster[i], cluster[j]));
            }
        }

        // Cheap filter: no '=' -> no equation.
        bool hasEquals = false;
        foreach (BallNumber b in cluster)
        {
            if (b == null) continue;
            if (b.GetMathContent().Trim() == "=") { hasEquals = true; break; }
        }
        if (!hasEquals) return result;

                if (TryBuildConnectedEquation(cluster, out ConnectedEquation eq,
                out HashSet<BallNumber> clusterIgnored,
                out HashSet<BallNumber> clusterAmbiguous))
        {
            result.equations.Add(eq);
            // Use clusterAmbiguous (every locally flagged ball), not
            // eq.ambiguousBalls (live-only). Removed balls must still be red.
            result.allAmbiguousBalls.UnionWith(clusterAmbiguous);
        }
        else
        {
            result.allAmbiguousBalls.UnionWith(clusterAmbiguous);
        }

        return result;
    }


    // ============================================================
    // Public result types (consumed by Spawner and EquationListDisplay)
    // ============================================================

    public sealed class ConnectedEquation
    {
        public ExprNode left;
        public ExprNode right;
        public List<BallNumber> balls = new List<BallNumber>();
        public BallNumber equalsBall;
        public HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguousBalls = new HashSet<BallNumber>();
        public string expression;

        // NEW:
        public List<ExprNode> slots = new List<ExprNode>();
        public List<List<BallNumber>> slotBalls = new List<List<BallNumber>>();
        public byte equationState = 0;   // 0 blue, 1 green, 2 red
    }

    public sealed class BuildResult
    {
        public List<ConnectedEquation> equations = new List<ConnectedEquation>();
        public HashSet<BallNumber> allAmbiguousBalls = new HashSet<BallNumber>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Every pair of connected balls considered by the builder, as unordered
        /// pairs. Spawner renders connection lines from this list so the visuals
        /// match the math graph exactly.
        /// </summary>
        public List<(BallNumber a, BallNumber b)> connectedPairs =
            new List<(BallNumber, BallNumber)>();
    }

     

    // ============================================================
    // Expression nodes
    // ============================================================

    public abstract class ExprNode
    {
        public List<BallNumber> sourceBalls = new List<BallNumber>();
    }

    public sealed class NumberNode : ExprNode
    {
        public double value;
        public string display;
    }

    public sealed class VarNode : ExprNode
    {
        public char name;
    }

    public sealed class UnknownNode : ExprNode { }

    public sealed class UnaryNode : ExprNode
    {
        public char op;
        public ExprNode operand;
    }

    public sealed class OpNode : ExprNode
    {
        public char op;             // + - * / ^
        public ExprNode left;
        public ExprNode right;
        public ExprNode fixedExponent;
        public bool hasFixedExponent;
        public DirectionalOperator directionalOperator;
    }

    public sealed class FuncNode : ExprNode
    {
        public string name;
        public ExprNode arg;
    }

    public sealed class BracketNode : ExprNode
    {
        public bool isOpen;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Internal graph
    // ============================================================

              private sealed class Node
    {
        public ExprNode expr;
        public List<Node> neighbors = new List<Node>();
                  public Vector2 position;
                  public bool alive = true;
                  public bool isBracketResult = false;
    }    

    private static List<BallNumber> FindTouchingGroup(
        List<BallNumber> activeBalls, Collider2D[] colliders,
        bool[] visited, int start)
    {
        var group = new List<BallNumber>();
        var pending = new Queue<int>();
        pending.Enqueue(start);
        visited[start] = true;

        while (pending.Count > 0)
        {
            int i = pending.Dequeue();
            group.Add(activeBalls[i]);
            Collider2D ci = colliders[i];
            if (ci == null) continue;

            for (int j = 0; j < activeBalls.Count; j++)
            {
                if (visited[j] || colliders[j] == null) continue;
                if (BallConnectionRules.AreConnected(ci, colliders[j]))
                {
                    visited[j] = true;
                    pending.Enqueue(j);
                }
            }
        }
        return group;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Cluster -> equation
    // ============================================================

        private static bool TryBuildConnectedEquation(
        List<BallNumber> cluster,
        out ConnectedEquation equation,
        out HashSet<BallNumber> clusterIgnored,
        out HashSet<BallNumber> clusterAmbiguous)
    {
        equation = null;
        clusterIgnored = new HashSet<BallNumber>();
        clusterAmbiguous = new HashSet<BallNumber>();

                

        // Red-flag any ball whose content was unrecognizable and was therefore
        // excluded from the graph (unknown non-empty, non-'=' content).
        foreach (BallNumber ball in cluster)
        {
            if (ball == null) continue;
            string raw = ball.GetMathContent().Trim();
            if (raw.Length == 0 || raw == "=") continue;

            ExprNode leaf = MakeLeaf(ball);
            if (leaf is UnknownNode)
                clusterAmbiguous.Add(ball);
        }

                    // --- Pass 1: raw adjacency for the local syntax checker ---
        // Use physical proximity (colliders), not graph edges, because the graph
        // doesn't exist yet. This is what the checker expects.
        var neighborsByBall = new Dictionary<BallNumber, List<BallNumber>>();
        for (int i = 0; i < cluster.Count; i++)
        {
            if (cluster[i] == null) continue;
            Collider2D ci = cluster[i].GetComponent<Collider2D>();
            var list = new List<BallNumber>();
            if (ci != null)
            {
                for (int j = 0; j < cluster.Count; j++)
                {
                    if (i == j || cluster[j] == null) continue;
                    Collider2D cj = cluster[j].GetComponent<Collider2D>();
                    if (cj == null) continue;
                    if (BallConnectionRules.AreConnected(ci, cj))
                        list.Add(cluster[j]);
                }
            }
            neighborsByBall[cluster[i]] = list;
        }

        HashSet<BallNumber> localSyntaxErrors = new HashSet<BallNumber>();
        BallSyntaxChecker.MarkLocalSyntaxErrors(cluster, neighborsByBall, localSyntaxErrors);

        // --- Pass 2: build the removal set ---
        // Red balls act as empty space: they are removed from the graph, their
        // neighbors do not connect through them, and they don't participate in
        // any region. Unknown-content balls (non-empty, not '=', MakeLeaf gave
        // UnknownNode) are treated the same way.
        var removed = new HashSet<BallNumber>(localSyntaxErrors);
        foreach (BallNumber ball in cluster)
        {
            if (ball == null) continue;
            string raw = ball.GetMathContent().Trim();
            if (raw.Length == 0 || raw == "=") continue;
            if (MakeLeaf(ball) is UnknownNode)
            {
                removed.Add(ball);
                clusterAmbiguous.Add(ball);
            }
        }
                clusterAmbiguous.UnionWith(localSyntaxErrors);

        // Balls that were removed from the graph (red-flagged or unknown content)
        // are excluded from the equation's ambiguous set. They still get red via
        // clusterAmbiguous → allAmbiguousBalls in Spawner, but they don't block
        // the solve. Only live-graph ambiguous balls should block it.
        var liveAmbiguous = new HashSet<BallNumber>();
        foreach (var b in clusterAmbiguous)
            if (!removed.Contains(b))
                liveAmbiguous.Add(b);

        // --- Pass 3: build the reduced graph ---
        List<Node> graph = BuildGraph(cluster, removed);

        // --- Find all '=' balls ---
        var equalsNodes = new List<Node>();
        foreach (Node n in graph)
            if (n.expr is EqualsMarker)
                equalsNodes.Add(n);

        if (equalsNodes.Count == 0)
            return false;   // no equation to build

        // --- Partition the graph by '=' into regions ---
        // Each region is a connected component of non-'=' nodes after removing all '=' nodes.
        var regions = PartitionByEquals(graph, equalsNodes);

        // --- Solve each region recursively ---
        var regionResults = new List<ExprNode>();
        var regionBalls = new List<List<BallNumber>>();
        foreach (var region in regions)
        {
            if (region.Count == 0) continue;
            ExprNode value = SolveRegion(region, clusterAmbiguous);
            regionResults.Add(value);
            var bl = new List<BallNumber>();
            foreach (Node n in region)
                bl.AddRange(n.expr.sourceBalls);
            regionBalls.Add(bl);
        }

        // --- Assign each region to the '=' slots it touches ---
        // Slots are (equalsNode, sideIndex) pairs. For now, treat a chain of
        // '=' balls connected to each other as a single multi-slot equation.
        // Each region touches one or more '=' balls; each '=' ball has the set
        // of regions adjacent to it as its slots.

        // --- Build the ConnectedEquation ---
        // We only support one equation per cluster for now (same as before:
        // multiple '=' in a cluster is rejected). If your game supports
        // separate equations in one cluster, this is where to branch.
        if (equalsNodes.Count != 1)
        {
            foreach (var eqNode in equalsNodes) clusterAmbiguous.Add(eqNode.expr.sourceBalls[0]);
            return false;
        }

        Node equalsNode = equalsNodes[0];
        var slotRegions = new List<ExprNode>();
        var slotBalls = new List<List<BallNumber>>();
        foreach (Node nb in equalsNode.neighbors)
        {
            if (nb == null || !nb.alive) continue;
            int idx = regionIndexOf(regions, nb);
            if (idx < 0) continue;
            slotRegions.Add(regionResults[idx]);
            slotBalls.Add(regionBalls[idx]);
        }

        if (slotRegions.Count < 2)
        {
            // 0 or 1 slot: '=' is blue. Still return true so the visual layer
            // can color it blue; callers check eq.equationState.
            var blueEq = new ConnectedEquation
            {
                left = slotRegions.Count > 0 ? slotRegions[0] : null,
                right = null,
                balls = cluster,
                equalsBall = equalsNode.expr.sourceBalls.Count > 0
                    ? equalsNode.expr.sourceBalls[0] : null,
                slots = slotRegions,
                slotBalls = slotBalls,
                ambiguousBalls = liveAmbiguous,
                expression = slotRegions.Count > 0 ? Render(slotRegions[0]) + "=" : "=",
                equationState = 0,   // blue
            };
            equation = blueEq;
            return true;
        }

        // 2+ slots: compare, optionally fill an empty ball.
        var emptyBalls = new List<BallNumber>();
        foreach (var bl in slotBalls)
            foreach (var b in bl)
                if (b != null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(b.GetMathContent().Trim()))
                    emptyBalls.Add(b);

        byte state;
        bool solved = EvaluateEquals(slotRegions, emptyBalls, out state);

        var eq = new ConnectedEquation
        {
            left = slotRegions[0],
            right = slotRegions.Count > 1 ? slotRegions[1] : null,
            balls = cluster,
            equalsBall = equalsNode.expr.sourceBalls.Count > 0
                ? equalsNode.expr.sourceBalls[0] : null,
            slots = slotRegions,
            slotBalls = slotBalls,
            ambiguousBalls = liveAmbiguous,
            expression = RenderSlots(slotRegions),
            equationState = state,
        };

        equation = eq;
        return true;
    }

    private static List<List<Node>> PartitionByEquals(
        List<Node> graph, List<Node> equalsNodes)
    {
        var regions = new List<List<Node>>();
        var equalsSet = new HashSet<Node>(equalsNodes);
        var visited = new HashSet<Node>();

        foreach (Node start in graph)
        {
            if (visited.Contains(start)) continue;
            if (equalsSet.Contains(start))
            {
                visited.Add(start);
                continue;
            }

            var region = new List<Node>();
            var queue = new Queue<Node>();
            queue.Enqueue(start);
            visited.Add(start);

            while (queue.Count > 0)
            {
                Node cur = queue.Dequeue();
                region.Add(cur);
                foreach (Node nb in cur.neighbors)
                {
                    if (visited.Contains(nb)) continue;
                    if (equalsSet.Contains(nb)) continue;   // don't cross '='
                    visited.Add(nb);
                    queue.Enqueue(nb);
                }
            }

            if (region.Count > 0) regions.Add(region);
        }

        return regions;
    }

    private static ExprNode SolveRegion(
        List<Node> nodes, HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        if (nodes == null || nodes.Count == 0) return null;

        // Each step mutates nodes in place (kills merged nodes
        // and adds a replacement node).

        // Step 1: brackets (recursive).
        ResolveBrackets(nodes, ambiguous);

        // Step 2: adjacency merge (numbers/algebra/fractions only).
        MergeAdjacent(nodes);

        // Step 3: functions.
        ApplyFunctions(nodes, ambiguous);

        // Step 4: powers.
        ApplyPowers(nodes, ambiguous);

        // Step 5: products and division.
        ApplyProducts(nodes, ambiguous);

        // Step 6: sums and subtraction.
        ApplySums(nodes, ambiguous);

                // Count live nodes. If not exactly one, the region is unsolvable.
                int liveCount = 0;
        Node only = null;
        foreach (Node n in nodes)
        {
            if (!n.alive) continue;
            if (n.expr is UnknownNode) continue;   // blanks don't count toward collapse
            liveCount++;
            only = n;
        }

                Debug.Log($"[Region] liveCount={liveCount} nodes=" +
                  string.Join(",", nodes.FindAll(n => n.alive).ConvertAll(n => Content(n))));

                if (liveCount == 1 && only != null) return only.expr;

        // If the only leftovers are blanks, the region is "just a blank".
        // Return an UnknownNode carrying them, but don't red-flag anything.
        if (liveCount == 0)
        {
            var blankOnly = new UnknownNode();
            foreach (Node n in nodes)
            {
                if (!n.alive) continue;
                if (n.expr is UnknownNode) blankOnly.sourceBalls.AddRange(n.expr.sourceBalls);
            }
            return blankOnly;
        }

        // Region did not fully collapse. Flag every remaining live non-blank
        // ball red and return an UnknownNode so the '=' cannot solve it.
        var unknown = new UnknownNode();
        foreach (Node n in nodes)
        {
            if (!n.alive) continue;
            if (n.expr is UnknownNode) continue;   // blanks never red-flag
            foreach (var b in n.expr.sourceBalls) ambiguous.Add(b);
            unknown.sourceBalls.AddRange(n.expr.sourceBalls);
        }
        return unknown;
    }

    private static void ResolveBrackets(
        List<Node> nodes, HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        while (true)
        {
            // Find an innermost '(' / ')' pair (one with no live bracket between them).
            Node open = null, close = null;
            foreach (Node n in nodes)
            {
                if (!n.alive || !(n.expr is BracketNode b) || !b.isOpen) continue;

                // BFS from open through non-bracket live neighbors until we hit a ')'.
                // Ignore already-consumed brackets. If we hit another '(' first, that
                // pair is inner and must resolve first — skip this open for now.
                var queue = new Queue<Node>();
                var seen = new HashSet<Node> { n };
                queue.Enqueue(n);
                bool foundInnerOpen = false;
                Node candidateClose = null;

                                while (queue.Count > 0 && candidateClose == null)
                {
                    Node cur = queue.Dequeue();
                    foreach (Node nb in cur.neighbors)
                    {
                        if (nb == n || !nb.alive || seen.Contains(nb)) continue;
                        seen.Add(nb);

                        if (nb.expr is BracketNode cb)
                        {
                            // Other open brackets are not walkable for this
                            // search: they mark the start of a nested pair that
                            // must resolve first. Only a close here is a match.
                            if (cb.isOpen) continue;
                            candidateClose = nb;
                            break;
                        }

                        if (nb.expr is EqualsMarker) continue;   // never cross '='

                        queue.Enqueue(nb);
                    }
                }

                if (candidateClose == null) continue;
                open = n; close = candidateClose;
                break;
            }

                        if (open == null)
            {
                Debug.Log($"[Bracket] no pair found in region of {nodes.Count} nodes: " +
                          string.Join(",", nodes.ConvertAll(n => Content(n))));
                return;
            }

            Debug.Log($"[Bracket] pairing open='{Content(open)}' close='{Content(close)}'");

                       // Build the interior: candidates reachable from 'open' without
            // crossing 'close' or '=', then filtered by 1D projection between
            // 'open' and 'close' so exterior balls adjacent to the bracket
            // cannot leak in.
            var candidates = new List<Node>();
            var interiorQueue = new Queue<Node>();
            var interiorSeen = new HashSet<Node> { open, close };
            foreach (Node nb in open.neighbors)
            {
                if (nb == close || !nb.alive || interiorSeen.Contains(nb)) continue;
                if (nb.expr is EqualsMarker) continue;
                interiorSeen.Add(nb);
                interiorQueue.Enqueue(nb);
            }
            while (interiorQueue.Count > 0)
            {
                Node cur = interiorQueue.Dequeue();
                candidates.Add(cur);
                foreach (Node nb in cur.neighbors)
                {
                    if (interiorSeen.Contains(nb)) continue;
                    if (nb.expr is BracketNode) continue;
                    if (nb.expr is EqualsMarker) continue;
                    interiorSeen.Add(nb);
                    interiorQueue.Enqueue(nb);
                }
            }

            Vector2 openPos = SourcePos(open);
            Vector2 closePos = SourcePos(close);
            Vector2 dir = closePos - openPos;
            float D = dir.magnitude;
            if (D < 1e-4f) return;
            Vector2 dirN = dir / D;

                        var interior = new List<Node>();
            foreach (Node c in candidates)
            {
                float t = Vector2.Dot(SourcePos(c) - openPos, dirN);
                bool inside = t > 1e-3f && t < D - 1e-3f;
                Debug.Log($"[Bracket] candidate '{Content(c)}' t={t:F3} D={D:F3} inside={inside}");
                if (inside) interior.Add(c);
            }

            var interiorSet = new HashSet<Node>(interior);

                        Debug.Log($"[Bracket] interior count={interior.Count}: " +
                      string.Join(",", interior.ConvertAll(n => Content(n))));
            ExprNode interiorResult = SolveRegion(interior, ambiguous);
            Debug.Log($"[Bracket] interiorResult='{(interiorResult != null ? Render(interiorResult) : "null")}'");
            if (interiorResult == null)
            {
                open.alive = false;
                close.alive = false;
                ambiguous.Add(open.expr.sourceBalls[0]);
                ambiguous.Add(close.expr.sourceBalls[0]);
                continue;
            }

            // Create a merged node holding the interior result plus both brackets.
            var mergedExpr = interiorResult;
            mergedExpr.sourceBalls.AddRange(open.expr.sourceBalls);
            mergedExpr.sourceBalls.AddRange(close.expr.sourceBalls);

            var mergedNode = new Node
            {
                expr = mergedExpr,
                position = (open.position + close.position) * 0.5f,
                isBracketResult = true,
            };

                        // Reattach neighbors: exterior neighbors of open/close/interior.
            // Use interiorSet (projection-filtered) so exterior candidates that
            // merely touch the bracket (e.g. 'sin' in 'sin(0+0)') stay attached
            // to the merged node.
            var mergedNeighbors = new HashSet<Node>();
            foreach (Node m in new[] { open, close })
                foreach (Node nb in m.neighbors)
                    if (nb.alive && nb != open && nb != close && !interiorSet.Contains(nb))
                        mergedNeighbors.Add(nb);
            foreach (Node i in interior)
                foreach (Node nb in i.neighbors)
                    if (nb.alive && nb != open && nb != close && !interiorSet.Contains(nb))
                        mergedNeighbors.Add(nb);

            foreach (Node nb in mergedNeighbors)
            {
                nb.neighbors.RemoveAll(x => x == open || x == close || interiorSet.Contains(x));
                nb.neighbors.Add(mergedNode);
                mergedNode.neighbors.Add(nb);
            }

            open.alive = false;
            close.alive = false;
            foreach (Node i in interior) i.alive = false;

            nodes.Add(mergedNode);
        }
    }

    // ---- Step 2: adjacency (numbers / algebra / fractions only) ----

    private static void MergeAdjacent(List<Node> nodes)
    {
        while (true)
        {
                                   Node a = null, b = null;
            foreach (Node n in nodes)
            {
                if (!n.alive || !IsAdjacencyLeaf(n.expr)) continue;
                foreach (Node nb in n.neighbors)
                {
                    if (nb.alive && IsAdjacencyLeaf(nb.expr)) { a = n; b = nb; break; }
                }
                if (a != null) break;
            }
            if (a == null) return;

                        ExprNode product = MakeProduct(a.expr, b.expr);
            MergeInto(nodes, new List<Node> { a, b }, product);
        }
    }

           private static bool IsAdjacencyLeaf(ExprNode expr)
    {
        // Blanks are unknown operands: they participate in adjacency exactly
        // like variables, so '2 [blank]' becomes '2 * ?' and the equation
        // solver can determine the blank's value.
        return expr is NumberNode || expr is VarNode || expr is UnknownNode;
    }

    public static byte ReEvaluateAfterFill(ConnectedEquation eq)
    {
        if (eq == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(eq.expression)) return eq?.equationState ?? 0;

        BallNumber solverOutput = null;
        foreach (BallNumber ball in eq.balls)
        {
            if (ball != null && ball.IsSolverOutput &&
                !string.IsNullOrEmpty(ball.GetSolverOutputContent()))
            {
                if (solverOutput != null) return eq.equationState;  // multiple fills: leave as-is
                solverOutput = ball;
            }
        }
        if (solverOutput == null) return eq.equationState;

        string text = eq.expression;
        char unknown = MathEquationSolver.UnknownSymbol;
        if (text.IndexOf(unknown) < 0) return eq.equationState;
        if (text.IndexOf(unknown) != text.LastIndexOf(unknown)) return eq.equationState;

        text = text.Replace(unknown.ToString(), "(" + solverOutput.GetSolverOutputContent() + ")");
        if (!TrySplitEqualityChain(text, out string[] parts)) return eq.equationState;

        var empty = new Dictionary<char, double>();
        if (!MathEquationSolver.TryEvaluateNumericExpression(parts[0], empty, out double first))
            return eq.equationState;

        for (int i = 1; i < parts.Length; i++)
        {
            if (!MathEquationSolver.TryEvaluateNumericExpression(parts[i], empty, out double value) ||
                !ValuesAreEquivalent(first, value))
                return eq.equationState;
        }
        return 1;   // green
    }

    

    private static ExprNode MakeProduct(ExprNode a, ExprNode b)
    {
        // Numeric collapse when both sides are numbers.
        if (a is NumberNode na && b is NumberNode nb)
        {
            var merged = new NumberNode
            {
                value = na.value * nb.value,
                display = "(" + na.display + MathEquationSolver.ImplicitMultiplicationSymbol + nb.display + ")",
            };
            return merged;
        }
        var op = new OpNode { op = '*' };
        op.left = a;
        op.right = b;
        return op;
    }

    // ---- Step 3: functions ----

    private static void ApplyFunctions(List<Node> nodes, HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        while (true)
        {
            Node fn = null;
            foreach (Node n in nodes)
            {
                if (!n.alive || !(n.expr is FuncNode f)) continue;
                if (f.name == "1/x" && f.arg != null) continue;   // already bound
                if (f.arg != null) continue;                       // ordinary function already bound
                fn = n; break;
            }
            if (fn == null) return;

            var func = (FuncNode)fn.expr;
            List<Node> neighbors = LiveNeighbors(fn);

                        // Count real operands. Bracket results count as operands even
            // though their internal ExprNode is often an OpNode. Operators,
            // brackets, and equals markers do not.
            var operands = new List<Node>();
            foreach (Node nb in neighbors)
            {
                if (nb.isBracketResult) { operands.Add(nb); continue; }
                if (nb.expr is OpNode) continue;
                if (nb.expr is BracketNode) continue;
                if (nb.expr is EqualsMarker) continue;
                operands.Add(nb);
            }

            if (operands.Count != 1)
            {
                // 0 or 2+ operands → red, inert.
                foreach (BallNumber b in func.sourceBalls) ambiguous.Add(b);
                fn.alive = false;
                continue;
            }

            Node arg = operands[0];

            // Try numeric collapse for pow / 1/x / normal functions.
            ExprNode collapsed = TryCollapseFunction(func, arg.expr);
            if (collapsed != null)
            {
                collapsed.sourceBalls.AddRange(func.sourceBalls);
                collapsed.sourceBalls.AddRange(arg.expr.sourceBalls);
                AbsorbInto(nodes, fn, new List<Node> { arg });
                fn.expr = collapsed;
            }
            else
            {
                func.arg = arg.expr;
                AbsorbInto(nodes, fn, new List<Node> { arg });
            }
        }
    }

    private static ExprNode TryCollapseFunction(FuncNode func, ExprNode arg)
    {
        if (func.name == "1/x" && func.arg != null)
        {
            // Already bound (from a '/x' ball).
            if (arg is NumberNode an && an.value != 0)
            {
                return new NumberNode
                {
                    value = 1.0 / an.value,
                    display = "(1/" + an.display + ")",
                };
            }
            return null;
        }

        if (arg is NumberNode num &&
            TryEvaluateFunction(func.name, num.value, out double result))
        {
            return new NumberNode
            {
                value = result,
                display = FormatFunctionDisplay(func.name, num.display, result),
            };
        }
        return null;
    }

    // ---- Step 4: powers ----

    private static void ApplyPowers(List<Node> nodes, HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        ApplyExplicitPowers(nodes, ambiguous);
        ApplyDirectionalOperators(nodes, '^', ambiguous);
    }

    private static void ApplyExplicitPowers(List<Node> nodes, HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        while (true)
        {
            Node powerNode = null;
            foreach (Node node in nodes)
            {
                if (!node.alive || !(node.expr is OpNode op) ||
                    op.op != '^' || !op.hasFixedExponent ||
                    op.left != null || op.right != null)
                    continue;

                powerNode = node;
                break;
            }

            if (powerNode == null)
                return;

            var power = (OpNode)powerNode.expr;
            Node argument = null;
            bool invalidNeighbor = false;
            foreach (Node neighbor in LiveNeighbors(powerNode))
            {
                if (IsPendingOperator(neighbor.expr))
                    continue;
                if (argument != null)
                    invalidNeighbor = true;
                argument = neighbor;
            }

            if (invalidNeighbor || argument == null)
            {
                foreach (BallNumber ball in power.sourceBalls)
                    ambiguous.Add(ball);
                powerNode.alive = false;
                continue;
            }

            var result = new OpNode
            {
                op = '^',
                left = argument.expr,
                right = power.fixedExponent,
            };
            result.sourceBalls.AddRange(power.sourceBalls);
            powerNode.expr = result;
            AbsorbInto(nodes, powerNode, new List<Node> { argument });
        }
    }

    // ---- Step 5: products (*, /, and implicit bracket-number) ----

    private static void ApplyProducts(List<Node> nodes, HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        ApplyDirectionalOperators(nodes, '/', ambiguous);

        while (true)
        {
            // First: any '*' with 2+ live groups touching it merges them.
            Node star = null;
            foreach (Node n in nodes)
            {
                if (!n.alive || !(n.expr is OpNode op) || op.op != '*') continue;
                if (op.left != null || op.right != null) continue;
                if (LiveNeighbors(n).Count >= 2) { star = n; break; }
            }

            if (star != null)
            {
                var neighbors = LiveNeighbors(star);
                // Merge everything the '*' touches into a single product.
                ExprNode acc = neighbors[0].expr;
                for (int i = 1; i < neighbors.Count; i++)
                {
                    var op = new OpNode { op = '*' };
                    op.left = acc;
                    op.right = neighbors[i].expr;
                    acc = op;
                }
                acc.sourceBalls.AddRange(star.expr.sourceBalls);
                AbsorbInto(nodes, star, neighbors);
                star.expr = acc;
                continue;
            }

                        // Then: implicit '*' between a bracket result and an adjacent
            // number/variable leaf. Exactly one side must be a bracket result,
            // and the other side must be a plain leaf. This prevents the
            // bracket result from consuming '=', operators, functions, or
            // another bracket (those are handled by their own steps).
            bool didImplicit = false;
            foreach (Node n in nodes)
            {
                if (!n.alive) continue;
                foreach (Node nb in n.neighbors)
                {
                    if (!nb.alive || nb == n) continue;
                    if (n.isBracketResult == nb.isBracketResult) continue;

                    Node otherSide = n.isBracketResult ? nb : n;
                    if (!IsAdjacencyLeaf(otherSide.expr)) continue;

                    ExprNode combined = MakeProduct(n.expr, nb.expr);
                    combined.sourceBalls.AddRange(n.expr.sourceBalls);
                    combined.sourceBalls.AddRange(nb.expr.sourceBalls);
                    var mergedNode = MergeInto(nodes, new List<Node> { n, nb }, combined);
                    mergedNode.isBracketResult = false;
                    didImplicit = true;
                    break;
                }
                if (didImplicit) break;
            }
            if (didImplicit) continue;

                        // Flag dangling '*' as red. Skip already-merged '*' nodes.
            foreach (Node n in nodes)
            {
                if (!n.alive || !(n.expr is OpNode op) || op.op != '*') continue;
                if (op.left != null || op.right != null) continue;   // already merged
                if (LiveNeighbors(n).Count < 2)
                {
                    ambiguous.Add(n.expr.sourceBalls[0]);
                    n.alive = false;
                }
            }

            return;
        }
    }

    

    // ---- Step 6: sums (+, -) ----

    private static void ApplySums(List<Node> nodes, HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        ApplyDirectionalOperators(nodes, '-', ambiguous);

        while (true)
        {
            Node plus = null;
            foreach (Node node in nodes)
            {
                if (!node.alive || !(node.expr is OpNode op) || op.op != '+')
                    continue;
                if (op.left == null && op.right == null && LiveNeighbors(node).Count >= 2)
                {
                    plus = node;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (plus != null)
            {
                var neighbors = LiveNeighbors(plus);
                ExprNode acc = neighbors[0].expr;
                for (int i = 1; i < neighbors.Count; i++)
                {
                    var op = new OpNode { op = '+' };
                    op.left = acc;
                    op.right = neighbors[i].expr;
                    acc = op;
                }
                acc.sourceBalls.AddRange(plus.expr.sourceBalls);
                AbsorbInto(nodes, plus, neighbors);
                plus.expr = acc;
                continue;
            }

            foreach (Node node in nodes)
            {
                if (!node.alive || !(node.expr is OpNode op) || op.op != '+')
                    continue;
                if (op.left != null || op.right != null)
                    continue;
                if (LiveNeighbors(node).Count < 2)
                {
                    ambiguous.Add(node.expr.sourceBalls[0]);
                    node.alive = false;
                }
            }

            return;
        }
    }

    private static void ApplyDirectionalOperators(
    List<Node> nodes, char operation, HashSet<BallNumber> ambiguous)
    {
        while (true)
        {
            Node operatorNode = null;
            foreach (Node node in nodes)
            {
                if (!node.alive || !(node.expr is OpNode op) ||
                    op.op != operation || op.directionalOperator == null ||
                    op.left != null || op.right != null)
                    continue;

                operatorNode = node;
                break;
            }

            if (operatorNode == null) return;

            var opExpr = (OpNode)operatorNode.expr;
            Node outsideOperand = null;
            Node insideOperand  = null;
            bool invalidNeighbor = false;
            bool adjacentCrescent = false;

            foreach (Node neighbor in LiveNeighbors(operatorNode))
            {
                if (neighbor.expr is OpNode nbOp &&
                    nbOp.directionalOperator != null &&
                    nbOp.left == null && nbOp.right == null)
                {
                    // Two crescents touching: both go red, stop trying to bind.
                    adjacentCrescent = true;
                    continue;
                }

                if (IsPendingOperator(neighbor.expr))
                    continue;

                if (neighbor.expr.sourceBalls.Count == 0)
                {
                    invalidNeighbor = true;
                    continue;
                }

                Collider2D operandCollider =
                    neighbor.expr.sourceBalls[0].GetComponent<Collider2D>();
                if (operandCollider == null)
                {
                    invalidNeighbor = true;
                    continue;
                }

                float operandRadius = Mathf.Max(
                    operandCollider.bounds.extents.x,
                    operandCollider.bounds.extents.y);
                if (!opExpr.directionalOperator.TryGetOperandSide(
                        neighbor.position, operandRadius, out bool isInside))
                {
                    invalidNeighbor = true;
                    continue;
                }

                if (isInside)
                {
                    if (insideOperand != null) invalidNeighbor = true;
                    insideOperand = neighbor;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (outsideOperand != null) invalidNeighbor = true;
                    outsideOperand = neighbor;
                }
            }

            if (adjacentCrescent || invalidNeighbor ||
                outsideOperand == null || insideOperand == null)
            {
                foreach (BallNumber ball in opExpr.sourceBalls)
                    ambiguous.Add(ball);
                operatorNode.alive = false;
                continue;
            }

            var result = new OpNode
            {
                op                = operation,
                left              = outsideOperand.expr,
                right             = insideOperand.expr,
                directionalOperator = opExpr.directionalOperator,
            };
            result.sourceBalls.AddRange(opExpr.sourceBalls);
            operatorNode.expr = result;
            AbsorbInto(nodes, operatorNode, new List<Node> { outsideOperand, insideOperand });
        }
    }

    private static bool IsPendingOperator(ExprNode expression)
    {
        return expression is OpNode op && op.left == null && op.right == null;
    }

    // ---- Step 7: equals evaluation ----

    private static bool EvaluateEquals(
        List<ExprNode> slots,
        List<BallNumber> emptyBalls,
        out byte state)
    {
        state = 2;   // default red
        if (slots == null || slots.Count < 2) { state = 0; return false; }

        // Try numeric evaluation of each slot.
        var empty = new Dictionary<char, double>();
        var values = new List<double>();
        foreach (var slot in slots)
        {
            string expr = Render(slot);
            if (!MathEquationSolver.TryEvaluateNumericExpression(expr, empty, out double v))
            {
                // Not numerically evaluable. Could be symbolic or empty.
                if (emptyBalls.Count == 1)
                {
                    // Single empty ball: leave blue until solver fills it.
                    state = 0;
                    return false;
                }
                state = 2;
                return false;
            }
            values.Add(v);
        }

        for (int i = 1; i < values.Count; i++)
        {
            if (!ValuesAreEquivalent(values[0], values[i]))
            {
                state = 2;
                return false;
            }
        }

        state = 1;
        return true;
    }

    private static int regionIndexOf(List<List<Node>> regions, Node node)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < regions.Count; i++)
            if (regions[i].Contains(node)) return i;
        return -1;
    }

    private static string RenderSlots(List<ExprNode> slots)
    {
        var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < slots.Count; i++)
        {
            if (i > 0) sb.Append('=');
            sb.Append(Render(slots[i]));
        }
        return sb.ToString();
    }

    // Marker node for the '=' ball; never participates in rewrite rules.
    private sealed class EqualsMarker : ExprNode { }

    // ============================================================
    // Graph construction
    // ============================================================

            private static List<Node> BuildGraph(
        List<BallNumber> cluster,
        HashSet<BallNumber> removed)
    {
        var byBall = new Dictionary<BallNumber, Node>();
        var nodes = new List<Node>();

        foreach (BallNumber ball in cluster)
        {
            ExprNode expr = MakeLeaf(ball);
            byBall[ball] = new Node { expr = expr, position = ball.transform.position };

            if (removed != null && removed.Contains(ball))
                continue;   // not added to the graph; acts as empty space

            nodes.Add(byBall[ball]);
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < cluster.Count; i++)
        {
            if (removed != null && removed.Contains(cluster[i])) continue;
            Collider2D ci = cluster[i].GetComponent<Collider2D>();
            if (ci == null) continue;

            for (int j = i + 1; j < cluster.Count; j++)
            {
                if (removed != null && removed.Contains(cluster[j])) continue;
                Collider2D cj = cluster[j].GetComponent<Collider2D>();
                if (cj == null) continue;
                if (!BallConnectionRules.AreConnected(ci, cj)) continue;

                Node a = byBall[cluster[i]];
                Node b = byBall[cluster[j]];
                a.neighbors.Add(b);
                b.neighbors.Add(a);
            }
        }

        return nodes;
    }

    private static ExprNode MakeLeaf(BallNumber ball)
    {
        string text = ball.GetMathContent().Trim();

        if (text.Length == 0)
        {
            var u = new UnknownNode();
            u.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return u;
        }

        if (text == "=")
        {
            var m = new EqualsMarker();
            m.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return m;
        }

        if (text == "(" || text == ")")
        {
            var b = new BracketNode { isOpen = text == "(" };
            b.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return b;
        }

        if (DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(text))
        {
            var op = new OpNode
            {
                op = text[0],
                directionalOperator = ball.GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>(),
            };
            op.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return op;
        }

        // --- Explicit fraction balls: '/N', '/x' are NUMBERS ---
        if (MathSyntax.IsFractionBall(text))
        {
            string suffix = MathSyntax.UnaryPrefixBoundValue(text);
