            // '/N' -> number 1/N.
            if (double.TryParse(suffix,
                    NumberStyles.Float,
                    CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    out double denom) && denom != 0)
            {
                var n = new NumberNode
                {
                    value = 1.0 / denom,
                    display = "1/" + suffix,
                };
                n.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
                return n;
            }

            // '/x' -> symbolic 1/x, baked in, no neighbor consumed.
            if (suffix.Length == 1 && MathSyntax.IsVariableLetter(suffix[0]))
            {
                var v = new VarNode { name = suffix[0] };
                v.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
                var f = new FuncNode { name = "1/x", arg = v };
                f.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
                return f;
            }

            // '/pi', '/e' -> constants.
            if (MathSyntax.IsConstantName(suffix))
            {
                double cv = suffix.ToLowerInvariant() == "pi" ? System.Math.PI : System.Math.E;
                var n = new NumberNode { value = 1.0 / cv, display = "1/" + suffix };
                n.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
                return n;
            }

            var bad = new UnknownNode();
            bad.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return bad;
        }

        // --- Explicit power balls: '^N', '^x' bind one base operand ---
        if (MathSyntax.IsPowerBall(text))
        {
            string suffix = MathSyntax.UnaryPrefixBoundValue(text);

            ExprNode exponentExpr;
            if (double.TryParse(suffix,
                    NumberStyles.Float,
                    CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    out double exp))
            {
                exponentExpr = new NumberNode { value = exp, display = suffix };
            }
            else if (suffix.Length == 1 && MathSyntax.IsVariableLetter(suffix[0]))
            {
                exponentExpr = new VarNode { name = suffix[0] };
            }
            else
            {
                var bad = new UnknownNode();
                bad.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
                return bad;
            }

            var power = new OpNode
            {
                op = '^',
                fixedExponent = exponentExpr,
                hasFixedExponent = true,
            };
            power.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return power;
        }

        // --- Remaining function balls (sin, cos, ..., %, !, 1/x typed literally) ---
        if (IsFunctionText(text))
        {
            var f = new FuncNode { name = text.ToLowerInvariant() };
            f.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return f;
        }

        // --- Multiplication operator ---
        if (text == "*" || text == "\u00d7")
        {
            var op = new OpNode { op = '*' };
            op.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return op;
        }

        // --- Addition operator ---
        if (text == "+")
        {
            var op = new OpNode { op = '+' };
            op.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return op;
        }

        // --- Numbers / constants / variables ---
        if (double.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Float,
                CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double num))
        {
            var n = new NumberNode { value = num, display = text };
            n.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return n;
        }

        string lowerText = text.ToLowerInvariant();
        if (lowerText == "pi" || lowerText == "e")
        {
            double constantValue = lowerText == "pi" ? System.Math.PI : System.Math.E;
            var c = new NumberNode { value = constantValue, display = text };
            c.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return c;
        }

        if (text.Length == 1 && MathSyntax.IsVariableLetter(text[0]))
        {
            var v = new VarNode { name = text[0] };
            v.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
            return v;
        }

        var unknown = new UnknownNode();
        unknown.sourceBalls.Add(ball);
        return unknown;
    }

    private static bool IsFunctionText(string text)
    {
        // Bare '-', '/', and '^' are directional operators handled earlier.
        return MathSyntax.IsUnaryFunctionName(text) ||
            text == "%" || text == "!" || text == "1/x";
    }


    private static Node FirstLive(List<Node> nodes)
    {
        foreach (Node n in nodes) if (n.alive) return n;
        return null;
    }

        private static string Content(Node n)
    {
        if (n == null) return "<null>";
        if (n.expr == null) return "<noexpr>";
        string s = Render(n.expr);
        return s.Length == 0 ? "<empty>" : s;
    }

        private static Vector2 SourcePos(Node n)
    {
        if (n?.expr?.sourceBalls != null && n.expr.sourceBalls.Count > 0)
        {
            var b = n.expr.sourceBalls[0];
            if (b != null) return b.transform.position;
        }
        return Vector2.zero;
    }

    private static List<Node> LiveNeighbors(Node n)
    {
        var list = new List<Node>();
        foreach (Node nb in n.neighbors) if (nb.alive) list.Add(nb);
        return list;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Graph surgery
    // ============================================================

    // Replace 'toMerge' with a single new node whose ExprNode is 'replacement'.
    private static Node MergeInto(
        List<Node> allNodes, List<Node> toMerge, ExprNode replacement)
    {
        var mergedSet = new HashSet<Node>(toMerge);
        var newNeighbors = new List<Node>();

        foreach (Node m in toMerge)
            foreach (Node nb in m.neighbors)
                if (!mergedSet.Contains(nb) && !newNeighbors.Contains(nb))
                    newNeighbors.Add(nb);

        Vector2 mergedPosition = Vector2.zero;
        foreach (Node node in toMerge)
            mergedPosition += node.position;
        if (toMerge.Count > 0)
            mergedPosition /= toMerge.Count;

        var mergedNode = new Node { expr = replacement, position = mergedPosition };

        foreach (Node nb in newNeighbors)
        {
            nb.neighbors.RemoveAll(x => mergedSet.Contains(x));
            nb.neighbors.Add(mergedNode);
            mergedNode.neighbors.Add(nb);
        }

        foreach (Node m in toMerge)
            foreach (BallNumber b in m.expr.sourceBalls)
                replacement.sourceBalls.Add(b);

        foreach (Node m in toMerge) m.alive = false;

        allNodes.Add(mergedNode);
        return mergedNode;
    }

    // Absorb 'absorbed' into 'keeper'. Keeper survives; absorbed die.
    private static void AbsorbInto(
        List<Node> allNodes, Node keeper, List<Node> absorbed)
    {
        var absorbedSet = new HashSet<Node>(absorbed);

        foreach (Node a in absorbed)
        {
            foreach (BallNumber b in a.expr.sourceBalls)
                keeper.expr.sourceBalls.Add(b);

            foreach (Node nb in a.neighbors)
            {
                if (absorbedSet.Contains(nb) || nb == keeper) continue;
                if (!keeper.neighbors.Contains(nb)) keeper.neighbors.Add(nb);
                nb.neighbors.RemoveAll(x => absorbedSet.Contains(x) || x == a);
                if (!nb.neighbors.Contains(keeper)) nb.neighbors.Add(keeper);
            }

            a.alive = false;
        }
    }

   
    private static string FormatFunctionDisplay(string name, string argDisplay, double value)
    {
        switch (name)
        {
            case "%":   return "(" + argDisplay + "%)";
            case "!":   return "(" + argDisplay + "!)";
            case "1/x": return "(1/(" + argDisplay + "))";
            default:    return name + "(" + argDisplay + ")";
        }
    }

    private static bool TryEvaluateFunction(string name, double arg, out double result)
    {
        string expr;
        switch (name)
        {
            case "%":   expr = "(" + arg.ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "%)"; break;
            case "!":   expr = "(" + arg.ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "!)"; break;
            case "1/x": expr = "(1/(" + arg.ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "))"; break;
            default:    expr = name + "(" + arg.ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + ")"; break;
        }

        var empty = new Dictionary<char, double>();
        if (MathEquationSolver.TryEvaluateNumericExpression(expr, empty, out result))
            return true;

        if (MathEquationSolver.TryEvaluateNumericExpressionComplex(expr, empty, out var c) &&
            System.Math.Abs(c.Imaginary) < 1e-10)
        {
            result = c.Real;
            return true;
        }

        result = 0;
        return false;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Rendering (used only for display and solver input)
    // ============================================================

    private static string RenderEquation(ExprNode left, ExprNode right)
    {
        return Render(left) + "=" + Render(right);
    }

    public static string Render(ExprNode node)
    {
        switch (node)
        {
            case NumberNode n:
                return n.display.Contains("/") ? "(" + n.display + ")" : n.display;
            case VarNode v:
                return v.name.ToString();
            case UnknownNode _:
                return MathEquationSolver.UnknownSymbol.ToString();
            case BracketNode b:
                return b.isOpen ? "(" : ")";
            case UnaryNode u:
                return u.op + Render(u.operand);
            case OpNode o:
                return RenderOp(o);
            case FuncNode f:
                return RenderFunc(f);
            default:
                return string.Empty;
        }
    }

    private static string RenderOp(OpNode o)
    {
        string left = Render(o.left);
        string right = Render(o.right);
        string inner = left + o.op + right;

        // Wrap non-trivial children in parens so re-parsing preserves shape.
        return "(" + inner + ")";
    }

    private static string RenderFunc(FuncNode f)
    {
        string arg = f.arg != null ? Render(f.arg) : "";
        switch (f.name)
        {
            case "%":   return "(" + arg + "%)";
            case "!":   return "(" + arg + "!)";
            case "1/x": return "(1/(" + arg + "))";
            default:    return f.name + "(" + arg + ")";
        }
    }


    // ============================================================
    // Variable definitions (unchanged externally; uses rendered strings)
    // ============================================================

        public static Dictionary<char, double> BuildVariableDefinitions(
        List<ConnectedEquation> equations)
    {
        // Variables we've decided can never be resolved because two
        // equations propose different values for them.
        var conflicts = new HashSet<char>();

        Dictionary<char, double> definitions = new Dictionary<char, double>();
        bool foundDefinition;
        do
        {
            Dictionary<char, double> candidates = new Dictionary<char, double>();
            HashSet<char> conflictingCandidates = new HashSet<char>();

            foreach (ConnectedEquation equation in equations)
            {
                if (HasAmbiguousOperands(equation)) continue;
                if (!TryGetCompleteEquation(equation, out string completeEquation)) continue;

                HashSet<char> variables = GetVariables(completeEquation);
                foreach (char variable in variables)
                {
                    if (definitions.ContainsKey(variable) ||
                        conflicts.Contains(variable)) continue;

                    string candidateEquation = ReplaceVariableWithUnknown(completeEquation, variable);
                    if (!TrySolveEqualityChain(candidateEquation, definitions, out string result))
                        continue;
                    if (!double.TryParse(result, NumberStyles.Float,
                            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double value))
                        continue;

                    if (candidates.TryGetValue(variable, out double existing) &&
                        !ValuesAreEquivalent(existing, value))
                    {
                        conflictingCandidates.Add(variable);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        candidates[variable] = value;
                    }
                }
            }

            foundDefinition = false;
            foreach (KeyValuePair<char, double> candidate in candidates)
            {
                if (conflictingCandidates.Contains(candidate.Key))
                {
                    conflicts.Add(candidate.Key);
                    continue;
                }
                definitions.Add(candidate.Key, candidate.Value);
                foundDefinition = true;
            }
        }
        while (foundDefinition);

        return definitions;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Solver hooks (unchanged externally)
    // ============================================================

    public static void TrySolveEquation(
        ConnectedEquation equation,
        Dictionary<char, double> definitions,
        HashSet<BallNumber> updatedAnswers)
    {
        if (HasAmbiguousOperands(equation)) return;

        BallNumber emptyBall = null;
        foreach (BallNumber ball in equation.balls)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ball.GetMathContent().Trim()))
            {
                if (ball.IsLocked || ball.IsBeingEdited || emptyBall != null) return;
                emptyBall = ball;
            }
        }
        if (emptyBall == null) return;

        if (TrySolveEqualityChain(equation.expression, definitions, out string result))
        {
            emptyBall.SetSolverOutput(result);
            updatedAnswers.Add(emptyBall);
        }
    }


    // ============================================================
    // Helpers reused from the old implementation
    // ============================================================

    private static bool HasAmbiguousOperands(ConnectedEquation equation)
    {
        return equation.ambiguousBalls != null && equation.ambiguousBalls.Count > 0;
    }

    private static bool TryGetCompleteEquation(ConnectedEquation eq, out string equation)
    {
        equation = string.Empty;
        foreach (BallNumber ball in eq.balls)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(ball.GetMathContent().Trim()) || ball.IsBeingEdited)
                return false;
        }
        equation = eq.expression;
        return TrySplitEqualityChain(equation, out _);
    }

    private static bool TrySolveEqualityChain(
        string equation,
        Dictionary<char, double> definitions,
        out string result)
    {
        result = string.Empty;
        if (!TrySplitEqualityChain(equation, out string[] parts)) return false;

        int unknownPartIndex = -1;
        var knownPartIndexes = new List<int>();
        var knownValues = new List<System.Numerics.Complex>();

        for (int i = 0; i < parts.Length; i++)
        {
            if (parts[i].IndexOf(MathEquationSolver.UnknownSymbol) >= 0)
            {
                if (unknownPartIndex >= 0) return false;
                unknownPartIndex = i;
                continue;
            }

            knownPartIndexes.Add(i);
            if (MathEquationSolver.TryEvaluateNumericExpressionComplex(
                    parts[i], definitions, out System.Numerics.Complex value))
            {
                knownValues.Add(value);
            }
            else if (parts.Length > 2)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if (unknownPartIndex < 0 || knownPartIndexes.Count == 0 ||
            knownValues.Count > 0 && knownValues.Count != knownPartIndexes.Count)
            return false;

        for (int i = 1; i < knownValues.Count; i++)
            if (!ValuesAreEquivalent(knownValues[0], knownValues[i]))
                return false;

        string knownPart = parts[knownPartIndexes[0]];
        string unknownPart = parts[unknownPartIndex];

        if (MathEquationSolver.TrySolve(unknownPart + "=" + knownPart,
                definitions, out result))
            return true;

        return parts.Length == 2 &&
               unknownPart == MathEquationSolver.UnknownSymbol.ToString() &&
               TrySimplifyWithDefinitions(knownPart, definitions, out result);
    }

    private static bool TrySimplifyWithDefinitions(
        string expression, Dictionary<char, double> definitions, out string simplified)
    {
        string substituted = MathEquationSolver.SubstituteVariables(expression, definitions);
        if (MathExpression.TrySimplifyAddition(substituted, out simplified)) return true;
        simplified = substituted;
        return false;
    }

    private static bool TrySplitEqualityChain(string equation, out string[] parts)
    {
        parts = equation.Split('=');
        if (parts.Length < 2) return false;
        for (int i = 0; i < parts.Length; i++)
        {
            parts[i] = parts[i].Trim();
            if (parts[i].Length == 0) return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    private static HashSet<char> GetVariables(string equation)
    {
        return MathEquationSolver.GetVariables(equation);
    }

    private static string ReplaceVariableWithUnknown(string equation, char variable)
    {
        return MathEquationSolver.ReplaceVariableWithUnknown(equation, variable);
    }

    private static bool ValuesAreEquivalent(double l, double r)
    {
        double t = 1e-9 * System.Math.Max(1d, System.Math.Max(System.Math.Abs(l), System.Math.Abs(r)));
        return System.Math.Abs(l - r) <= t;
    }

    private static bool ValuesAreEquivalent(System.Numerics.Complex l, System.Numerics.Complex r)
    {
        return (l - r).Magnitude <=
               1e-9 * System.Math.Max(1d, System.Math.Max(l.Magnitude, r.Magnitude));
    }

    private static bool TryGetVariableLetter(string expression, out char variable)
    {
        variable = '\0';
        if (expression.Length != 1 || !MathSyntax.IsVariableLetter(expression[0])) return false;
        variable = expression[0];
        return true;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\MathEquationSolver.cs =====
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Numerics;
using System.Text;

// Parses and evaluates equation text, including implicit multiplication and functions.
public static class MathEquationSolver
{
    public const char UnknownSymbol = '\u0001';
    public const char ImplicitMultiplicationSymbol = '\u0002';

    private enum NodeKind
    {
        Number,
        Variable,
        Unknown,
        Unary,
        Binary,
        Function,
        Postfix,
        Limit
    }

    private sealed class Node
    {
        public NodeKind kind;
        public string operation;
        public Complex number;
        public char variable;
        public Node left;
        public Node right;
    }

    private sealed class Parser
    {
        private readonly string expression;
        private int index;

        public Parser(string expression)
        {
            this.expression = expression;
        }

        public bool TryParse(out Node left, out Node right)
        {
            left = null;
            right = null;
            if (!TryParseAdditive(out left))
            {
                return false;
            }

            SkipWhitespace();
            if (TryConsume('='))
            {
                if (!TryParseAdditive(out right))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                SkipWhitespace();
                if (index < expression.Length && expression[index] == '=')
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }

            SkipWhitespace();
            return index == expression.Length;
        }

        private bool TryParseAdditive(out Node result)
        {
            if (!TryParseMultiplicative(out result))
            {
                return false;
            }

            while (true)
            {
                SkipWhitespace();
                if (!TryConsume('+') && !TryConsume('-'))
                {
                    return true;
                }

                string operation = expression[index - 1].ToString();
                if (!TryParseMultiplicative(out Node right))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Binary, operation, result, right);
            }
        }

        private bool TryParseMultiplicative(out Node result)
        {
            if (!TryParseUnary(out result))
            {
                return false;
            }

            while (true)
            {
                SkipWhitespace();
                if (TryConsume('*') || TryConsume('\u00d7') ||
                    TryConsume(ImplicitMultiplicationSymbol))
                {
                    if (!TryParseUnary(out Node right))
                    {
                        return false;
                    }

                    result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Binary, "*", result, right);
                }
                else if (TryConsume('/') || TryConsume('\u00f7'))
                {
                    if (!TryParseUnary(out Node right))
                    {
                        return false;
                    }

                    result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Binary, "/", result, right);
                }
                else if (StartsPrimary())
                {
                    if (!TryParseUnary(out Node right))
                    {
                        return false;
                    }

                    result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Binary, "*", result, right);
                }
                else
                {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        private bool TryParseUnary(out Node result)
        {
            SkipWhitespace();
            if (TryConsume('+') || TryConsume('-'))
            {
                string operation = expression[index - 1].ToString();

                // Accept one leading sign where an operand is expected, such as
                // 4 + -4 or 4 * -4, but reject repeated signs such as + - + 4.
                // Parsing the power here still allows signed exponents (for example 2^-3).
                if (!TryParsePower(out Node operand))
                {
                    result = null;
                    return false;
                }

                result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Unary, operation, operand, null);
                return true;
            }

            return TryParsePower(out result);
        }

        private bool TryParsePower(out Node result)
        {
            if (!TryParsePostfix(out result))
            {
                return false;
            }

            SkipWhitespace();
            if (TryConsume('^'))
            {
                if (!TryParseUnary(out Node exponent))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Binary, "^", result, exponent);
            }

            return true;
        }

        private bool TryParsePostfix(out Node result)
        {
            if (!TryParsePrimary(out result))
            {
                return false;
            }

            while (true)
            {
                SkipWhitespace();
                if (TryConsume('!'))
                {
                    result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Postfix, "!", result, null);
                }
                else if (TryConsume('%'))
                {
                    result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Postfix, "%", result, null);
                }
                else
                {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        private bool TryParsePrimary(out Node result)
        {
            SkipWhitespace();
            result = null;
            if (index >= expression.Length)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (expression[index] == UnknownSymbol)
            {
                index++;
                result = new Node { kind = NodeKind.Unknown };
                return true;
            }

            if (expression[index] == '(')
            {
                index++;
                if (!TryParseAdditive(out result))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                SkipWhitespace();
                return TryConsume(')');
            }

            if (char.IsLetter(expression[index]))
            {
                int start = index;
                while (index < expression.Length && char.IsLetter(expression[index]))
                {
                    index++;
                }

                string identifier = expression.Substring(start, index - start).ToLowerInvariant();
                if (identifier == "pi")
                {
                    result = new Node { kind = NodeKind.Number, number = new Complex(Math.PI, 0d) };
                    return true;
                }

                if (identifier == "e")
                {
                    result = new Node { kind = NodeKind.Number, number = new Complex(Math.E, 0d) };
                    return true;
                }

                if (identifier == "i")
                {
                    result = new Node { kind = NodeKind.Number, number = Complex.ImaginaryOne };
                    return true;
                }

                if (IsFunction(identifier))
                {
                    return TryParseFunction(identifier, out result);
                }

                if (identifier.Length == 1)
                {
                    result = new Node { kind = NodeKind.Variable, variable = expression[start] };
                    return true;
                }

                return false;
            }

            int numberStart = index;
            bool hasDigit = false;
            while (index < expression.Length && char.IsDigit(expression[index]))
            {
                hasDigit = true;
                index++;
            }

            if (index < expression.Length && expression[index] == '.')
            {
                index++;
                while (index < expression.Length && char.IsDigit(expression[index]))
                {
                    hasDigit = true;
                    index++;
                }
            }

            if (!hasDigit ||
                !double.TryParse(
                    expression.Substring(numberStart, index - numberStart),
                    NumberStyles.AllowDecimalPoint,
                    CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    out double number))
            {
                index = numberStart;
                return false;
            }

            result = new Node { kind = NodeKind.Number, number = new Complex(number, 0d) };
            return true;
        }

        private bool TryParseFunction(string name, out Node result)
        {
            result = null;
            SkipWhitespace();
            if (name == "lim")
            {
                if (!TryConsume('(') ||
                    !TryParseAdditive(out Node body))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                SkipWhitespace();
                if (!TryConsume(','))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                SkipWhitespace();
                if (index >= expression.Length || !char.IsLetter(expression[index]))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                char variable = expression[index++];
                SkipWhitespace();
                if (!TryConsume('-') || !TryConsume('>') ||
                    !TryParseAdditive(out Node point))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                SkipWhitespace();
                if (!TryConsume(')'))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Limit, variable.ToString(), body, point);
                return true;
            }

            if (TryConsume('('))
            {
                if (!TryParseAdditive(out Node argument))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                SkipWhitespace();
                if (!TryConsume(')'))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Function, name, argument, null);
                return true;
            }

            if (!TryParseUnary(out Node firstArgument))
            {
                return false;
            }

            Node combinedArgument = firstArgument;
            while (true)
            {
                SkipWhitespace();
                if (TryConsume(ImplicitMultiplicationSymbol))
                {
                    if (!TryParsePostfix(out Node markedAdjacentArgument))
                    {
                        return false;
                    }

                    combinedArgument = CreateNode(
                        NodeKind.Binary,
                        "*",
                        combinedArgument,
                        markedAdjacentArgument);
                    continue;
                }

                if (!StartsPrimary())
                {
                    break;
                }

                if (!TryParsePostfix(out Node adjacentArgument))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                combinedArgument = CreateNode(
                    NodeKind.Binary,
                    "*",
                    combinedArgument,
                    adjacentArgument);
            }

            result = CreateNode(NodeKind.Function, name, combinedArgument, null);
            return true;
        }

        private bool StartsPrimary()
        {
            return index < expression.Length &&
                   (expression[index] == '(' ||
                    expression[index] == UnknownSymbol ||
                    char.IsDigit(expression[index]) ||
                    char.IsLetter(expression[index]) ||
                    expression[index] == '.');
        }

        private bool TryConsume(char character)
        {
            if (index >= expression.Length || expression[index] != character)
            {
                return false;
            }

            index++;
            return true;
        }

        private void SkipWhitespace()
        {
            while (index < expression.Length && char.IsWhiteSpace(expression[index]))
            {
                index++;
            }
        }
    }

    public static bool TryValidateExpression(string expression)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(expression))
        {
            return true;
        }

        return new Parser(expression).TryParse(out _, out _);
    }

    public static HashSet<char> GetVariables(string expression)
    {
        HashSet<char> variables = new HashSet<char>();
        for (int index = 0; index < expression.Length;)
        {
            if (!char.IsLetter(expression[index]))
            {
                index++;
                continue;
            }

            int start = index++;
            while (index < expression.Length && char.IsLetter(expression[index]))
            {
                index++;
            }

            string identifier = expression.Substring(start, index - start);
            if (identifier.Length == 1 && !IsReservedIdentifier(identifier))
            {
                variables.Add(identifier[0]);
            }
        }

        return variables;
    }

    public static string ReplaceVariableWithUnknown(string expression, char variable)
    {
        return TransformIdentifiers(expression, identifier =>
            identifier.Length == 1 && identifier[0] == variable
                ? UnknownSymbol.ToString()
                : identifier);
    }

    public static string SubstituteVariables(
        string expression,
        IDictionary<char, double> variables)
    {
        if (variables == null)
        {
            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(variables));
        }

        return TransformIdentifiers(expression, identifier =>
        {
            if (identifier.Length != 1 ||
                !variables.TryGetValue(identifier[0], out double value))
            {
                return identifier;
            }

            return "(" + value.ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + ")";
        });
    }

    private static string TransformIdentifiers(string expression, Func<string, string> transform)
    {
        StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder(expression.Length);
        for (int index = 0; index < expression.Length;)
        {
            if (!char.IsLetter(expression[index]))
            {
                result.Append(expression[index++]);
                continue;
            }

            int start = index++;
            while (index < expression.Length && char.IsLetter(expression[index]))
            {
                index++;
            }

            result.Append(transform(expression.Substring(start, index - start)));
        }

        return result.ToString();
    }

    private static bool IsReservedIdentifier(string identifier)
    {
        string name = identifier.ToLowerInvariant();
        return name == "pi" || name == "e" || name == "i" || IsFunction(name);
    }

    private static bool IsFunction(string name)
    {
        switch (name)
        {
            case "root":
            case "log":
            case "factorial":
            case "sin":
            case "cos":
            case "tan":
            case "asin":
            case "acos":
            case "atan":
            case "lim":
                return true;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }

    public static bool TrySolve(string equation, out string result)
    {
        return TrySolve(equation, new Dictionary<char, double>(), out result);
    }

    public static bool TrySolve(
        string equation,
        IDictionary<char, double> variables,
        out string result)
    {
        result = string.Empty;
        if (variables == null)
        {
            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(variables));
        }

        if (!new Parser(equation).TryParse(out Node left, out Node right) ||
            right == null ||
            ContainsUnknown(left) == ContainsUnknown(right))
        {
            return false;
        }

        Node unknownExpression = ContainsUnknown(left) ? left : right;
        Node knownExpression = ContainsUnknown(left) ? right : left;
        if (!TryEvaluate(knownExpression, Complex.Zero, ToComplexVariables(variables), out Complex knownValue) ||
            !TrySolveNode(unknownExpression, knownValue, ToComplexVariables(variables), out Complex unknownValue))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (!TryEvaluate(left, unknownValue, ToComplexVariables(variables), out Complex leftValue) ||
            !TryEvaluate(right, unknownValue, ToComplexVariables(variables), out Complex rightValue) ||
            !ValuesAreEquivalent(leftValue, rightValue))
        {
            return false;
        }

        result = FormatComplex(unknownValue);
        return true;
    }

    public static bool TryEvaluateNumericExpression(
        string expression,
        IDictionary<char, double> variables,
        out double value)
    {
        value = 0d;
        if (!TryEvaluateNumericExpressionComplex(expression, variables, out Complex result) ||
            Math.Abs(result.Imaginary) > 1e-10 * Math.Max(1d, Math.Abs(result.Real)))
        {
            return false;
        }

        value = result.Real;
        return IsFinite(value);
    }

    public static bool TryEvaluateNumericExpressionComplex(
        string expression,
        IDictionary<char, double> variables,
        out Complex value)
    {
        value = Complex.Zero;
        if (variables == null)
        {
            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(variables));
        }

        if (!new Parser(expression).TryParse(out Node parsedExpression, out Node right) || right != null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        return !ContainsUnknown(parsedExpression) &&
               TryEvaluate(parsedExpression, Complex.Zero, ToComplexVariables(variables), out value);
    }

    private static Dictionary<char, Complex> ToComplexVariables(IDictionary<char, double> variables)
    {
        Dictionary<char, Complex> converted = new Dictionary<char, Complex>();
        foreach (KeyValuePair<char, double> variable in variables)
        {
            converted.Add(variable.Key, new Complex(variable.Value, 0d));
        }

        return converted;
    }

    private static bool TrySolveNode(
        Node node,
        Complex target,
        IDictionary<char, Complex> variables,
        out Complex value)
    {
        value = Complex.Zero;
        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Unknown)
        {
            value = target;
            return IsFinite(value);
        }

        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Unary)
        {
            return TrySolveNode(
                node.left,
                node.operation == "-" ? -target : target,
                variables,
                out value);
        }

        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Function)
        {
            if (!ContainsUnknown(node.left) ||
                !TryInvertFunction(node.operation, target, out Complex inverse))
            {
                return false;
            }

            return TrySolveNode(node.left, inverse, variables, out value);
        }

        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Postfix)
        {
            if (node.operation != "%")
            {
                return false;
            }

            return TrySolveNode(node.left, target * 100d, variables, out value);
        }

        if (node.kind != NodeKind.Binary)
        {
            return false;
        }

        bool unknownOnLeft = ContainsUnknown(node.left);
        Node knownNode = unknownOnLeft ? node.right : node.left;
        if (knownNode == null || ContainsUnknown(knownNode) ||
            !TryEvaluate(knownNode, Complex.Zero, variables, out Complex knownValue))
        {
            return false;
        }

        Complex nextTarget;
        switch (node.operation)
        {
            case "+":
                nextTarget = target - knownValue;
                break;
            case "-":
                nextTarget = unknownOnLeft ? target + knownValue : knownValue - target;
                break;
            case "*":
                if (knownValue == Complex.Zero)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                nextTarget = target / knownValue;
                break;
            case "/":
                if (unknownOnLeft)
                {
                    nextTarget = target * knownValue;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (target == Complex.Zero)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }

                    nextTarget = knownValue / target;
                }

                break;
            case "^":
                if (unknownOnLeft)
                {
                    if (knownValue == Complex.Zero)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }

                    nextTarget = Complex.Pow(target, Complex.One / knownValue);
                }
                else
                {
                    if (target == Complex.Zero || knownValue == Complex.Zero ||
                        Complex.Log(knownValue) == Complex.Zero)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }

                    nextTarget = Complex.Log(target) / Complex.Log(knownValue);
                }

                break;
            default:
                return false;
        }

        return IsFinite(nextTarget) && TrySolveNode(
            unknownOnLeft ? node.left : node.right,
            nextTarget,
            variables,
            out value);
    }

    private static bool TryInvertFunction(string function, Complex target, out Complex inverse)
    {
        switch (function)
        {
            case "root":
                inverse = target * target;
                return true;
            case "log":
                inverse = Complex.Pow(new Complex(10d, 0d), target);
                return true;
            case "sin":
                inverse = Complex.Asin(target);
                return true;
            case "cos":
                inverse = Complex.Acos(target);
                return true;
            case "tan":
                inverse = Complex.Atan(target);
                return true;
            case "asin":
                inverse = Complex.Sin(target);
                return true;
            case "acos":
                inverse = Complex.Cos(target);
                return true;
            case "atan":
                inverse = Complex.Tan(target);
                return true;
            default:
                inverse = Complex.Zero;
                return false;
        }
    }

    private static bool TryEvaluate(
        Node node,
        Complex unknownValue,
        IDictionary<char, Complex> variables,
        out Complex value)
    {
        value = Complex.Zero;
        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Number)
        {
            value = node.number;
            return true;
        }

        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Unknown)
        {
            value = unknownValue;
            return true;
        }

        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Variable)
        {
            return variables.TryGetValue(node.variable, out value) && IsFinite(value);
        }

        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Limit)
        {
            return TryEvaluateLimit(node, unknownValue, variables, out value);
        }

        if (!TryEvaluate(node.left, unknownValue, variables, out Complex leftValue))
        {
            return false;
        }

        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Unary)
        {
            value = node.operation == "-" ? -leftValue : leftValue;
            return IsFinite(value);
        }

        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Postfix)
        {
            if (node.operation == "%")
            {
                value = leftValue / 100d;
                return IsFinite(value);
            }

            if (node.operation == "!" &&
                Math.Abs(leftValue.Imaginary) < 1e-12 &&
                leftValue.Real >= 0d &&
                leftValue.Real <= 170d &&
                Math.Floor(leftValue.Real) == leftValue.Real)
            {
                double factorial = 1d;
                for (int factor = 2; factor <= (int)leftValue.Real; factor++)
                {
                    factorial *= factor;
                }

                value = new Complex(factorial, 0d);
                return true;
            }

            return false;
        }

        if (node.kind == NodeKind.Function)
        {
            return TryEvaluateFunction(node.operation, leftValue, out value);
        }

        if (!TryEvaluate(node.right, unknownValue, variables, out Complex rightValue))
        {
            return false;
        }

        switch (node.operation)
        {
            case "+":
                value = leftValue + rightValue;
                break;
            case "-":
                value = leftValue - rightValue;
                break;
            case "*":
                value = leftValue * rightValue;
                break;
            case "/":
                if (rightValue == Complex.Zero)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                value = leftValue / rightValue;
                break;
            case "^":
                value = Complex.Pow(leftValue, rightValue);
                break;
            default:
                return false;
        }

        return IsFinite(value);
    }

    private static bool TryEvaluateFunction(string function, Complex argument, out Complex value)
    {
        switch (function)
        {
            case "factorial":
                if (Math.Abs(argument.Imaginary) >= 1e-12 ||
                    argument.Real < 0d ||
                    argument.Real > 170d ||
                    Math.Floor(argument.Real) != argument.Real)
                {
                    value = Complex.Zero;
                    return false;
                }

                double factorial = 1d;
                for (int factor = 2; factor <= (int)argument.Real; factor++)
                {
                    factorial *= factor;
                }

                value = new Complex(factorial, 0d);
                return true;
            case "root":
                value = Complex.Sqrt(argument);
                break;
            case "log":
                value = Complex.Log(argument) / Math.Log(10d);
                break;
            case "sin":
                value = Complex.Sin(argument);
                break;
            case "cos":
                value = Complex.Cos(argument);
                break;
            case "tan":
                value = Complex.Tan(argument);
                break;
            case "asin":
                value = Complex.Asin(argument);
                break;
            case "acos":
                value = Complex.Acos(argument);
                break;
            case "atan":
                value = Complex.Atan(argument);
                break;
            default:
                value = Complex.Zero;
                return false;
        }

        return IsFinite(value);
    }

    private static bool TryEvaluateLimit(
        Node node,
        Complex unknownValue,
        IDictionary<char, Complex> variables,
        out Complex value)
    {
        value = Complex.Zero;
        if (!TryEvaluate(node.right, unknownValue, variables, out Complex point) ||
            Math.Abs(point.Imaginary) > 1e-12)
        {
            return false;
        }

        char variable = node.operation[0];
        Complex? previousAverage = null;
        double initialStep = Math.Max(1d, Math.Abs(point.Real)) * 0.1d;
        for (int iteration = 0; iteration < 9; iteration++)
        {
            double step = initialStep * Math.Pow(0.1d, iteration);
            Dictionary<char, Complex> leftVariables = new Dictionary<char, Complex>(variables)
            {
                [variable] = new Complex(point.Real - step, 0d)
            };
            Dictionary<char, Complex> rightVariables = new Dictionary<char, Complex>(variables)
            {
                [variable] = new Complex(point.Real + step, 0d)
            };
            if (!TryEvaluate(node.left, unknownValue, leftVariables, out Complex leftValue) ||
                !TryEvaluate(node.left, unknownValue, rightVariables, out Complex rightValue))
            {
                continue;
            }

            Complex average = (leftValue + rightValue) / 2d;
            double scale = Math.Max(1d, Math.Max(leftValue.Magnitude, rightValue.Magnitude));
            bool sidesAgree = (leftValue - rightValue).Magnitude <= 1e-5d * scale;
            bool stable = previousAverage.HasValue &&
                (average - previousAverage.Value).Magnitude <= 1e-5d * Math.Max(1d, average.Magnitude);
            if (sidesAgree && stable && IsFinite(average))
            {
                value = average;
                return true;
            }

            previousAverage = average;
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static bool ContainsUnknown(Node node)
    {
        return node != null &&
               (node.kind == NodeKind.Unknown ||
                ContainsUnknown(node.left) ||
                ContainsUnknown(node.right));
    }

    private static Node CreateNode(NodeKind kind, string operation, Node left, Node right)
    {
        return new Node
        {
            kind = kind,
            operation = operation,
            left = left,
            right = right
        };
    }

    private static bool ValuesAreEquivalent(Complex left, Complex right)
    {
        return (left - right).Magnitude <=
               1e-9d * Math.Max(1d, Math.Max(left.Magnitude, right.Magnitude));
    }

    private static bool IsFinite(Complex value)
    {
        return IsFinite(value.Real) && IsFinite(value.Imaginary);
    }

    private static bool IsFinite(double value)
    {
        return !double.IsNaN(value) && !double.IsInfinity(value);
    }

    private static string FormatComplex(Complex value)
    {
        if (Math.Abs(value.Imaginary) < 1e-12)
        {
            return value.Real.ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        }

        if (Math.Abs(value.Real) < 1e-12)
        {
            if (Math.Abs(value.Imaginary - 1d) < 1e-12)
            {
                return "i";
            }

            if (Math.Abs(value.Imaginary + 1d) < 1e-12)
            {
                return "-i";
            }

            return value.Imaginary.ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "i";
        }

        string sign = value.Imaginary < 0d ? "-" : "+";
        return value.Real.ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) +
               sign +
               Math.Abs(value.Imaginary).ToString("G", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) +
               "i";
    }
}


// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\MathExpression.cs =====
// Categories used to select a ball's color and detect invalid-looking content.
public enum MathContentKind
{
    // No content has been entered.
    Empty,
    // Ordinary numeric text or a symbol that is not an operator.
    NumberOrSymbol,
    // Text containing one or more variables but no separate number.
    Algebra,
    // Text containing both variables and separate numeric values.
    Mixed,
    // A standalone arithmetic/equality operator.
    Operator,
    // Text made of math characters that fails the syntax check.
    InvalidMath,
    // Text containing characters outside the recognized math syntax.
    Other
}

// Classifies mathematical text and forwards syntax/simplification requests.
public static class MathExpression
{
    // Distinguish numbers, variables, operators, mixed content, and other text.
    public static MathContentKind Classify(string content)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
        {
            return MathContentKind.Empty;
        }

        bool hasVariable = false;
        bool hasStandaloneNumber = false;
        bool hasMathSymbol = false;
        bool hasOtherCharacter = false;

        for (int i = 0; i < content.Length; i++)
        {
            char character = content[i];
            if (char.IsLetter(character))
            {
                int identifierStart = i;
                while (i + 1 < content.Length && char.IsLetter(content[i + 1]))
                {
                    i++;
                }

                string identifier = content.Substring(identifierStart, i - identifierStart + 1);
                if (MathSyntax.IsFunctionName(identifier))
                {
                    hasMathSymbol = true;
                }
                else if (MathSyntax.IsConstantName(identifier))
                {
                    hasStandaloneNumber = true;
                }
                else
                {
                    hasVariable = true;
                }
            }
            else if (char.IsDigit(character))
            {
                int numberStart = i;
                while (i + 1 < content.Length && char.IsDigit(content[i + 1]))
                {
                    i++;
                }

                bool nextToVariable =
                    numberStart > 0 && MathSyntax.IsVariableLetter(content[numberStart - 1]) ||
                    i + 1 < content.Length && MathSyntax.IsVariableLetter(content[i + 1]);
                bool isExponent = numberStart > 1 &&
                    content[numberStart - 1] == '^' &&
                    MathSyntax.IsVariableLetter(content[numberStart - 2]);
                if (!nextToVariable && !isExponent)
                {
                    hasStandaloneNumber = true;
                }
            }
            else if (character == '.' &&
                     i > 0 && char.IsDigit(content[i - 1]) &&
                     i + 1 < content.Length && char.IsDigit(content[i + 1]))
            {
                continue;
            }
            else if ((character == '+' || character == '-') &&
                     IsLeadingNumericSign(content, i))
            {
                continue;
            }
            else if (MathSyntax.IsMathSymbol(character))
            {
                hasMathSymbol = true;
            }
            else if (!char.IsWhiteSpace(character))
            {
                hasOtherCharacter = true;
            }
        }

        if (hasOtherCharacter)
        {
            return MathContentKind.Other;
        }

        if (hasVariable && hasStandaloneNumber)
        {
            return MathContentKind.Mixed;
        }

        if (hasVariable)
        {
            return MathContentKind.Algebra;
        }

        return hasMathSymbol ? MathContentKind.Operator : MathContentKind.NumberOrSymbol;
    }

    // A leading sign belongs to a numeric literal, not to a standalone operator.
    private static bool IsLeadingNumericSign(string content, int signIndex)
    {
        for (int index = 0; index < signIndex; index++)
        {
            if (!char.IsWhiteSpace(content[index]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        int nextIndex = signIndex + 1;
        while (nextIndex < content.Length && char.IsWhiteSpace(content[nextIndex]))
        {
            nextIndex++;
        }

        return nextIndex < content.Length && char.IsDigit(content[nextIndex]);
    }

    // Report whether any recognized mathematical character occurs in the string.
    public static bool IsMathematicalSyntaxContent(string content)
    {
        return MathSyntax.IsMathematicalSyntaxContent(content);
    }

    // Validate the expression grammar used by typed mathematical content.
    public static bool HasValidSyntax(string content)
    {
        return MathSyntax.HasValidSyntax(content);
    }

    // Simplify like terms when this text is a supported addition-only expression.
    public static bool TrySimplifyAddition(string expression, out string simplified)
    {
        return MathSimplifier.TrySimplifyAddition(expression, out simplified);
    }
}


// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\MathSimplifier.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;

// Combines like terms in simple addition/subtraction expressions without evaluation.
public static class MathSimplifier
{
    // A term is a coefficient times either a constant or one variable power.
    private struct Term
    {
        public char variable;
        public int power;
        public int coefficient;
    }

    // Parse a sum of terms, combine equal variable/power pairs, and format the result.
    public static bool TrySimplifyAddition(string expression, out string simplified)
    {
        simplified = expression;
        List<Term> terms = new List<Term>();
        int index = 0;
        bool firstTerm = true;

        while (true)
        {
            SkipWhitespace(expression, ref index);
            if (index >= expression.Length)
            {
                break;
            }

            int sign = 1;
            bool foundSign = false;
            while (index < expression.Length &&
                (expression[index] == '+' || expression[index] == '-'))
            {
                if (expression[index] == '-')
                {
                    sign *= -1;
                }

                foundSign = true;
                index++;
                SkipWhitespace(expression, ref index);
            }

            if (!firstTerm && !foundSign)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (!TryParseTerm(expression, ref index, sign, out Term term))
            {
                return false;
            }

            int matchingTerm = terms.FindIndex(existing =>
                existing.variable == term.variable && existing.power == term.power);
            if (matchingTerm >= 0)
            {
                Term existing = terms[matchingTerm];
                long combinedCoefficient = (long)existing.coefficient + term.coefficient;
                if (combinedCoefficient > int.MaxValue || combinedCoefficient < int.MinValue)
                {
                    return false;
                }

                existing.coefficient = (int)combinedCoefficient;
                terms[matchingTerm] = existing;
            }
            else
            {
                terms.Add(term);
            }

            firstTerm = false;
        }

        if (firstTerm)
        {
            return false;
        }

        StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder();
        foreach (Term term in terms)
        {
            if (term.coefficient == 0)
            {
                continue;
            }

            bool negative = term.coefficient < 0;
            long absoluteCoefficient = System.Math.Abs((long)term.coefficient);
            if (result.Length > 0)
            {
                result.Append(negative ? "-" : "+");
            }
            else if (negative)
            {
                result.Append("-");
            }

            if (term.variable == '\0' || absoluteCoefficient != 1)
            {
                result.Append(absoluteCoefficient.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
            }

            if (term.variable != '\0')
            {
                result.Append(term.variable);
                if (term.power > 1)
                {
                    result.Append("^");
                    result.Append(term.power.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
                }
            }
        }

        simplified = result.Length == 0 ? "0" : result.ToString();
        return true;
    }

    // Parse one signed coefficient, optional variable, and optional exponent.
    private static bool TryParseTerm(string expression, ref int index, int sign, out Term term)
    {
        term = new Term();
        int numberStart = index;
        while (index < expression.Length && char.IsDigit(expression[index]))
        {
            index++;
        }

        int coefficient = 1;
        if (index > numberStart &&
            !int.TryParse(
                expression.Substring(numberStart, index - numberStart),
                NumberStyles.None,
                CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                out coefficient))
        {
            return false;
        }

        SkipWhitespace(expression, ref index);
        char variable = '\0';
        int power = 0;
        if (index < expression.Length && MathSyntax.IsVariableLetter(expression[index]))
        {
            variable = expression[index];
            power = 1;
            index++;
            SkipWhitespace(expression, ref index);

            if (index < expression.Length && expression[index] == '^')
            {
                index++;
                SkipWhitespace(expression, ref index);
                int powerStart = index;
                while (index < expression.Length && char.IsDigit(expression[index]))
                {
                    index++;
                }

                if (powerStart == index ||
                    !int.TryParse(
                        expression.Substring(powerStart, index - powerStart),
                        NumberStyles.None,
                        CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                        out power))
                {
                    return false;
                }

                if (power == 0)
                {
                    variable = '\0';
                }
            }
        }
        else if (numberStart == index)
        {
            return false;
        }

        long signedCoefficient = (long)coefficient * sign;
        if (signedCoefficient > int.MaxValue || signedCoefficient < int.MinValue)
        {
            return false;
        }

        term.variable = variable;
        term.power = variable == '\0' ? 0 : power;
        term.coefficient = (int)signedCoefficient;
        return true;
    }

    // Skip spaces while advancing through the input expression.
    private static void SkipWhitespace(string expression, ref int index)
    {
        while (index < expression.Length && char.IsWhiteSpace(expression[index]))
        {
            index++;
        }
    }
}


// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\MathSyntax.cs =====
// Shared character tests and syntax validation for mathematical input text.
public static class MathSyntax
{
    // Variables are currently single English letters, upper- or lowercase.
    public static bool IsVariableLetter(char character)
    {
        return character >= 'a' && character <= 'z' ||
               character >= 'A' && character <= 'Z';
    }

    // Return whether a character is one of the symbols recognized by the UI/parser.
    public static bool IsMathSymbol(char character)
    {
        return character == '+' ||
               character == '-' ||
               character == '*' || character == '/' ||
               character == '\u00d7' || character == '\u00f7' ||
               character == '=' || character == '^' ||
               character == '\u2205' ||
               character == '(' || character == ')' ||
               character == '[' || character == ']' ||
               character == '.' || character == '%' ||
               character == '!' || character == ',' ||
               character == '>';
    }

    // Check whether content contains any number, variable, or recognized math symbol.
    public static bool IsMathematicalSyntaxContent(string content)
    {
        foreach (char character in content)
        {
            if (char.IsDigit(character) || IsVariableLetter(character) || IsMathSymbol(character))
            {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    // Check expression structure, optional single equality, and complete input use.
    public static bool HasValidSyntax(string content)
    {
        return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(content) ||
               IsStandaloneOperator(content.Trim()) ||
               IsUnaryFunctionName(content.Trim()) ||
               IsUnaryPrefixName(content.Trim()) ||
               content.Trim() == "(" ||
               content.Trim() == ")" ||
               MathEquationSolver.TryValidateExpression(content);
    }

    public static bool IsFunctionName(string identifier)
    {
        switch (identifier.ToLowerInvariant())
        {
            case "root":
            case "log":
            case "factorial":
            case "sin":
            case "cos":
            case "tan":
            case "asin":
            case "acos":
            case "atan":
            case "lim":
                return true;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }

    public static bool IsUnaryFunctionName(string identifier)
    {
        string name = identifier.ToLowerInvariant();
        return name == "root" || name == "log" || name == "factorial" ||
               name == "sin" || name == "cos" || name == "tan" ||
               name == "asin" || name == "acos" || name == "atan";
    }

    public static bool IsConstantName(string identifier)
    {
        string name = identifier.ToLowerInvariant();
        return name == "pi" || name == "e" || name == "i";
    }

    // Standalone operators are allowed while users are composing an expression.
    private static bool IsStandaloneOperator(string content)
    {
        return content == "+" || content == "-" ||
               content == "*" || content == "/" || content == "^" ||
               content == "\u00d7" || content == "\u00f7" ||
               content == "=" || content == "%" ||
               content == "!" || content == "1/x";
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Explicit reciprocal and power-token helpers (/N, /x, ^N, ^x).
    // ============================================================

    // Bare symbols are directional operators; these tokens require a suffix.
    public static bool IsUnaryPrefixName(string text)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) return false;
        char prefix = text[0];
        if (prefix != '/' && prefix != '^') return false;

        string rest = text.Substring(1).Trim();
        return rest.Length > 0 && IsValidUnaryPrefixSuffix(rest);
    }

    // Extract the suffix from an explicit reciprocal or power token.
    public static string UnaryPrefixBoundValue(string text)
    {
        if (!IsUnaryPrefixName(text)) return "";
        return text.Substring(1).Trim();
    }

    // Fraction tokens are numeric operands; suffixed power tokens are powers.
    public static bool IsFractionBall(string text)
    {
        return IsUnaryPrefixName(text) && text[0] == '/' &&
               UnaryPrefixBoundValue(text).Length > 0;
    }

    public static bool IsPowerBall(string text)
    {
        return IsUnaryPrefixName(text) && text[0] == '^' &&
               UnaryPrefixBoundValue(text).Length > 0;
    }

    private static bool IsValidUnaryPrefixSuffix(string rest)
    {
        if (rest.Length == 1 && char.IsDigit(rest[0])) return true;          // /3
        if (rest.Length == 1 && IsVariableLetter(rest[0])) return true;      // /x
        if (double.TryParse(rest,
                System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Float,
                System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                out _)) return true;                                         // /12, /3.5
        if (IsConstantName(rest)) return true;                               // /pi
        return false;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\NetworkBall.cs =====
using System.IO;
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

public enum BallZone : byte { Hand = 0, ServerBoard = 1 }

[RequireComponent(typeof(BallNumber))]
public class NetworkBall : NetworkBehaviour
{
    [SerializeField] private float positionSendHz = 20f;

    private BallNumber ball;
    private float nextSendTime;
    private float _lastLockActivity;
    private const float LockTimeoutSeconds = 5f;

    public BallNumber Ball => ball;

    public readonly NetworkVariable<byte> CurrentZone = new(
        0,
        NetworkVariableReadPermission.Everyone,
        NetworkVariableWritePermission.Server);

    public readonly NetworkVariable<int> SlotIndex = new(
        -1,
        NetworkVariableReadPermission.Everyone,
        NetworkVariableWritePermission.Server);

    public readonly NetworkVariable<ulong> LockedBy = new(
        ulong.MaxValue,
        NetworkVariableReadPermission.Everyone,
        NetworkVariableWritePermission.Server);

    public readonly NetworkVariable<bool> IsBagBall = new(
    false,
    NetworkVariableReadPermission.Everyone,
    NetworkVariableWritePermission.Server);

    private void Awake() => ball = GetComponent<BallNumber>();

    // ==================================================================
    // Spawn / despawn
    // ==================================================================

    public override void OnNetworkSpawn()
    {
        CurrentZone.OnValueChanged += OnZoneChanged;
        IsBagBall.OnValueChanged += OnIsBagBallChanged;

        if (IsServer)
        {
            ApplyVisibilityForZone(CurrentZone.Value);
            SendStateToAll();
            if (NetworkManager != null)
                NetworkManager.OnClientDisconnectCallback += OnClientDisconnected;
        }

        ApplyLayerForZone(CurrentZone.Value);

        var appearance = GetComponent<BallAppearance>();
        if (appearance != null)
            appearance.SetHandPiece(CurrentZone.Value == (byte)BallZone.Hand);

        // Attach lock-ring visual (client-side only; safe on server too, will no-op).
        if (GetComponent<LockRing>() == null)
            gameObject.AddComponent<LockRing>();

        if (IsBagBall.Value && GetComponent<BagRing>() == null)
        gameObject.AddComponent<BagRing>();

        if (NetworkBallManager.Instance != null)
            NetworkBallManager.Instance.NotifyBallSpawned(gameObject);
    }

    private void OnIsBagBallChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
    {
        if (newValue && GetComponent<BagRing>() == null)
            gameObject.AddComponent<BagRing>();
    }

    public override void OnNetworkDespawn()
    {
        CurrentZone.OnValueChanged -= OnZoneChanged;

        if (IsServer && NetworkManager != null)
            NetworkManager.OnClientDisconnectCallback -= OnClientDisconnected;

        if (IsServer) LockedBy.Value = ulong.MaxValue;

        if (NetworkBallManager.Instance != null)
            NetworkBallManager.Instance.NotifyBallDespawned(gameObject);
    }

    private void OnZoneChanged(byte oldZone, byte newZone)
    {
        ApplyVisibilityForZone(newZone);
        ApplyLayerForZone(newZone);

        var appearance = GetComponent<BallAppearance>();
        if (appearance != null)
            appearance.SetHandPiece(newZone == (byte)BallZone.Hand);
    }

    private void OnClientDisconnected(ulong clientId)
    {
        if (LockedBy.Value == clientId)
            LockedBy.Value = ulong.MaxValue;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Layer + visibility
    // ==================================================================

    private void ApplyLayerForZone(byte zone)
    {
        int layer = zone == (byte)BallZone.Hand
            ? LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces")
            : LayerMask.NameToLayer("BoardPieces");

        if (layer < 0)
        {
            Debug.LogError(
                $"Layer for zone {zone} not found. Add 'HandPieces' and 'BoardPieces' " +
                $"in Project Settings -> Tags and Layers.", this);
            return;
        }

        gameObject.layer = layer;
        foreach (Transform child in transform)
            ApplyLayerRecursive(child, layer);
    }

    private void ApplyLayerRecursive(Transform t, int layer)
    {
        t.gameObject.layer = layer;
        foreach (Transform child in t)
            ApplyLayerRecursive(child, layer);
    }

    private void ApplyVisibilityForZone(byte zone)
    {
        if (!IsServer || !IsSpawned) return;

        if (zone == (byte)BallZone.Hand)
        {
            foreach (var clientId in NetworkManager.ConnectedClientsIds)
            {
                if (clientId != OwnerClientId)
                    NetworkObject.NetworkHide(clientId);
                else
                    NetworkObject.NetworkShow(clientId);
            }
        }
        else
        {
            foreach (var clientId in NetworkManager.ConnectedClientsIds)
                NetworkObject.NetworkShow(clientId);
        }
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // State broadcast
    // ==================================================================

    private void Update()
    {        
        if (!IsSpawned || !IsServer) return;

        // Stale-lock timeout.
        if (LockedBy.Value != ulong.MaxValue &&
            Time.unscaledTime - _lastLockActivity > LockTimeoutSeconds)
        {
            LockedBy.Value = ulong.MaxValue;
        }

        if (Time.unscaledTime < nextSendTime) return;
        nextSendTime = Time.unscaledTime + 1f / Mathf.Max(1f, positionSendHz);
        SendStateToAll();
    }

    public void PushImmediate()
    {
        if (IsServer) SendStateToAll();
    }

    private void SendStateToAll()
    {
        var state = BallState.FromBall(
            ball,
            (uint)OwnerClientId,
            CurrentZone.Value,
            IsBagBall.Value);
        byte[] payload = Serialize(state);
        ReceiveStateClientRpc(payload);
    }

    [ClientRpc]
    private void ReceiveStateClientRpc(byte[] payload)
    {
        if (IsServer) return;

        var state = Deserialize(payload);
        if (IsOwner && BallDrag.isDragging && CurrentZone.Value == (byte)BallZone.Hand)
            state.position = ball.transform.position;

        state.ApplyTo(ball, authoritative: true);
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Lock RPCs
    // ==================================================================

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestGrabServerRpc(RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (CurrentZone.Value != (byte)BallZone.ServerBoard) return;

        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;

        // Clear stale locks first.
        if (LockedBy.Value != ulong.MaxValue &&
            Time.unscaledTime - _lastLockActivity > LockTimeoutSeconds)
        {
            LockedBy.Value = ulong.MaxValue;
        }

        if (LockedBy.Value == ulong.MaxValue || LockedBy.Value == sender)
        {
            LockedBy.Value = sender;
            _lastLockActivity = Time.unscaledTime;
            var targetParams = new ClientRpcParams
        {
            Send = new ClientRpcSendParams
            {
                TargetClientIds = new[] { sender }
            }
        };
        GrabResultClientRpc(true, targetParams);
        }
        else
        {
            var targetParams = new ClientRpcParams
        {
            Send = new ClientRpcSendParams
            {
                TargetClientIds = new[] { sender }
            }
        };
        GrabResultClientRpc(false, targetParams);
        }
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestReleaseServerRpc(RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;

        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;
        if (LockedBy.Value == sender)
        {
            LockedBy.Value = ulong.MaxValue;
            var directionalOperator = GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();
            if (directionalOperator != null && directionalOperator.IsActive)
                directionalOperator.EndDrag();
        }
    }

    [ClientRpc]
    private void GrabResultClientRpc(bool acquired, ClientRpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        BallDrag.OnGrabResult(this, acquired);
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Server RPCs
    // ==================================================================

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestHandPositionServerRpc(Vector2 pos, RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (CurrentZone.Value != (byte)BallZone.Hand) return;
        if (rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId != OwnerClientId) return;

        ball.transform.position = pos;
        var rb = ball.GetComponent<Rigidbody2D>();
        if (rb != null) rb.linearVelocity = Vector2.zero;
        SendStateToAll();
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestBoardMoveServerRpc(
        Vector2 pos, float rotationZ, RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (CurrentZone.Value != (byte)BallZone.ServerBoard) return;

        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;

        // Reject if someone else holds the lock.
        if (LockedBy.Value != ulong.MaxValue && LockedBy.Value != sender)
        {
            SendStateToAll();
            return;
        }

        // Implicit grab if no lock was set (safety net).
        if (LockedBy.Value == ulong.MaxValue)
            LockedBy.Value = sender;

        _lastLockActivity = Time.unscaledTime;

        var directionalOperator = GetComponent<DirectionalOperator>();
        if (directionalOperator != null && directionalOperator.IsActive)
        {
            directionalOperator.BeginDrag();
            var operatorBody = ball.GetComponent<Rigidbody2D>();
            if (operatorBody != null)
            {
                operatorBody.MovePosition(pos);
                operatorBody.MoveRotation(rotationZ);
            }
            return;
        }

        ball.transform.position = pos;
        var rb = ball.GetComponent<Rigidbody2D>();
        if (rb != null) rb.linearVelocity = Vector2.zero;
        SendStateToAll();
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestDespawnServerRpc(RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (IsBagBall.Value) return;   // ← bag balls cannot be deleted normally

        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;
        if (LockedBy.Value != ulong.MaxValue && LockedBy.Value != sender) return;

        NetworkObject.Despawn(true);
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestContentServerRpc(int number, char variable, int power,
                                    string key, byte mode, bool solverOutput, bool locked,
                                    RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (IsBagBall.Value) return;

        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;

        if (CurrentZone.Value == (byte)BallZone.Hand &&
            sender != OwnerClientId) return;

        if (LockedBy.Value != ulong.MaxValue && LockedBy.Value != sender) return;

        ball.SetInputModeRaw((BallInputMode)mode);
        ball.SetAlgebraContentRaw(number, variable, power);
        ball.SetRawDisplayedKey(key ?? string.Empty, solverOutput);
        ball.SetLockedRaw(locked);
        ball.RefreshDisplay();
        SendStateToAll();
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestBoardToHandServerRpc(RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (CurrentZone.Value != (byte)BallZone.ServerBoard) return;

        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;
        if (LockedBy.Value != ulong.MaxValue && LockedBy.Value != sender) return;

        NetworkBallManager.Instance.PerformBoardToHandTransfer(this, sender);
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Serialization
    // ==================================================================

    private static byte[] Serialize(in BallState state)
    {
        using var ms = new MemoryStream(64);
        using var bw = new BinaryWriter(ms);
        state.Write(bw);
        bw.Flush();
        return ms.ToArray();
    }

    private static BallState Deserialize(byte[] data)
    {
        using var ms = new MemoryStream(data);
        using var br = new BinaryReader(ms);
        return BallState.Read(br);
    }
}
