// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\NetworkBallManager.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

public class NetworkBallManager : NetworkBehaviour
{
    public static NetworkBallManager Instance { get; private set; }

    [Header("Prefab")]
    [SerializeField] private GameObject ballPrefab;
    [SerializeField] private GameObject directionalOperatorPrefab;

    [Header("Board world-space")]
    [SerializeField] private Vector2 boardCenter = new Vector2(0f, 0f);
    [SerializeField] private Vector2 boardSize   = new Vector2(12f, 8f);

    private Spawner spawner;

    public Vector2 BoardCenter => boardCenter;
    public Vector2 BoardSize   => boardSize;

    private void Awake()
    {
        Instance = this;
        spawner = GetComponent<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null)
            Debug.LogError("NetworkBallManager must live on the same GameObject as Spawner.");
    }

    public override void OnNetworkSpawn()
    {
        if (IsServer && NetworkManager.IsHost)
            StartCoroutine(ConvertLocalBoardPiecesToNetworked());
    }

    private System.Collections.IEnumerator ConvertLocalBoardPiecesToNetworked()
    {
        // Wait one frame so the network is fully up before converting.
        yield return null;

        var spawner = GetComponent<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null) yield break;

        var local = spawner.GetAllBoardPieces();
        foreach (var go in local)
        {
            if (go == null) continue;

            // Skip pieces that are already networked (avoid duplicates on re-host).
            var nb = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
            if (nb != null && nb.IsSpawned) continue;

            var ball = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
            if (ball == null) continue;

            string content = ball.GetRawDisplayedKey();
            Vector3 pos = go.transform.position;

            // Host IS the server → direct spawn, no RPC.
            SpawnBoardBall(pos, content);

            // Remove the local version.
            spawner.DestroyBoardBall(go);
        }
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Board-side spawn (server, unchanged)
    // ==================================================================

    public NetworkBall SpawnBoardBall(Vector3 pos, string content, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return null;
        var nb = SpawnBallServerInternal(pos, content,
            ownerId: NetworkManager.ServerClientId,
            zone: BallZone.ServerBoard,
            rotationZ: rotationZ);
        if (nb != null) nb.PushImmediate();
        return nb;
    }

    public NetworkBall SpawnBoardBagBall(Vector3 pos, string content, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return null;
        var nb = SpawnBallServerInternal(pos, content,
            ownerId: NetworkManager.ServerClientId,
            zone: BallZone.ServerBoard,
            rotationZ: rotationZ);
        if (nb == null) return null;

        nb.IsBagBall.Value = true;   // set after SpawnWithOwnership so it's a proper NetworkVariable write
        nb.PushImmediate();
        return nb;
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestSpawnBoardBagBallServerRpc(Vector3 position, string content, float rotationZ)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        SpawnBoardBagBall(position, content, rotationZ);
    }

    private NetworkBall SpawnBallServerInternal(Vector3 position, string content,
        ulong ownerId, BallZone zone, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        bool isDirectionalOperator = DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(
            content == null ? string.Empty : content.Trim());
        GameObject prefab = isDirectionalOperator
            ? directionalOperatorPrefab
            : ballPrefab;
        if (prefab == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError(isDirectionalOperator
                ? "NetworkBallManager: directionalOperatorPrefab not assigned."
                : "NetworkBallManager: ballPrefab not assigned.");
            return null;
        }

        var go = Instantiate(prefab, position, Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, rotationZ));
        var netObj = go.GetComponent<NetworkObject>();
        var ball   = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        var nb     = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();

        ball.SetInputMode(BallInputMode.DisplayKey);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
        {
            ball.ApplyTextInput(content, false);
            ball.RefreshDisplay();
        }

        nb.CurrentZone.Value = (byte)zone;
        netObj.SpawnWithOwnership(ownerId, destroyWithScene: true);

        return nb;

    }

    public void DespawnBallServer(GameObject go)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        var netObj = go.GetComponent<NetworkObject>();
        if (netObj != null && netObj.IsSpawned) netObj.Despawn(true);
        else Destroy(go);
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Hand → Board transfer
    // ==================================================================

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestHandToBoardServerRpc(Vector3 worldPos, string content,
        int number, char variable, int power, float rotationZ, RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;

        // Server spawns a brand-new board piece with the hand piece's content.
        var nb = SpawnBallServerInternal(
            worldPos,
            content ?? string.Empty,
            ownerId: NetworkManager.ServerClientId,
            zone: BallZone.ServerBoard,
            rotationZ: rotationZ);

        if (nb == null) return;

        // Apply algebra data if it wasn't just text.
        if (number != 0 || variable != '\0' || power != 0)
        {
            nb.Ball.SetAlgebraContentRaw(number, variable, power);
            nb.Ball.RefreshDisplay();
        }

        nb.PushImmediate();

      
    }

 

    //(client → server, for hand→board transfer from a client)
    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestSpawnBoardBallServerRpc(Vector3 position, string content, float rotationZ)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        SpawnBoardBall(position, content, rotationZ);
    }


    // ==================================================================
    // Board → Hand transfer
    // ==================================================================

    // Called server-side when a client requests taking a board piece into hand.
    public void PerformBoardToHandTransfer(NetworkBall nb, ulong requestingClientId)
    {
        if (!IsServer || nb == null) return;

        var ball = nb.Ball;
        string content = ball.GetRawDisplayedKey();
        int number = ball.NumberValue;
        char variable = ball.Variable;
        int power = ball.VariablePower;
        float rotationZ = ball.transform.eulerAngles.z;
        byte inputMode = (byte)ball.InputMode;
        bool solverOutput = ball.IsSolverOutput;
        bool locked = ball.IsLocked;
        bool isBagBall = nb.IsBagBall.Value;   // ← NEW

        nb.NetworkObject.Despawn(true);

        TransferBoardContentToClientRpc(
            requestingClientId, content, number, variable, power,
            inputMode, solverOutput, locked, isBagBall, rotationZ);
    }

    [ClientRpc]
    private void TransferBoardContentToClientRpc(
        ulong targetClientId, string content, int number, char variable, int power,
        byte inputMode, bool solverOutput, bool locked, bool isBagBall, float rotationZ)
    {
        if (NetworkManager.Singleton.LocalClientId != targetClientId) return;
        if (BallTransferManager.Instance == null) return;
        BallTransferManager.Instance.ReceiveBoardContentFromServer(
            content, number, variable, power, inputMode, solverOutput, locked, isBagBall, rotationZ);
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestBagReturnFromBoardServerRpc(NetworkObjectReference netObjRef, string content, RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (!netObjRef.TryGet(out NetworkObject netObj)) return;

        var nb = netObj.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (nb == null || !nb.IsBagBall.Value) return;

        if (BagManager.Instance != null)
            BagManager.Instance.RequestReturnToBag(content);

        nb.NetworkObject.Despawn(true);
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // Register / unregister with Spawner
    // ==================================================================

    public void NotifyBallSpawned(GameObject go)
    {
        if (spawner != null) spawner.RegisterBall(go);
    }

    public void NotifyBallDespawned(GameObject go)
    {
        if (spawner != null) spawner.UnregisterBall(go);
    }

        // ============================================================
    // Prefab-swap support
    // ============================================================

    public NetworkBall SpawnBoardOperator(Vector3 pos, string content, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return null;
        if (directionalOperatorPrefab == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError("NetworkBallManager: directionalOperatorPrefab not assigned.");
            return null;
        }

        var go = Instantiate(directionalOperatorPrefab, pos,
                             Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, rotationZ));
        var netObj = go.GetComponent<NetworkObject>();
        var ball   = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        var nb     = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();

        ball.SetInputMode(BallInputMode.DisplayKey);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
        {
            ball.ApplyTextInput(content, false);
            ball.RefreshDisplay();
        }

        nb.CurrentZone.Value = (byte)BallZone.ServerBoard;
        netObj.SpawnWithOwnership(NetworkManager.ServerClientId, destroyWithScene: true);
        nb.PushImmediate();
        return nb;
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void RequestSwapBallServerRpc(
        NetworkObjectReference netObjRef,
        bool shouldBeDirectional,
        string key, int number, char variable, int power,
        byte mode, bool solverOutput, bool locked, bool isBag, float rotZ,
        RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (!netObjRef.TryGet(out NetworkObject netObj)) return;

        var nb = netObj.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (nb == null) return;

        // Server-side lock check: only allow a swap if the sender holds the
        // lock, or nobody does, or the ball is unowned.
        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;
        if (nb.LockedBy.Value != ulong.MaxValue && nb.LockedBy.Value != sender)
            return;

        Vector3 pos = netObj.transform.position;
        netObj.Despawn(true);

        NetworkBall newBall = shouldBeDirectional
            ? SpawnBoardOperator(pos, key, rotZ)
            : SpawnBoardBall(pos, key, rotZ);

        if (newBall == null) return;

        newBall.Ball.SetInputModeRaw((BallInputMode)mode);
        newBall.Ball.SetRawDisplayedKey(key, solverOutput);
        newBall.Ball.SetAlgebraContentRaw(number, variable, power);
        newBall.Ball.SetLockedRaw(locked);
        newBall.Ball.RefreshDisplay();

        if (isBag)
        {
            newBall.IsBagBall.Value = true;
        }
        newBall.PushImmediate();
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\NetworkGameSession.cs =====
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Unity.Netcode;
using Unity.Netcode.Transports.UTP;
using Unity.Services.Authentication;
using Unity.Services.Core;
using Unity.Services.Relay;
using Unity.Services.Relay.Models;
using Unity.Networking.Transport.Relay;
using UnityEngine;

public class NetworkGameSession : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static NetworkGameSession Instance { get; private set; }

    public string JoinCode { get; private set; }
    public bool   IsHost   { get; private set; }

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this) { Destroy(this); return; }
        Instance = this;
        DontDestroyOnLoad(gameObject);
    }

    public async Task InitializeAsync()
    {
        if (UnityServices.State != ServicesInitializationState.Initialized)
            await UnityServices.InitializeAsync();

        if (!AuthenticationService.Instance.IsSignedIn)
            await AuthenticationService.Instance.SignInAnonymouslyAsync();
    }

    public async Task<string> HostRoomAsync(int maxPlayers = 8)
    {
        NetworkManager networkManager = GetNetworkManager();
        UnityTransport transport = GetUnityTransport(networkManager);

        await InitializeAsync();

        Allocation alloc = await RelayService.Instance.CreateAllocationAsync(maxPlayers);
        if (alloc == null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Relay did not return an allocation.");

        string joinCode = await RelayService.Instance.GetJoinCodeAsync(alloc.AllocationId);
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(joinCode))
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Relay did not return a room code.");

        Debug.Log($"Relay allocation and room code created ({joinCode}). Configuring transport.");

        transport.SetRelayServerData(new RelayServerData(alloc, "dtls"));

        Debug.Log("Relay transport is configured. Starting the network host.");
        if (!networkManager.StartHost())
            throw new InvalidOperationException("NetworkManager.StartHost() returned false. Check the Unity Netcode logs for the startup failure.");

        JoinCode = joinCode;
        IsHost = true;
        return JoinCode;
    }

    public async Task JoinRoomAsync(string joinCode)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(joinCode))
            throw new ArgumentException("A room code is required.", nameof(joinCode));

        joinCode = joinCode.Trim().ToUpperInvariant();
        NetworkManager networkManager = GetNetworkManager();
        UnityTransport transport = GetUnityTransport(networkManager);

        await InitializeAsync();

        JoinAllocation alloc = await RelayService.Instance.JoinAllocationAsync(joinCode);
        transport.SetRelayServerData(new RelayServerData(alloc, "dtls"));

        if (!networkManager.StartClient())
            throw new InvalidOperationException("NetworkManager.StartClient() returned false. Check the Unity Netcode logs for the startup failure.");

        IsHost = false;
        JoinCode = joinCode;
    }

    private static NetworkManager GetNetworkManager()
    {
        if (NetworkManager.Singleton != null)
            return NetworkManager.Singleton;

        NetworkManager[] managers = FindObjectsByType<NetworkManager>(
            FindObjectsInactive.Include, FindObjectsSortMode.None);
        string managerDetails = managers.Length == 0
            ? "none"
            : string.Join("; ", Array.ConvertAll(managers, manager =>
                $"{manager.name} (enabled={manager.enabled}, active={manager.gameObject.activeInHierarchy}, scene={manager.gameObject.scene.name})"));

        string message = $"NetworkManager.Singleton is null. Runtime NetworkManager components found: {managerDetails}.";
        Debug.LogError(message);
        throw new InvalidOperationException(message);
    }

    private static UnityTransport GetUnityTransport(NetworkManager networkManager)
    {
        UnityTransport transport = networkManager.GetComponent<UnityTransport>();
        if (transport == null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("The active NetworkManager has no UnityTransport component.");

        if (networkManager.NetworkConfig == null ||
            networkManager.NetworkConfig.NetworkTransport != transport)
            throw new InvalidOperationException("The active NetworkManager's Network Transport is not its UnityTransport component.");

        return transport;
    }

    public void Leave()
    {
        if (NetworkManager.Singleton != null && NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening)
            NetworkManager.Singleton.Shutdown();
        JoinCode = null;
        IsHost = false;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\NetworkLobbyUI.cs =====
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

public class NetworkLobbyUI : MonoBehaviour
{
    public NetworkGameSession session;

    private string joinCodeInput = "";
    private string statusMessage = "";
    private bool busy;

    private void OnGUI()
    {
        if (session == null) return;

        // Don't draw lobby UI while the name prompt is open.
        if (PlayerNamePrompt.Instance != null && PlayerNamePrompt.Instance.IsOpen) return;

        bool connected = NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
                         NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening;

        GUILayout.BeginArea(new Rect(20, 20, 340, connected ? 220 : 300), GUI.skin.box);

        GUILayout.Label("<b>Multiplayer</b>", new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label)
            { richText = true, fontSize = 16 });

        if (connected)
        {
            GUILayout.Label(session.IsHost ? "Hosting session" : "Connected to session");

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(session.JoinCode))
            {
                GUILayout.Label($"Room code: {session.JoinCode}");
                if (GUILayout.Button("Copy room code"))
                    GUIUtility.systemCopyBuffer = session.JoinCode;
            }
            else
            {
                GUILayout.Label("No room code is available.");
            }

            if (GUILayout.Button("Leave room"))
            {
                session.Leave();
                statusMessage = "Left room.";
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(statusMessage))
                GUILayout.Label(statusMessage);

            GUILayout.EndArea();
            return;
        }

        GUI.enabled = !busy;

        if (GUILayout.Button("Host Room", GUILayout.Height(34)))
        {
            PromptForNameAndThen(name =>
            {
                busy = true;
                _ = HostAsync(name);
            });
        }

        GUILayout.Space(8);
        GUILayout.Label("Join code:");
        joinCodeInput = GUILayout.TextField(joinCodeInput, 8).ToUpperInvariant();

        if (GUILayout.Button("Join Room", GUILayout.Height(34)) &&
            joinCodeInput.Length >= 4)
        {
            string code = joinCodeInput;
            PromptForNameAndThen(name =>
            {
                busy = true;
                _ = JoinAsync(code, name);
            });
        }

        GUI.enabled = true;

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(session.JoinCode))
        {
            GUILayout.Space(8);
            GUILayout.Label($"Room code: {session.JoinCode}");
            if (GUILayout.Button("Copy"))
                GUIUtility.systemCopyBuffer = session.JoinCode;
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(statusMessage))
            GUILayout.Label(statusMessage);

        GUILayout.EndArea();
    }

    private void PromptForNameAndThen(System.Action<string> onConfirm)
    {
        if (PlayerNamePrompt.Instance == null)
        {
            // No prompt in scene — fall back to auto-name.
            onConfirm?.Invoke($"Player{Random.Range(1000, 9999)}");
            return;
        }

        PlayerNamePrompt.Instance.Open(onConfirm);
    }

    private async Task HostAsync(string playerName)
    {
        try
        {
            await session.HostRoomAsync();
            statusMessage = "Hosting.";
            _ = SubmitNameAsync(playerName);
        }
        catch (System.Exception e)
        {
            Debug.LogException(e);
            statusMessage = "Host failed: " + e.Message;
        }
        finally { busy = false; }
    }

    private async Task JoinAsync(string code, string playerName)
    {
        try
        {
            await session.JoinRoomAsync(code);
            statusMessage = "Joined.";
            _ = SubmitNameAsync(playerName);
        }
        catch (System.Exception e)
        {
            statusMessage = "Join failed: " + e.Message;
            // Reopen prompt with error.
            if (PlayerNamePrompt.Instance != null)
                PlayerNamePrompt.Instance.SetError("Connection failed, try again.");
        }
        finally { busy = false; }
    }

    private async Task SubmitNameAsync(string name)
    {
        // Wait a moment for PlayerData to be spawned by PlayerManager.
        for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++)
        {
            if (PlayerManager.Instance != null && NetworkManager.Singleton != null)
            {
                var pd = FindMyPlayerData();
                if (pd != null) break;
            }
            await Task.Delay(50);
        }

        // Send name to server.
        PlayerNameSubmitter.SubmitName(name);
    }

    private PlayerData FindMyPlayerData()
    {
        if (NetworkManager.Singleton == null) return null;
        ulong myId = NetworkManager.Singleton.LocalClientId;

        foreach (var pd in FindObjectsByType<PlayerData>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
        {
            if (pd != null && pd.OwnerClientId == myId && pd.IsSpawned)
                return pd;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\PlayerData.cs =====
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

public class PlayerData : NetworkBehaviour
{
    public readonly NetworkVariable<FixedString32Bytes> PlayerName = new(
        default,
        NetworkVariableReadPermission.Everyone,
        NetworkVariableWritePermission.Server);

    public readonly NetworkVariable<int> HandBagCount = new(
        0,
        NetworkVariableReadPermission.Everyone,
        NetworkVariableWritePermission.Server);

    public bool HasSetName => PlayerName.Value.Length > 0;
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\PlayerManger.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Unity.Collections;
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Spawns a PlayerData NetworkObject per connected client and manages
/// name uniqueness. Scene-level singleton.
/// </summary>
public class PlayerManager : NetworkBehaviour
{
    public static PlayerManager Instance { get; private set; }

    [SerializeField] private GameObject playerDataPrefab;

    private readonly Dictionary<ulong, PlayerData> _players = new();
    private readonly HashSet<string> _claimedNames = new();

    public const int MaxNameLength = 16;

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
        Instance = this;
    }

    public override void OnNetworkSpawn()
    {
        if (IsServer)
        {
            NetworkManager.OnClientConnectedCallback += OnClientConnected;
            NetworkManager.OnClientDisconnectCallback += OnClientDisconnected;

            // Host is already connected by the time OnNetworkSpawn runs.
            if (NetworkManager.IsHost && NetworkManager.LocalClientId >= 0)
                SpawnPlayerData(NetworkManager.LocalClientId);
        }
    }

    public override void OnNetworkDespawn()
    {
        if (IsServer && NetworkManager != null)
        {
            NetworkManager.OnClientConnectedCallback -= OnClientConnected;
            NetworkManager.OnClientDisconnectCallback -= OnClientDisconnected;
        }
    }

    private void OnClientConnected(ulong clientId)
    {
        SpawnPlayerData(clientId);
    }

    private void OnClientDisconnected(ulong clientId)
    {
        if (_players.TryGetValue(clientId, out var pd))
        {
            if (pd != null && pd.PlayerName.Value.Length > 0)
                _claimedNames.Remove(pd.PlayerName.Value.ToString());

            if (pd != null && pd.IsSpawned)
                pd.NetworkObject.Despawn(true);

            _players.Remove(clientId);
        }
    }

    private void SpawnPlayerData(ulong clientId)
    {
        if (_players.ContainsKey(clientId)) return;
        if (playerDataPrefab == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError("PlayerManager: playerDataPrefab not assigned.");
            return;
        }

        var go = Instantiate(playerDataPrefab);
        var netObj = go.GetComponent<NetworkObject>();
        var pd = go.GetComponent<PlayerData>();

        netObj.SpawnWithOwnership(clientId, destroyWithScene: true);
        _players[clientId] = pd;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Server-side: attempts to set the player's name.
    /// Called via RPC from the owning client.
    /// </summary>
    public bool TrySetName(ulong clientId, string name, out string error)
    {
        error = null;

        if (!_players.TryGetValue(clientId, out var pd) || pd == null)
        {
            error = "Player data not found.";
            return false;
        }

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
        {
            error = "Name cannot be empty.";
            return false;
        }

        name = name.Trim();
        if (name.Length > MaxNameLength)
        {
            error = $"Name must be at most {MaxNameLength} characters.";
            return false;
        }

        // If they already had a name, release it first.
        if (pd.PlayerName.Value.Length > 0)
        {
            _claimedNames.Remove(pd.PlayerName.Value.ToString());
        }

        if (_claimedNames.Contains(name))
        {
            // Re-add their old name if it existed and re-set it back.
            if (pd.PlayerName.Value.Length > 0)
                _claimedNames.Add(pd.PlayerName.Value.ToString());

            error = "That name is already taken.";
            return false;
        }

        _claimedNames.Add(name);
        pd.PlayerName.Value = new FixedString32Bytes(name);
        return true;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Returns the display name for a client. Falls back to a default
    /// if the player hasn't set their name yet.
    /// </summary>
    public static string GetPlayerName(ulong clientId)
    {
        var inst = Instance;
        if (inst == null) return $"Player {clientId}";

        if (inst._players.TryGetValue(clientId, out var pd) &&
            pd != null &&
            pd.PlayerName.Value.Length > 0)
        {
            return pd.PlayerName.Value.ToString();
        }

        return $"Player {clientId}";
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    public void ReportHandBagCountServerRpc(int count, RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;

        if (_players.TryGetValue(sender, out var pd) && pd != null)
        {
            pd.HandBagCount.Value = Mathf.Max(0, count);
        }
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\PlayerNamePrompt.cs =====
using System;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Modal name prompt. Calls the provided callback with the confirmed name.
/// While active, blocks other lobby input.
/// </summary>
public class PlayerNamePrompt : MonoBehaviour
{
    public static PlayerNamePrompt Instance { get; private set; }

    [SerializeField] private float panelWidth = 400f;
    [SerializeField] private float panelHeight = 200f;

    private bool _isOpen;
    private string _inputText = "";
    private string _error = "";
    private Action<string> _onConfirm;

    public bool IsOpen => _isOpen;

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
        Instance = this;
    }

    public void Open(Action<string> onConfirm, string initialError = "")
    {
        _onConfirm = onConfirm;
        _inputText = "";
        _error = initialError;
        _isOpen = true;
    }

    public void Close()
    {
        _isOpen = false;
        _onConfirm = null;
        _error = "";
    }

    public void SetError(string error)
    {
        _error = error;
        _isOpen = true;
    }

    private void OnGUI()
    {
        if (!_isOpen) return;

        // Dim background.
        GUI.color = new Color(0, 0, 0, 0.5f);
        GUI.DrawTexture(new Rect(0, 0, Screen.width, Screen.height), Texture2D.whiteTexture);
        GUI.color = Color.white;

        Rect panel = new Rect(
            (Screen.width - panelWidth) * 0.5f,
            (Screen.height - panelHeight) * 0.5f,
            panelWidth, panelHeight);

        GUI.Box(panel, GUIContent.none, GUI.skin.window);

        GUILayout.BeginArea(new Rect(panel.x + 20, panel.y + 20, panel.width - 40, panel.height - 40));

        GUILayout.Label("Enter your name:", new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label) { fontSize = 16 });
        GUILayout.Space(8);

        GUI.SetNextControlName("NameField");
        _inputText = GUILayout.TextField(_inputText, PlayerManager.MaxNameLength, GUILayout.Height(30));
        GUI.FocusControl("NameField");

        GUILayout.Space(6);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_error))
        {
            var errStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label) { fontSize = 13 };
            errStyle.normal.textColor = new Color(1f, 0.4f, 0.4f);
            GUILayout.Label(_error, errStyle);
        }

        GUILayout.FlexibleSpace();

        GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
        if (GUILayout.Button("Cancel", GUILayout.Height(32)))
        {
            Close();
        }

        GUI.enabled = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_inputText);

        bool confirmByEnter = Event.current.type == EventType.KeyDown &&
                              Event.current.keyCode == KeyCode.Return;

        if (GUILayout.Button("Confirm", GUILayout.Height(32)) || confirmByEnter)
        {
            string name = _inputText.Trim();
            _isOpen = false;
            var callback = _onConfirm;
            _onConfirm = null;
            callback?.Invoke(name);
        }

        GUI.enabled = true;
        GUILayout.EndHorizontal();

        GUILayout.EndArea();
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\PlayerNameSubmitter.cs =====
using Unity.Netcode;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Helper to send the client's name to the server after PlayerData spawns.
/// </summary>
public class PlayerNameSubmitter : NetworkBehaviour
{
    public static PlayerNameSubmitter Instance { get; private set; }

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (Instance != null && Instance != this) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
        Instance = this;
    }

    public static void SubmitName(string name)
    {
        if (Instance == null)
        {
            Debug.LogWarning("PlayerNameSubmitter.Instance is null — name not sent.");
            return;
        }
        Instance.SubmitNameServerRpc(name);
    }

    [Rpc(SendTo.Server, InvokePermission = RpcInvokePermission.Everyone)]
    private void SubmitNameServerRpc(string name, RpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (!IsServer) return;
        if (PlayerManager.Instance == null) return;

        ulong sender = rpcParams.Receive.SenderClientId;

        if (PlayerManager.Instance.TrySetName(sender, name, out string error))
        {
            NameResultClientRpc(true, "", TargetedTo(sender));
        }
        else
        {
            NameResultClientRpc(false, error ?? "Name rejected.", TargetedTo(sender));
        }
    }

    [ClientRpc]
    private void NameResultClientRpc(bool success, string error, ClientRpcParams rpcParams = default)
    {
        if (success)
        {
            Debug.Log("[Name] Name accepted.");
        }
        else
        {
            Debug.Log($"[Name] Name rejected: {error}");
            // Reopen the prompt with the error message.
            if (PlayerNamePrompt.Instance != null)
            {
                PlayerNamePrompt.Instance.SetError(error);
            }
        }
    }

    private static ClientRpcParams TargetedTo(ulong clientId)
    {
        return new ClientRpcParams
        {
            Send = new ClientRpcSendParams { TargetClientIds = new[] { clientId } }
        };
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\RandomBallSummoner.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

public class RandomBallSummoner : MonoBehaviour
{
    private const int BallCount = 5;
    private const float BallSpacing = 2.1f;

    private static readonly int[] SmallNumberWeights =
    {
        1, 8, 7, 6, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1
    };

    private static readonly string[] CommonVariables =
    {
        "x", "x", "y", "y", "a", "b", "c", "n"
    };

    private static readonly string[] Functions =
    {
        "root", "log", "sin", "cos", "tan", "asin", "acos", "atan"
    };

    private Spawner spawner;

    private void Awake()
    {
        spawner = GetComponent<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError("RandomBallSummoner requires a Spawner on the same object.", this);
        }
    }

    public void SpawnRandomBallsAt(Vector3 center)
    {
        if (spawner == null)
        {
            return;
        }

        for (int index = 0; index < BallCount; index++)
        {
            float horizontalOffset = (index - (BallCount - 1) / 2f) * BallSpacing;
            Vector3 position = center + Vector3.right * horizontalOffset;
            spawner.SpawnBallWithContent(position, ChooseMathToken());
        }
    }

    private static string ChooseMathToken()
    {
        int choice = Random.Range(0, 100);
        if (choice < 65)
        {
            return ChooseNumber().ToString();
        }

        if (choice < 86)
        {
            return ChooseCommonOperator();
        }

        if (choice < 92)
        {
            return "=";
        }

        if (choice < 98)
        {
            return CommonVariables[Random.Range(0, CommonVariables.Length)];
        }

        return ChooseAdvancedToken();
    }

    private static int ChooseNumber()
    {
        int size = Random.Range(0, 1000);
        int number;
        if (size < 850)
        {
            number = ChooseSmallNumber();
        }
        else if (size < 995)
        {
            number = Random.Range(10, 101);
        }
        else
        {
            number = Random.Range(101, 1001);
        }

        return Random.Range(0, 100) < 10 ? -number : number;
    }

    private static int ChooseSmallNumber()
    {
        int totalWeight = 0;
        foreach (int weight in SmallNumberWeights)
        {
            totalWeight += weight;
        }

        int roll = Random.Range(0, totalWeight);
        for (int index = 0; index < SmallNumberWeights.Length; index++)
        {
            if (roll < SmallNumberWeights[index])
            {
                return index + 1;
            }

            roll -= SmallNumberWeights[index];
        }

        return SmallNumberWeights.Length;
    }

    private static string ChooseCommonOperator()
    {
        int choice = Random.Range(0, 100);
        if (choice < 35)
        {
            return "+";
        }

        if (choice < 60)
        {
            return "-";
        }

        if (choice < 80)
        {
            return "*";
        }

        return "/";
    }

    private static string ChooseAdvancedToken()
    {
        int choice = Random.Range(0, 100);
        if (choice < 30)
        {
            return "^";
        }

        if (choice < 45)
        {
            return "\u00d7";
        }

        if (choice < 60)
        {
            return "\u00f7";
        }

        if (choice < 75)
        {
            return "!";
        }

        if (choice < 90)
        {
            return "%";
        }

        return Functions[Random.Range(0, Functions.Length)];
    }
}
