// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\SelectionInputRouter.cs =====
using UnityEngine;

public class SelectionInputRouter : MonoBehaviour
{
    private void Update()
    {
        var selected = LassoSelection.GetSelectedBalls();
        if (selected == null || selected.Count == 0) return;
        if (TestMenuUI.ShouldBlockSpawnerInput) return;

        // Delete (server-authoritative)
        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Delete))
        {
            foreach (var drag in selected)
            {
                if (drag == null) continue;
                // Skip bag balls — they're read-only and not deletable.
                if (drag.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null) continue;
                var nbCheck = drag.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
                if (nbCheck != null && nbCheck.IsBagBall.Value) continue;
                var nb = drag.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
                if (nb != null && nb.IsSpawned) nb.RequestDespawnServerRpc();
                else Destroy(drag.gameObject);
            }
            StartCoroutine(DeselectNextFrame());
            return;
        }

        // Backspace (content edit — routed through server for spawned balls)
        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Backspace))
        {
            foreach (var drag in selected)
            {
                if (drag == null) continue;
                // Skip bag balls — they're read-only and not deletable.
                if (drag.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null) continue;
                var nbCheck = drag.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
                if (nbCheck != null && nbCheck.IsBagBall.Value) continue;
                var ball = drag.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
                var nb   = drag.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
                if (ball == null) continue;

                ball.ClearContent();
                ball.RefreshDisplay();

                if (nb != null && nb.IsSpawned)
                {
                    nb.RequestContentServerRpc(
                        ball.NumberValue, ball.Variable, ball.VariablePower,
                        ball.GetRawDisplayedKey(), (byte)ball.InputMode,
                        ball.IsSolverOutput, ball.IsLocked);
                }
            }
            return;
        }

        // Typing
        string typed = Input.inputString;
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(typed))
        {
            string cleaned = string.Empty;
            foreach (char c in typed) if (!char.IsControl(c)) cleaned += c;

            if (cleaned.Length > 0)
            {
                foreach (var drag in selected)
                {
                    if (drag == null) continue;
                    // Skip bag balls — they're read-only and not deletable.
                    if (drag.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null) continue;
                    var nbCheck = drag.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
                    if (nbCheck != null && nbCheck.IsBagBall.Value) continue;
                    var ball = drag.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
                    var nb   = drag.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
                    if (ball == null) continue;

                    ball.ClearContent();
                    ball.ApplyTextInput(cleaned, false);
                    ball.RefreshDisplay();

                    if (nb != null && nb.IsSpawned)
                    {
                        nb.RequestContentServerRpc(
                            ball.NumberValue, ball.Variable, ball.VariablePower,
                            ball.GetRawDisplayedKey(), (byte)ball.InputMode,
                            ball.IsSolverOutput, ball.IsLocked);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private System.Collections.IEnumerator DeselectNextFrame()
    {
        yield return null;
        LassoSelection.DeselectAll();
    }
}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\Spawner.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

public class Spawner : MonoBehaviour
{
    private const int TendrilPointCount = 7;
    private const float MaxBallRadius = 2f;

    public GameObject ballPrefab;
    public GameObject directionalOperatorPrefab;
    public EquationListDisplay equationListDisplay;

    private readonly List<GameObject> boardBalls = new List<GameObject>();
    private readonly List<GameObject> handBalls  = new List<GameObject>();
        private int _lastStructuralVersion = -1;
    private List<MathEquationBuilder.CachedCluster> _boardClusters = new();
    private List<MathEquationBuilder.CachedCluster> _handClusters  = new();
    private MathEquationBuilder.BuildResult _boardCache;
    private MathEquationBuilder.BuildResult _handCache;

    private readonly List<LineRenderer> boardTendrils = new List<LineRenderer>();
    private readonly List<LineRenderer> handTendrils  = new List<LineRenderer>();

    private Material tendrilMaterial;
    private RandomBallSummoner randomBallSummoner;
    private TestMenuUI testMenuUI;

    [Header("Connection Lines")]
    [SerializeField] private float tendrilWidth = 0.03f;
    [SerializeField] private Color tendrilColor = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.9f);
    [Tooltip("Extra sorting order above the ball sprite's order. Higher = drawn in front.")]
    [SerializeField] private int tendrilSortingOrderOffset = 10;
    [Tooltip("Leave empty to auto-match the balls' sorting layer.")]
    [SerializeField] private string tendrilSortingLayer = "";

        [System.Serializable]
    public class StartingHandBall
    {
        public string content = "";
        [Tooltip("Horizontal offset from the hand-viewport center, in viewport units (-0.5 to 0.5).")]
        public float xOffset;
    }

    [Header("Starting Hand")]
    [SerializeField] private StartingHandBall[] startingHandBalls = new StartingHandBall[]
    {
        new StartingHandBall { content = "", xOffset = -0.16f },
        new StartingHandBall { content = "", xOffset = -0.08f },
        new StartingHandBall { content = "", xOffset =  0.00f },
        new StartingHandBall { content = "", xOffset =  0.08f },
        new StartingHandBall { content = "", xOffset =  0.16f },
    };


    void Awake()
    {
        testMenuUI = GetComponent<TestMenuUI>();
        randomBallSummoner = GetComponent<RandomBallSummoner>();
        var shader = Shader.Find("Sprites/Default");
        if (shader == null) { Debug.LogError("Missing Sprites/Default shader.", this); return; }
        tendrilMaterial = new Material(shader);
    }

    void Start()
    {
        // Offline: spawn the starting hand locally.
        var nm = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton;
        bool online = nm != null && nm.IsListening;
        if (!online)
            SpawnStartingLocalHand();
    }

             void Update()
    {
        HandleMouseInput();

        if (BallDrag.isDragging)
            MathEquationBuilder.MarkConnectionsChanged();

        int structural = MathEquationBuilder.CurrentStructuralVersion;
        bool connectionChanged = MathEquationBuilder.ConsumeConnectionDirty();
        bool contentChanged    = MathEquationBuilder.ConsumeContentDirty();

        bool repartition =
            structural != _lastStructuralVersion || connectionChanged;

        if (repartition)
        {
            _lastStructuralVersion = structural;
            _boardClusters = Repartition(_boardClusters, boardBalls);
            _handClusters  = Repartition(_handClusters,  handBalls);
        }

        if (repartition || contentChanged)
        {
            _boardCache = SolveDirtyClusters(_boardClusters, "board");
            _handCache  = SolveDirtyClusters(_handClusters,  "hand");
        }

        var boardMask = LayerMask.GetMask("BoardPieces");
        var handMask  = LayerMask.GetMask("HandPieces");

        if (_boardCache != null)
            RefreshTendrils(_boardCache.connectedPairs, boardTendrils, boardMask);
        if (_handCache != null)
            RefreshTendrils(_handCache.connectedPairs,  handTendrils,  handMask);
    }

    void OnDestroy()
    {
        if (tendrilMaterial != null) Destroy(tendrilMaterial);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Camera routing
    // ============================================================

    private Camera GetCameraForScreenPoint(Vector2 screenPos)
    {
        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        if (setup == null) return Camera.main;
        bool isHand = screenPos.y < Screen.height * setup.HandFraction;
        return isHand ? setup.HandCamera : setup.BoardCamera;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Spawn — board (networked)
    // ============================================================

    public void SpawnBall()
    {
        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        Camera cam = setup != null ? setup.HandCamera : Camera.main;
        if (cam == null) return;

        Vector3 p = cam.ViewportToWorldPoint(new Vector3(0.5f, 0.5f, 0f));
        p.z = 0f;

        // Small random offset so repeated presses don't stack pieces.
        float radius = 0.6f * (cam.orthographicSize / 5f);   // scale offset with zoom
        Vector2 dir = Random.insideUnitCircle.normalized;
        p.x += dir.x * Random.Range(0.2f, 1f) * radius;
        p.y += dir.y * Random.Range(0.2f, 1f) * radius;

        SpawnHandBallAt(p);
    }


    public void SpawnBallAtPublic(Vector3 position, string content, float rotationZ = 0f)
{
    SpawnBallAt(position, content, rotationZ);
}

private void SpawnBallAt(Vector3 position, string initialContent = null, float rotationZ = 0f)
{
    MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallSpawned();
    

    bool netActive = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
                     Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening;

    if (netActive)
    {
        if (Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsServer &&
            NetworkBallManager.Instance != null)
            NetworkBallManager.Instance.SpawnBoardBall(position, initialContent, rotationZ);
        return;
    }

    // Offline board piece
    GameObject prefab = GetPrefabForContent(initialContent);
    if (prefab == null) return;
    var go = Instantiate(prefab, position, Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, rotationZ));
    var netObj = go.GetComponent<Unity.Netcode.NetworkObject>();
    if (netObj != null) Destroy(netObj);
    var networkBall = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
    if (networkBall != null) Destroy(networkBall);
    var ballNumber = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
    if (ballNumber == null) { Destroy(go); return; }
    ballNumber.SetInputMode(BallInputMode.DisplayKey);
    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(initialContent))
    {
        ballNumber.ApplyTextInput(initialContent, false);
        ballNumber.RefreshDisplay();
    }
    int layer = LayerMask.NameToLayer("BoardPieces");
    if (layer >= 0) ApplyLayerRecursive(go.transform, layer);
    boardBalls.Add(go);
}

    public void SpawnBallWithContent(Vector3 position, string content)
        => SpawnBallAt(position, content);

    private GameObject GetPrefabForContent(string content)
    {
        if (DirectionalOperator.IsDirectionalOperatorText(content == null ? string.Empty : content.Trim()))
        {
            if (directionalOperatorPrefab == null)
            {
                Debug.LogError("directionalOperatorPrefab not assigned.", this);
                return null;
            }

            return directionalOperatorPrefab;
        }

        if (ballPrefab == null)
            Debug.LogError("ballPrefab not assigned.", this);
        return ballPrefab;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Spawn — hand (local only)
    // ============================================================

    public void SpawnHandBallAt(Vector3 handWorldPos, string initialContent = null)
    {
        SpawnLocalHandBall(handWorldPos, initialContent, 0, '\0', 0);
    }

    public void SpawnLocalHandBagBall(Vector3 handWorldPos, string content, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        GameObject prefab = GetPrefabForContent(content);
        if (prefab == null) return;

        var go = Instantiate(prefab, handWorldPos, Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, rotationZ));

        // Strip networking.
        var netObj = go.GetComponent<Unity.Netcode.NetworkObject>();
        if (netObj != null) Destroy(netObj);
        var networkBall = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (networkBall != null) Destroy(networkBall);

        // Mark as a hand piece and a bag ball.
        go.AddComponent<LocalHandBall>().Initialize(GetLocalClientId());
        go.AddComponent<BagBallMarker>();
        go.AddComponent<BagRing>();

        var ballNumber = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ballNumber == null) { Destroy(go); return; }

        ballNumber.SetInputMode(BallInputMode.DisplayKey);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
        {
            ballNumber.ApplyTextInput(content, false);
            ballNumber.RefreshDisplay();
        }

                int layer = LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces");
        if (layer >= 0) ApplyLayerRecursive(go.transform, layer);

                handBalls.Add(go);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallSpawned();
        
    }

    public void SpawnLocalHandBall(Vector3 handWorldPos, string content,
        int number = 0, char variable = '\0', int power = 0, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        GameObject prefab = GetPrefabForContent(content);
        if (prefab == null) return;

        // Hand pieces must NOT be networked. Strip NetworkObject and NetworkBall.
        var go = Instantiate(prefab, handWorldPos, Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, rotationZ));
        var netObj = go.GetComponent<Unity.Netcode.NetworkObject>();
        if (netObj != null) Destroy(netObj);
        var networkBall = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (networkBall != null) Destroy(networkBall);

        // Mark as a local hand piece.
        go.AddComponent<LocalHandBall>().Initialize(GetLocalClientId());

        var ballNumber = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ballNumber == null) { Destroy(go); return; }

        ballNumber.SetInputMode(BallInputMode.DisplayKey);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
        {
            ballNumber.ApplyTextInput(content, false);
            ballNumber.RefreshDisplay();
        }
        if (number != 0 || variable != '\0' || power != 0)
        {
            ballNumber.SetAlgebraContentRaw(number, variable, power);
            ballNumber.RefreshDisplay();
        }

                int layer = LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces");
        if (layer >= 0) ApplyLayerRecursive(go.transform, layer);

                handBalls.Add(go);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallSpawned();
        
    }

    private ulong GetLocalClientId()
    {
        var nm = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton;
        return (nm != null && nm.IsListening) ? nm.LocalClientId : 0;
    }

   

    // ============================================================
    // Mouse input
    // ============================================================

    private void HandleMouseInput()
    {
        if (TestMenuUI.ShouldBlockSpawnerInput) return;

        Camera cam = GetCameraForScreenPoint(Input.mousePosition);
        if (cam == null) return;

        Vector3 mousePos = Input.mousePosition;
        mousePos.z = Mathf.Abs(cam.transform.position.z);
        Vector3 worldPosition = cam.ScreenToWorldPoint(mousePos);
        worldPosition.z = 0f;

        bool rightClickPressed = Input.GetMouseButtonDown(1);
        bool shiftHeld = Input.GetKey(KeyCode.LeftShift) || Input.GetKey(KeyCode.RightShift);
        if (rightClickPressed && shiftHeld)
        {
            var menuSetup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
            bool menuOverHand = menuSetup != null &&
                                Input.mousePosition.y < Screen.height * menuSetup.HandFraction;

            if (testMenuUI != null) testMenuUI.OpenAt(worldPosition, menuOverHand);
            else if (randomBallSummoner != null) randomBallSummoner.SpawnRandomBallsAt(worldPosition);
            return;
        }

        if (EventSystem.current != null && EventSystem.current.IsPointerOverGameObject()) return;

        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        bool overHand = setup != null &&
                        Input.mousePosition.y < Screen.height * setup.HandFraction;

        if (rightClickPressed && GetBallAtPosition(worldPosition, overHand) == null)
        {
            if (overHand) SpawnHandBallAt(worldPosition);
            else          SpawnBallAt(worldPosition);
        }

        if (Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Delete))
        {
            if (LassoSelection.GetSelectedBalls().Count > 0) return;
            BallNumber ball = GetBallAtPosition(worldPosition, overHand);
            if (ball != null) TryDeleteBall(ball);
        }
    }

    private void TryDeleteBall(BallNumber ball)
    {
        if (ball == null) return;

        // Skip bag balls — they cannot be deleted by normal means.
        if (ball.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null) return;
        var nbCheck = ball.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (nbCheck != null && nbCheck.IsBagBall.Value) return;

        var go = ball.gameObject;
        bool netActive = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
                        Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening;

        var nb = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (netActive && nb != null && nb.IsSpawned)
        {
            nb.RequestDespawnServerRpc();
            return;
        }

        if (handBalls.Remove(go))  Destroy(go);
        else if (boardBalls.Remove(go)) Destroy(go);
    }

    private BallNumber GetBallAtPosition(Vector3 position, bool handWorld)
    {
        int mask = handWorld
            ? LayerMask.GetMask("HandPieces")
            : LayerMask.GetMask("BoardPieces");

        Collider2D[] colliders = Physics2D.OverlapPointAll(position, mask);   // ← masked
        foreach (Collider2D collider in colliders)
        {
            BallNumber ball = collider.GetComponentInParent<BallNumber>();
            if (ball != null && (boardBalls.Contains(ball.gameObject) || handBalls.Contains(ball.gameObject)))
                return ball;
        }
        return null;
    }

    public void SpawnGrayBall() => SpawnBall();
    public void SpawnKeyBall()  => SpawnBall();

    public void DeleteAllBalls()
    {
        foreach (var go in new List<GameObject>(handBalls))
        {
            if (go == null) continue;
            if (go.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null) continue;   // ← skip bag balls
            Destroy(go);
        }

                // Keep bag balls in the list; rebuild without them.
        handBalls.RemoveAll(go => go == null || go.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() == null);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallDespawned();
        
    }

    public void DeleteAllBoardBalls()
    {
        var snapshot = new List<GameObject>(boardBalls);
        bool isServer = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
                        Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsServer;
        bool netActive = Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
                        Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsListening;

                foreach (var go in snapshot)
        {
            if (go == null) continue;

            var nb = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
            if (nb != null && nb.IsSpawned && nb.IsBagBall.Value) continue;   // skip networked bag balls
            if (go.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null) continue;           // skip offline bag balls

            if (netActive && nb != null && nb.IsSpawned)
            {
                if (isServer) nb.RequestDespawnServerRpc();
            }
            else
            {
                boardBalls.Remove(go);
                Destroy(go);
            }
        }

                if (!netActive) boardBalls.RemoveAll(go => go == null);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallDespawned();
        
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Solver — runs separately per world (board, hand)
    // ============================================================

    private List<BallNumber> GetActiveBalls(List<GameObject> source)
    {
        var list = new List<BallNumber>();
        foreach (var go in source)
        {
            if (go == null) continue;
            var b = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
            if (b != null) list.Add(b);
        }
        return list;
    }

        private List<MathEquationBuilder.CachedCluster> Repartition(
        List<MathEquationBuilder.CachedCluster> oldClusters,
        List<GameObject> source)
    {
        var active = GetActiveBalls(source);
        var fresh = MathEquationBuilder.PartitionClusters(active);

        var result = new List<MathEquationBuilder.CachedCluster>(fresh.Count);
        foreach (var balls in fresh)
        {
            MathEquationBuilder.CachedCluster reused = null;
            foreach (var old in oldClusters)
            {
                if (old.balls == null) continue;
                if (SameBallSet(old.balls, balls)) { reused = old; break; }
            }
            if (reused != null) result.Add(reused);
            else result.Add(new MathEquationBuilder.CachedCluster { balls = balls });
        }
        return result;
    }

    private static bool SameBallSet(List<BallNumber> a, List<BallNumber> b)
    {
        if (a.Count != b.Count) return false;
        var set = new HashSet<BallNumber>(a);
        for (int i = 0; i < b.Count; i++)
            if (!set.Contains(b[i])) return false;
        return true;
    }

    private MathEquationBuilder.BuildResult SolveDirtyClusters(
        List<MathEquationBuilder.CachedCluster> clusters,
        string context)
    {
        var aggregate = new MathEquationBuilder.BuildResult();

        foreach (var c in clusters)
        {
            long sum = 0;
            foreach (var b in c.balls) if (b != null) sum += b.ChangeVersion;
            bool dirty = c.result == null || c.contentVersionAtLastSolve != sum;
            if (dirty)
            {
                c.result = MathEquationBuilder.SolveCluster(c.balls);
                c.contentVersionAtLastSolve = sum;
            }
            aggregate.equations.AddRange(c.result.equations);
            aggregate.allAmbiguousBalls.UnionWith(c.result.allAmbiguousBalls);
            aggregate.connectedPairs.AddRange(c.result.connectedPairs);
        }

        var definitions = MathEquationBuilder.BuildVariableDefinitions(aggregate.equations);

        foreach (var c in clusters)
            foreach (var b in c.balls)
            {
                if (b == null) continue;
                b.SetEquationState(0);   // reset to neutral
                b.SetAmbiguousOperand(false);
            }
        foreach (var b in aggregate.allAmbiguousBalls) b.SetAmbiguousOperand(true);

        var updated = new HashSet<BallNumber>();

                    foreach (var eq in aggregate.equations)
        {
            Debug.Log($"[Solve] expr='{eq.expression}' state={eq.equationState} " +
                      $"slots={eq.slots.Count} slotBalls={eq.slotBalls.Count} " +
                      $"ambiguous={eq.ambiguousBalls.Count} balls={eq.balls.Count} " +
                      $"emptyBalls={CountEmpty(eq)}");
            MathEquationBuilder.TrySolveEquation(eq, definitions, updated);
        }

        foreach (var c in clusters)
            foreach (var b in c.balls)
                if (b != null && b.IsSolverOutput && !b.IsLocked && !updated.Contains(b))
                    b.ClearSolverOutput();

                foreach (var eq in aggregate.equations)
        {
            foreach (var b in eq.ambiguousBalls) b.SetAmbiguousOperand(true);

            // If a blank was just filled by TrySolveEquation, the pre-fill
            // state may be stale (blue/red even though the equation now holds).
            // Re-evaluate against the filled value.
            byte state = eq.equationState;
            if (state != 1)
                state = MathEquationBuilder.ReEvaluateAfterFill(eq);

            eq.equalsBall?.SetEquationState(state);
        }

        if (context == "board" && equationListDisplay != null)
            equationListDisplay.Refresh(aggregate.equations);

        if (context == "board" &&
            Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton != null &&
            Unity.Netcode.NetworkManager.Singleton.IsServer)
        {
            foreach (var nb in FindObjectsByType<NetworkBall>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
            {
                if (nb == null || !nb.IsSpawned) continue;
                if (nb.CurrentZone.Value == (byte)BallZone.ServerBoard) nb.PushImmediate();
            }
        }

        return aggregate;
    }

              private static int CountEmpty(MathEquationBuilder.ConnectedEquation eq)
    {
        int n = 0;
        foreach (var b in eq.balls)
            if (b != null && string.IsNullOrEmpty(b.GetMathContent().Trim())) n++;
        return n;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Tendrils (per-context pools)
    // ============================================================

        private void RefreshTendrils(List<(BallNumber a, BallNumber b)> pairs,
        List<LineRenderer> pool, int layerMask)
    {
        int layer = layerMask == LayerMask.GetMask("HandPieces")
            ? LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces")
            : LayerMask.NameToLayer("BoardPieces");

        string sortingLayer = "Default";
        int sortingOrder = tendrilSortingOrderOffset;

        int activeCount = 0;
        foreach (var (ballA, ballB) in pairs)
        {
            if (ballA == null || ballB == null) continue;

            Collider2D ci = ballA.GetComponent<Collider2D>();
            Collider2D cj = ballB.GetComponent<Collider2D>();
            if (ci == null || cj == null) continue;

            if (activeCount == 0)
            {
                var sr = ballA.GetComponent<SpriteRenderer>();
                if (sr != null)
                {
                    sortingLayer = string.IsNullOrEmpty(tendrilSortingLayer)
                        ? sr.sortingLayerName
                        : tendrilSortingLayer;
                    sortingOrder = sr.sortingOrder + tendrilSortingOrderOffset;
                }
            }

            var tendril = GetOrCreateTendril(
                pool, activeCount++, layer, sortingLayer, sortingOrder);

            // Endpoints: closest point on each collider to the other's
            // center. Correct for circles and triangles alike.
            Vector2 centerA = ci.bounds.center;
            Vector2 centerB = cj.bounds.center;

            Vector3 start = ci.ClosestPoint(centerB);
            Vector3 end   = cj.ClosestPoint(centerA);

            // Small pull-back so the line doesn't poke out of the ball.
            Vector2 dir = (Vector2)(end - start);
            float len = dir.magnitude;
            if (len > 0.0001f)
            {
                Vector2 dirN = dir / len;
                const float inset = 0.02f;
                if (len > inset * 2f)
                {
                    start += (Vector3)(dirN * inset);
                    end   -= (Vector3)(dirN * inset);
                }
            }

            SetTendrilPoints(tendril, start, end, ci.GetInstanceID() ^ cj.GetInstanceID());
        }

        for (int i = activeCount; i < pool.Count; i++) pool[i].enabled = false;
    }
    


    private LineRenderer GetOrCreateTendril(List<LineRenderer> pool, int index, int layer,
    string sortingLayer, int sortingOrder)
    {
        LineRenderer lr;
        if (index < pool.Count)
        {
            lr = pool[index];
            lr.enabled = true;
        }
        else
        {
            var go = new GameObject("Ball Connection");
            go.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            lr = go.AddComponent<LineRenderer>();
            lr.useWorldSpace = true;
            lr.positionCount = TendrilPointCount;
            lr.sharedMaterial = tendrilMaterial;
            lr.numCapVertices = 3;
            pool.Add(lr);
        }

        lr.startWidth = tendrilWidth;
        lr.endWidth = tendrilWidth;
        lr.startColor = tendrilColor;
        lr.endColor = tendrilColor;
        lr.sortingLayerName = sortingLayer;
        lr.sortingOrder = sortingOrder;
        if (layer >= 0) lr.gameObject.layer = layer;
        return lr;
    }

    private void SetTendrilPoints(LineRenderer tendril, Vector3 start, Vector3 end, int seed)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < TendrilPointCount; i++)
        {
            float t = i / (TendrilPointCount - 1f);
            Vector3 p = Vector3.Lerp(start, end, t);
            p.z = -0.05f;
            tendril.SetPosition(i, p);
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Registration
    // ============================================================

        public void RegisterBall(GameObject go)
    {
        if (go == null) return;
        if (boardBalls.Contains(go) || handBalls.Contains(go)) return;
        // Networked board piece → boardBalls.
        if (go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>() != null) boardBalls.Add(go);
        else handBalls.Add(go);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallSpawned();
        
    }

        public void UnregisterBall(GameObject go)
    {
        if (go == null) return;
        boardBalls.Remove(go);
        handBalls.Remove(go);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallDespawned();
       
    }

    private static void ApplyLayerRecursive(Transform t, int layer)
    {
        t.gameObject.layer = layer;
        foreach (Transform child in t) ApplyLayerRecursive(child, layer);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // Offline starting hand
    // ============================================================

        private void SpawnStartingLocalHand()
    {
        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        if (setup == null) return;

        if (startingHandBalls == null) return;

        foreach (var entry in startingHandBalls)
        {
            if (entry == null) continue;

            Vector3 world = setup.HandCamera.ViewportToWorldPoint(
                new Vector3(0.5f + entry.xOffset, 0.5f, 10f));
            world.z = 0f;
            SpawnLocalHandBall(world, entry.content ?? "");
        }
    }

        public void DestroyHandBall(GameObject handPiece)
    {
        if (handPiece == null) return;
        handBalls.Remove(handPiece);
        Destroy(handPiece);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallDespawned();
       
    }

        public void DestroyBoardBall(GameObject boardPiece)
    {
        if (boardPiece == null) return;
        boardBalls.Remove(boardPiece);
        Destroy(boardPiece);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallDespawned();
        
    }

    public List<GameObject> GetAllBoardPieces()
        {
            return new List<GameObject>(boardBalls);
        }

    public void OnBagButtonClicked()
    {
        if (BagManager.Instance == null) return;

        BagManager.Instance.RequestDraw(content =>
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(content)) return;

            var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
            Camera cam = setup != null ? setup.HandCamera : Camera.main;
            if (cam == null) return;

            Vector3 p = cam.ViewportToWorldPoint(new Vector3(0.5f, 0.5f, 0f));
            p.z = 0f;

            float radius = 0.6f * (cam.orthographicSize / 5f);
            Vector2 dir = Random.insideUnitCircle.normalized;
            p.x += dir.x * Random.Range(0.2f, 1f) * radius;
            p.y += dir.y * Random.Range(0.2f, 1f) * radius;

            SpawnLocalHandBagBall(p, content);
        });
    }

    public int GetHandBagBallCount()
    {
        int count = 0;
        foreach (var go in handBalls)
        {
            if (go == null) continue;
            if (go.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null) count++;
        }
        return count;
    }

        public void DeleteAllHandBagBalls()
    {
        var toDelete = new List<GameObject>();
        foreach (var go in handBalls)
        {
            if (go == null) continue;
            if (go.GetComponent<BagBallMarker>() != null)
                toDelete.Add(go);
        }
        foreach (var go in toDelete)
        {
            handBalls.Remove(go);
            Destroy(go);
        }
        if (toDelete.Count > 0) MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallDespawned();
    }

        // ============================================================
    // Prefab-swap helpers (used by BallPrefabSwapper)
    // ============================================================

    public GameObject SpawnLocalHandOperator(Vector3 handWorldPos, string content, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        if (directionalOperatorPrefab == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError("Spawner: directionalOperatorPrefab not assigned.", this);
            return null;
        }

        var go = Instantiate(directionalOperatorPrefab, handWorldPos,
                             Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, rotationZ));

        var netObj = go.GetComponent<Unity.Netcode.NetworkObject>();
        if (netObj != null) Destroy(netObj);
        var networkBall = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (networkBall != null) Destroy(networkBall);

        go.AddComponent<LocalHandBall>().Initialize(GetLocalClientId());

        var ballNumber = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ballNumber == null) { Destroy(go); return null; }
        ballNumber.SetInputMode(BallInputMode.DisplayKey);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
        {
            ballNumber.ApplyTextInput(content, false);
            ballNumber.RefreshDisplay();
        }

        int layer = LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces");
        if (layer >= 0) ApplyLayerRecursive(go.transform, layer);

        handBalls.Add(go);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallSpawned();
        return go;
    }

    public GameObject SpawnLocalHandBallReturning(
        Vector3 handWorldPos, string content,
        int number, char variable, int power, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        // Same as SpawnLocalHandBall but returns the new GameObject so the
        // caller can restore state (solver output, lock, bag flag).
        GameObject prefab = GetPrefabForContent(content);
        if (prefab == null) return null;

        var go = Instantiate(prefab, handWorldPos, Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, rotationZ));

        var netObj = go.GetComponent<Unity.Netcode.NetworkObject>();
        if (netObj != null) Destroy(netObj);
        var networkBall = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (networkBall != null) Destroy(networkBall);

        go.AddComponent<LocalHandBall>().Initialize(GetLocalClientId());

        var ballNumber = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ballNumber == null) { Destroy(go); return null; }
        ballNumber.SetInputMode(BallInputMode.DisplayKey);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
        {
            ballNumber.ApplyTextInput(content, false);
            ballNumber.RefreshDisplay();
        }
        if (number != 0 || variable != '\0' || power != 0)
        {
            ballNumber.SetAlgebraContentRaw(number, variable, power);
            ballNumber.RefreshDisplay();
        }

        int layer = LayerMask.NameToLayer("HandPieces");
        if (layer >= 0) ApplyLayerRecursive(go.transform, layer);

        handBalls.Add(go);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallSpawned();
        return go;
    }

    public GameObject SpawnLocalBoardOperator(Vector3 position, string content, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        if (directionalOperatorPrefab == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError("Spawner: directionalOperatorPrefab not assigned.", this);
            return null;
        }

        var go = Instantiate(directionalOperatorPrefab, position,
                             Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, rotationZ));

        var netObj = go.GetComponent<Unity.Netcode.NetworkObject>();
        if (netObj != null) Destroy(netObj);
        var networkBall = go.GetComponent<NetworkBall>();
        if (networkBall != null) Destroy(networkBall);

        var ballNumber = go.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ballNumber == null) { Destroy(go); return null; }
        ballNumber.SetInputMode(BallInputMode.DisplayKey);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
        {
            ballNumber.ApplyTextInput(content, false);
            ballNumber.RefreshDisplay();
        }

        int layer = LayerMask.NameToLayer("BoardPieces");
        if (layer >= 0) ApplyLayerRecursive(go.transform, layer);

        boardBalls.Add(go);
        MathEquationBuilder.MarkBallSpawned();
        return go;
    }

    public GameObject SpawnBallAtPublicReturning(Vector3 position, string content, float rotationZ = 0f)
    {
        // Same as SpawnBallAtPublic but returns the new GameObject.
        // Uses GetPrefabForContent, so it picks the right prefab.
        SpawnBallAt(position, content, rotationZ);

        // The most recently added entry in boardBalls is the one we just made.
        if (boardBalls.Count == 0) return null;
        return boardBalls[boardBalls.Count - 1];
    }

}

// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\TestManuUI.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using System.Text;

/// <summary>
/// Runtime test menu drawn with Unity IMGUI, so no Canvas setup is required.
/// Attach this to the same GameObject as Spawner and TestSpawner.
/// </summary>
public class TestMenuUI : MonoBehaviour
{
    public TestSpawner testSpawner;
    public float menuWidth = 520f;
    public float menuHeight = 520f;

    private bool isOpen;
    private Vector3 spawnWorldPosition;
    private bool spawnInHand;
    private Vector2 menuPosition;
    private Vector2 testListScrollPosition;
    private Vector2 categoryScrollPosition;   
    private string selectedCategory = "SUM";
        private readonly string[] categories = {
        "SUM", "PRODUCT", "FUNCTIONS", "BRACKETS",
        "SYNTAX", "VALID", "STUCK", "COMPLEX", "POSITIONAL",
        "FRACTIONS", "POWERS", "EQUALS", "DANGLING"
    };
    private GUIStyle windowStyle;
    private GUIStyle categoryStyle;
    private GUIStyle selectedCategoryStyle;
    private GUIStyle testButtonStyle;
    private GUIStyle expectedStyle;
    private GUIStyle titleStyle;
    private GUIStyle actionButtonStyle;
    public static bool BlockWheelForMenu { get; private set; }

    // Spawner checks this so a click that closes the menu cannot also affect the game world.
    public static bool ShouldBlockSpawnerInput { get; private set; }

    public bool IsOpen => isOpen;

    private void Awake()
    {
        if (testSpawner == null)
            testSpawner = GetComponent<TestSpawner>();

        if (testSpawner == null)
            Debug.LogError("TestMenuUI needs a TestSpawner component on the same GameObject.", this);
    }

    private void Update()
    {
        if (isOpen)
        {
            Vector2 mouseTopLeft = new Vector2(
                Input.mousePosition.x,
                Screen.height - Input.mousePosition.y);
            BlockWheelForMenu = GetMenuRect().Contains(mouseTopLeft);
        }
        else
        {
            BlockWheelForMenu = false;
        }

        if (isOpen && Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape))
        {
            Close();
            return;
        }

        // Keep world input blocked through the click that closed the menu.
        if (!isOpen && !Input.GetMouseButton(0) && !Input.GetMouseButton(1) &&
            !Input.GetMouseButton(2) && !Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0) &&
            !Input.GetMouseButtonDown(1) && !Input.GetMouseButtonDown(2))
        {
            ShouldBlockSpawnerInput = false;
        }

        if (!isOpen)
            return;

        if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0))
        {
            Vector2 mouse = new Vector2(Input.mousePosition.x, Screen.height - Input.mousePosition.y);
            if (!GetMenuRect().Contains(mouse))
                Close();
        }
    }

    public void OpenAt(Vector3 worldPosition, bool isHand)
    {
        spawnWorldPosition = worldPosition;
        spawnInHand = isHand;

        Vector2 mouse = new Vector2(Input.mousePosition.x, Screen.height - Input.mousePosition.y);

        float desiredX = mouse.x;
        float desiredY = mouse.y;

        // Clamp position so the menu stays inside its own viewport.
        var setup = DualCameraSetup.Instance;
        if (setup != null)
        {
            float dividerYFromTop = Screen.height * (1f - setup.HandFraction);
            float barThickness = 8f;

            float minY, maxY;
            if (isHand)
            {
                minY = dividerYFromTop + barThickness;
                maxY = Screen.height;
            }
            else
            {
                minY = 0f;
                maxY = dividerYFromTop - barThickness;
            }

            // Ensure the menu fits vertically inside the viewport.
            float availableHeight = Mathf.Max(0f, maxY - minY);
            if (menuHeight > availableHeight)
                menuHeight = availableHeight;   // shrink if viewport is too small

            desiredY = Mathf.Clamp(desiredY, minY, Mathf.Max(minY, maxY - menuHeight));

            // If the menu would spill past the right edge, pull it left.
            desiredX = Mathf.Clamp(desiredX, 0f, Mathf.Max(0f, Screen.width - menuWidth));
        }

        menuPosition = new Vector2(desiredX, desiredY);
        isOpen = true;
        ShouldBlockSpawnerInput = true;
    }

    public void Close()
    {
        isOpen = false;
        // Do not release the block until the closing click has finished.
        ShouldBlockSpawnerInput = true;
    }

    private Rect GetMenuRect()
    {
        return new Rect(menuPosition.x, menuPosition.y, menuWidth, menuHeight);
    }

    private void EnsureStyles()
    {
        if (windowStyle != null)
            return;

        windowStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.box)
        {
            padding = new RectOffset(12, 12, 10, 12)
        };
        windowStyle.normal.background = MakeTexture(new Color(0.10f, 0.12f, 0.14f, 0.98f));

        categoryStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.button)
        {
            alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft,
            fontSize = 12,
            padding = new RectOffset(8, 6, 7, 7)
        };
        categoryStyle.normal.textColor = Color.white;

        selectedCategoryStyle = new GUIStyle(categoryStyle);
        selectedCategoryStyle.normal.background = MakeTexture(new Color(0.12f, 0.48f, 0.42f, 1f));

        testButtonStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.button)
        {
            alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft,
            fontSize = 13,
            wordWrap = true,
            padding = new RectOffset(8, 8, 7, 7)
        };

        expectedStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label)
        {
            fontSize = 10,
            wordWrap = true
        };
        expectedStyle.normal.textColor = new Color(0.72f, 0.78f, 0.80f);

        titleStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label)
        {
            fontSize = 16,
            fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold,
            alignment = TextAnchor.MiddleLeft
        };
        titleStyle.normal.textColor = Color.white;

        actionButtonStyle = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.button)
        {
            fontSize = 12,
            wordWrap = true,
            padding = new RectOffset(8, 8, 8, 8)
        };
    }

    private void OnGUI()
    {
        if (!isOpen || testSpawner == null)
            return;

        EnsureStyles();
        Rect rect = GetMenuRect();
        GUI.Box(rect, GUIContent.none, windowStyle);

        GUILayout.BeginArea(new Rect(rect.x + 12, rect.y + 8, rect.width - 24, rect.height - 16));
        GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();
        GUILayout.Label("BALL TESTS", titleStyle, GUILayout.Height(28));
        GUILayout.FlexibleSpace();
        if (GUILayout.Button("X", GUILayout.Width(30), GUILayout.Height(26)))
        {
            Close();
            Event.current.Use();
            GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
            GUILayout.EndArea();
            return;
        }
        GUILayout.EndHorizontal();

        GUILayout.Space(6);
        GUILayout.BeginHorizontal();

        GUILayout.BeginVertical(GUILayout.Width(150));
        if (GUILayout.Button("SPAWN ALL TESTS", actionButtonStyle, GUILayout.MinHeight(42)))
        {
            testSpawner.SpawnAllTests(spawnWorldPosition, spawnInHand);
            Close();
            GUILayout.EndVertical();
            GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
            GUILayout.EndArea();
            return;
        }

        if (GUILayout.Button("COPY FORMATION TO CLIPBOARD",
        actionButtonStyle, GUILayout.MinHeight(42)))
        {
            CopySelectedFormation();
        }

        GUILayout.Space(8);

        // Scroll view for the category list.
        categoryScrollPosition = GUILayout.BeginScrollView(
            categoryScrollPosition,
            GUILayout.Width(150),
            GUILayout.ExpandHeight(true));

        foreach (string category in categories)
        {
            GUIStyle style = category == selectedCategory ? selectedCategoryStyle : categoryStyle;
            if (GUILayout.Button(category, style, GUILayout.Height(34)))
                selectedCategory = category;
        }

        GUILayout.EndScrollView();
        GUILayout.EndVertical();

        GUILayout.Space(12);
        GUILayout.BeginVertical();
        if (GUILayout.Button("SPAWN ALL " + selectedCategory + " TESTS", actionButtonStyle, GUILayout.MinHeight(38)))
        {
            testSpawner.SpawnCategoryTests(selectedCategory, spawnWorldPosition, spawnInHand);
            Close();
            GUILayout.EndVertical();
            GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
            GUILayout.EndArea();
            return;
        }

        GUILayout.Space(6);
        List<TestSpawner.TestSetup> visibleTests = new List<TestSpawner.TestSetup>();
        foreach (TestSpawner.TestSetup setup in testSpawner.Tests)
        {
            if (setup.category == selectedCategory)
                visibleTests.Add(setup);
        }

        testListScrollPosition = GUILayout.BeginScrollView(testListScrollPosition, GUILayout.ExpandHeight(true));
        foreach (TestSpawner.TestSetup setup in visibleTests)
        {
            if (GUILayout.Button(setup.name, testButtonStyle, GUILayout.MinHeight(32)))
            {
                testSpawner.SpawnTest(setup, spawnWorldPosition, spawnInHand);
                Close();
                GUILayout.EndScrollView();
                GUILayout.EndVertical();
                GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
                GUILayout.EndArea();
                return;
            }

            GUILayout.Label("Expected: " + setup.expected, expectedStyle);
            GUILayout.Space(5);
        }
        GUILayout.EndScrollView();

        GUILayout.EndVertical();
        GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
        GUILayout.EndArea();
    }

    private Texture2D MakeTexture(Color color)
    {
        Texture2D texture = new Texture2D(1, 1);
        texture.hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave;
        texture.SetPixel(0, 0, color);
        texture.Apply();
        return texture;
    }

    private void OnDestroy()
    {
        DestroyStyleTexture(windowStyle);
        DestroyStyleTexture(selectedCategoryStyle);
        ShouldBlockSpawnerInput = false;
    }

    private void DestroyStyleTexture(GUIStyle style)
    {
        if (style != null && style.normal.background != null)
            Destroy(style.normal.background);
    }

    
private void CopySelectedFormation()
{
    List<BallDrag> selected = LassoSelection.GetSelectedBalls();
    List<BallDrag> validBalls = new List<BallDrag>();
    List<string> contents = new List<string>();

    foreach (BallDrag drag in selected)
    {
        if (drag == null)
            continue;

        BallNumber ball = drag.GetComponent<BallNumber>();
        if (ball == null)
            continue;

        string content = ball.GetMathContent();

        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(content))
            content = ball.GetSolverOutputContent();

        validBalls.Add(drag);
        contents.Add(content);
    }

    if (validBalls.Count == 0)
    {
        Debug.LogWarning("Select some balls with the lasso first.");
        return;
    }

    float spacing = testSpawner != null ? testSpawner.spacing : 1.8f;
    Vector3 origin = validBalls[0].transform.position;

    System.Text.StringBuilder code = new System.Text.StringBuilder();

    code.AppendLine(
        "tests.Add(new TestSetup(\"SUM\", \"My formation\", \"Expected result\",");

    for (int i = 0; i < validBalls.Count; i++)
    {
        Vector3 position = validBalls[i].transform.position;

        float x = (position.x - origin.x) / spacing;
        float y = (position.y - origin.y) / spacing;

        string xText = x.ToString(
            "0.###", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "f";

        string yText = y.ToString(
            "0.###", System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) + "f";

        string content = contents[i]
            .Replace("\\", "\\\\")
            .Replace("\"", "\\\"");

        code.Append("    new BallPlacement(")
            .Append(xText)
            .Append(", ")
            .Append(yText)
            .Append(", \"")
            .Append(content)
            .Append("\")");

        code.AppendLine(i < validBalls.Count - 1 ? "," : "));");
    }

    GUIUtility.systemCopyBuffer = code.ToString();
    
}


}


// ===== FILE: C:\Users\manue\MatheMOnster\Assets\scripts\TestSpawner.cs =====
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

/// <summary>
/// Hardcoded test arrangements for the algebra-ball solver.
/// Attach this to the same GameObject as Spawner.
/// </summary>
public class TestSpawner : MonoBehaviour
{
    [Serializable]
    public class BallPlacement
    {
        public Vector2 offset;
        public string content;

        public BallPlacement(float x, float y, string content)
        {
            offset = new Vector2(x, y);
            this.content = content;
        }
    }

    [Serializable]
    public class TestSetup
    {
        public string category;
        public string name;
        public string expected;
        public BallPlacement[] balls;

        public TestSetup(string category, string name, string expected, params BallPlacement[] balls)
        {
            this.category = category;
            this.name = name;
            this.expected = expected;
            this.balls = balls;
        }
    }

    [Min(0.1f)]
    [Tooltip("Distance between neighboring ball centers in row-based tests. Adjust if balls do not connect.")]
    public float spacing = 1.8f;

    [Min(0f)]
    [Tooltip("Vertical gap between test constructions when spawning a whole category or all tests.")]
    public float stackGap = 2.5f;

    private Spawner spawner;
    private readonly List<TestSetup> tests = new List<TestSetup>();

    public IReadOnlyList<TestSetup> Tests => tests;

    private void Awake()
    {
        spawner = GetComponent<Spawner>();
        if (spawner == null)
            Debug.LogError("TestSpawner needs to be on the same GameObject as Spawner.", this);

        BuildTests();
    }

    public void SpawnTest(TestSetup setup, Vector3 worldOrigin, bool isHand)
    {
        if (!CanSpawn(setup))
            return;

        SpawnTestAt(setup, worldOrigin, isHand);
    }

    public void SpawnAllTests(Vector3 worldOrigin, bool isHand)
    {
        SpawnStacked(tests, worldOrigin, "ALL TESTS", isHand);
    }

    public void SpawnCategoryTests(string category, Vector3 worldOrigin, bool isHand)
    {
        List<TestSetup> categoryTests = new List<TestSetup>();
        foreach (TestSetup setup in tests)
        {
            if (setup.category == category)
                categoryTests.Add(setup);
        }

        SpawnStacked(categoryTests, worldOrigin, category, isHand);
    }

    private bool CanSpawn(TestSetup setup)
    {
        if (spawner == null)
            spawner = GetComponent<Spawner>();

        if (spawner == null || setup == null || setup.balls == null)
        {
            Debug.LogError("Cannot spawn test: Spawner or test setup is missing.", this);
            return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    private void SpawnStacked(IReadOnlyList<TestSetup> setups, Vector3 worldOrigin, string label, bool isHand)
    {
        if (setups == null || setups.Count == 0)
            return;

        float verticalOffset = 0f;
        foreach (TestSetup setup in setups)
        {
            if (!CanSpawn(setup))
                continue;

            GetVerticalBounds(setup, out float minY, out float maxY);
            Vector3 testOrigin = new Vector3(
                worldOrigin.x,
                worldOrigin.y - verticalOffset - maxY * spacing,
                0f);

            SpawnTestAt(setup, testOrigin, isHand);
            verticalOffset += (maxY - minY) * spacing + stackGap * spacing;
        }

    }

    private void SpawnTestAt(TestSetup setup, Vector3 worldOrigin, bool isHand)
    {
        worldOrigin.z = 0f;

        foreach (BallPlacement ball in setup.balls)
        {
            // Empty strings are intentional: they spawn the blank output ball at the end of a test.
            if (ball == null || ball.content == null)
                continue;

            Vector3 position = worldOrigin + new Vector3(ball.offset.x * spacing, ball.offset.y * spacing, 0f);

            if (isHand)
                spawner.SpawnHandBallAt(position, ball.content);
            else
                spawner.SpawnBallWithContent(position, ball.content);
        }
    }

    private void GetVerticalBounds(TestSetup setup, out float minY, out float maxY)
    {
        minY = 0f;
        maxY = 0f;
        if (setup.balls == null || setup.balls.Length == 0)
            return;

        minY = float.MaxValue;
        maxY = float.MinValue;
        foreach (BallPlacement ball in setup.balls)
        {
            if (ball == null)
                continue;
            minY = Mathf.Min(minY, ball.offset.y);
            maxY = Mathf.Max(maxY, ball.offset.y);
        }

        if (minY == float.MaxValue)
            minY = maxY = 0f;
    }

    private void BuildTests()
    {
        // SUM
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("SUM", "Two numbers", "2 + 3 = 5",
            Row("2", "+", "3", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("SUM", "Three terms", "2 + 3 + 4 = 9",
            Row("2", "+", "3", "+", "4", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("SUM", "Subtraction", "8 - 3 = 5",
            Row("8", "-", "3", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("SUM", "Mixed precedence", "2 + 3 * 4 = 14",
            Row("2", "+", "3", "*", "4", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("SUM", "Branched sum", "Check branch traversal and grouping",
            new BallPlacement(0, 0, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(1, 0, "+"),
            new BallPlacement(2, 0, "3"),
            new BallPlacement(1, 1, "+"),
            new BallPlacement(1, 2, "4"),
            new BallPlacement(3, 0, "="),
            new BallPlacement(4, 0, "")));
        
        // MY TESTS

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("SUM", "number and + touching", "Expected result",
            new BallPlacement(0f, 0f, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(0.303f, 1.137f, "+"),
            new BallPlacement(1.103f, -0.112f, "="),
            new BallPlacement(2.21f, -0.062f, ""),
            new BallPlacement(-0.786f, 0.782f, "3")));
        
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("SUM", "T Fork", "Expected result",
            new BallPlacement(0f, 0f, ""),
            new BallPlacement(-2.197f, -0.232f, "3"),
            new BallPlacement(-2.245f, -1.339f, "-"),
            new BallPlacement(-2.481f, -2.422f, "1"),
            new BallPlacement(-2.677f, 1.986f, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(-1.107f, -0.035f, "="),
            new BallPlacement(-2.504f, 0.889f, "+")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("SUM", "2 touching number groups", "Expected result",
            new BallPlacement(0f, 0f, "+"),
            new BallPlacement(1.835f, 1.846f, "+"),
            new BallPlacement(4.351f, 1.224f, ""),
            new BallPlacement(3.242f, 1.224f, "="),
            new BallPlacement(-1.104f, -0.093f, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(1.757f, 2.952f, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(2.161f, 0.787f, "3"),

            new BallPlacement(2.292f, -0.363f, "=")));

        







        // PRODUCT
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("PRODUCT", "Multiplication", "2 * 3 = 6",
            Row("2", "*", "3", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("PRODUCT", "Implicit multiplication", "2 touching 3 means 2 * 3 = 6",
            Row("2", "3", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("PRODUCT", "Division", "8 / 2 = 4",
            Row("8", "/", "2", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("PRODUCT", "Power", "2 ^ 3 = 8",
            Row("2", "^", "3", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("PRODUCT", "Branched product", "Check branch traversal around multiplication",
            new BallPlacement(0, 0, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(1, 0, "*"),
            new BallPlacement(2, 0, "3"),
            new BallPlacement(1, 1, "*"),
            new BallPlacement(1, 2, "4"),
            new BallPlacement(3, 0, "="),
            new BallPlacement(4, 0, "")));

        // FUNCTIONS
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FUNCTIONS", "sin", "sin(0) = 0",
            Row("sin", "0", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FUNCTIONS", "log", "log(1) = 0",

            // Numbers merge into 6, but then nothing else can happen. Should be red.

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("STUCK", "Only operators", "all stuck, red",
            Row("+", "*", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("STUCK", "Two equals", "invalid, no equations built",
            Row("2", "=", "3", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("STUCK", "No equals", "invalid cluster, nothing red (or everything red)",
            Row("2", "+", "3")));
            // Nothing to solve, no '=' ball. What should happen?

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("STUCK", "Dangling operator before equals", "operator red via stuck",
            Row("2", "+", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("STUCK", "Bracket without close", "whole thing fails, red",
            Row("(", "2", "+", "3", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("STUCK", "Close bracket without open", "whole thing fails, red",
            Row("2", "+", "3", ")", "=")));

            tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Bracket inside function", "no red, solves",
            Row("sin", "(", "0", ")", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Function result plus number", "no red, solves",
            Row("sin", "0", "+", "4", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Number times function", "no red, solves",
            Row("2", "*", "sin", "0", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Implicit multiplication of constants", "no red",
            Row("2", "pi", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Constant pi alone", "no red",
            Row("pi", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Constant e plus 1", "no red",
            Row("e", "+", "1", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Variable plus number", "no red",
            Row("x", "+", "2", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Deeply nested brackets", "no red, solves",
            Row("(", "(", "(", "2", "+", "3", ")", ")", ")", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Two separate brackets multiplied", "no red, solves",
            Row("(", "2", ")", "*", "(", "3", ")", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Function on bracketed sum", "no red, solves",
            Row("sin", "(", "0", "+", "0", ")", "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("COMPLEX", "Bracket containing function", "no red, solves",
            Row("(", "sin", "0", ")", "=")));

            tests.Add(new TestSetup("POSITIONAL", "T-fork of plus", "some balls red — ambiguous",
        new BallPlacement(0, 0, "2"),
        new BallPlacement(1, 0, "+"),
        new BallPlacement(2, 0, "3"),
        new BallPlacement(1, 1, "+"),
        new BallPlacement(1, 2, "4"),
        new BallPlacement(3, 0, "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("POSITIONAL", "Two functions fighting over a number", "middle number red",
            new BallPlacement(0, 0, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(1, 1, "sin"),
            new BallPlacement(2, 2, "5"),
            new BallPlacement(1, -1, "cos"),
            new BallPlacement(3, 1, "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("POSITIONAL", "Operator between two brackets", "no red, solves",
            new BallPlacement(0, 0, "("),
            new BallPlacement(1, 0, "1"),
            new BallPlacement(2, 0, ")"),
            new BallPlacement(3, 0, "*"),
            new BallPlacement(4, 0, "("),
            new BallPlacement(5, 0, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(6, 0, ")"),
            new BallPlacement(7, 0, "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("POSITIONAL", "Vertical bracket pair", "no red, solves",
            new BallPlacement(0, 0, "("),
            new BallPlacement(1, 1, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(1, -1, "+"),
            new BallPlacement(2, 0, "3"),
            new BallPlacement(3, 0, ")"),
            new BallPlacement(4, 0, "=")));

        tests.Add(new TestSetup("POSITIONAL", "Operators on both sides of a number", "number red if two functions",
            new BallPlacement(0, 0, "2"),
            new BallPlacement(1, 1, "sin"),
            new BallPlacement(1, -1, "cos"),
            new BallPlacement(2, 0, "=")));

                    // ============================================================
        // NEW TESTS: fractions, powers, brackets-implicit-mult, equals states
        // ============================================================

        // ---------- FRACTIONS ----------
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("DIRECTIONAL OPERATORS", "Outside / inside = division", "8 / 2 = 4",
            Row("8", "/", "2", "=", "4")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FRACTIONS", "'/3' = 1/3", "0.333...",
            Row("/3", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FRACTIONS", "'/x' symbolic", "1/x (unsolvable numeric)",
            Row("/x", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FRACTIONS", "2 touching 1/3 = 2/3", "0.666...",
            Row("2", "/3", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FRACTIONS", "1/3 * 1/4 = 1/12", "0.08333...",
            Row("/3", "/4", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FRACTIONS", "2 * 1/3 * 3 = 2", "2",
            Row("2", "/3", "*", "3", "=")));

        // ---------- POWERS ----------
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("DIRECTIONAL OPERATORS", "Outside base / inside exponent", "2 ^ 3 = 8",
            Row("2", "^", "3", "=", "8")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("POWERS", "Explicit '^2' squares operand", "2^2 = 4",
            Row("2", "^2", "=", "4")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("POWERS", "'^3' cubes operand", "2^3 = 8",
            Row("2", "^3", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("POWERS", "'^x' symbolic", "2^x (unsolvable without x)",
            Row("2", "^x", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("DIRECTIONAL OPERATORS", "Outside minus inside", "8 - 3 = 5",
            Row("8", "-", "3", "=", "5")));

        // ---------- FUNCTIONS (new cases) ----------
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FUNCTIONS", "sin sees two numbers → red", "sin red",
            Row("2", "sin", "3", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FUNCTIONS", "sin sees two with bracket → red", "sin red",
            Row("2", "sin", "(", "8", "+", "2", ")", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("FUNCTIONS", "sin with one bracket group → ok", "sin(10)",
            Row("sin", "(", "8", "+", "2", ")", "=")));

        // ---------- BRACKETS (new cases) ----------
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("BRACKETS", "Number touching bracket = implicit *", "2 (3+4) = 14",
            Row("2", "(", "3", "+", "4", ")", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("BRACKETS", "Number and bracket and *", "2 (3+4) * 5 = 70",
            Row("2", "(", "3", "+", "4", ")", "*", "5", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("BRACKETS", "Bracket with no interior → red", "( ) =",
            Row("(", ")", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("BRACKETS", "Unmatched '(' → red", "( 2 =",
            Row("(", "2", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("BRACKETS", "Unmatched ')' → red", "2 ) =",
            Row("2", ")", "=")));

        // ---------- EQUALS STATES ----------
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("EQUALS", "Equals with 1 slot → blue", "2 = (blue)",
            Row("2", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("EQUALS", "Equals with 2 equal slots → green", "2 = 2",
            Row("2", "=", "2")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("EQUALS", "Equals with 2 disagreeing slots → red", "2 = 3",
            Row("2", "=", "3")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("EQUALS", "Empty ball solved to 4", "2+2 = [4]",
            Row("2", "+", "2", "=", "")));

        // ---------- DANGLING ----------
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("DANGLING", "Dangling '+' → red", "2 + =",
            Row("2", "+", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("DANGLING", "Dangling '*' → red", "2 * =",
            Row("2", "*", "=")));
        tests.Add(new TestSetup("DANGLING", "Dangling function → red", "sin =",
            Row("sin", "=")));  

    }

    // Row-based tests always finish with '=' followed by a blank output ball.
    private BallPlacement[] Row(params string[] contents)
    {
        int equalsIndex = Array.LastIndexOf(contents, "=");
        bool hasEquals = equalsIndex >= 0;
        int totalCount = contents.Length + (hasEquals ? 1 : 0);
        var placements = new BallPlacement[totalCount];
        float middle = (contents.Length - 1) / 2f;

        for (int i = 0; i < contents.Length; i++)
            placements[i] = new BallPlacement(i - middle, 0f, contents[i]);

        if (hasEquals)
            placements[contents.Length] = new BallPlacement(contents.Length - middle, 0f, "");

        return placements;
    }
}
